# DBTITLE 0,Journal Entry Reconciliation and DQC Golden Checks
# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC # Overview
# MAGIC This script transforms and prepares the General Ledger, Trial Balance and Chart of Accounts for Common Data Models and standard analytics. The script also performs reconciliation, provides General Ledger summary statistics, and identifies data integrity issues in the input data.
# MAGIC
# MAGIC **IMPORTANT**: Please review the script by using the Table of Contents at the upper left and update the applicable sections for accurate data preparation. **User input in sections 2 & 3 is prerequisite for running the script.** Go to each section for the detail instructions.
# MAGIC
# MAGIC <br>
# MAGIC Version : 9.3.1

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC # 1. Installing Libraries
# MAGIC
# MAGIC **Do not change any codes under this section!** Go to section 2 to start. 

# COMMAND ----------

# DBTITLE 1,Set PYPI_TOKEN & CortexPy for Serverless Compute
import os
os.environ['PYPI_TOKEN'] = dbutils.secrets.get('all-users-scope', 'PYPI_TOKEN')
cortexpy_package = dbutils.secrets.get("all-users-scope", "CORTEXPY_VERSION")
os.environ["VERSION"] = cortexpy_package.split("==")[1].strip()

# COMMAND ----------

# DBTITLE 1,Install CortexPy
pip install --index-url https://$PYPI_TOKEN@symphonyvsts.pkgs.visualstudio.com/_packaging/Cortex-PYPI/pypi/simple/ cortexpy==$VERSION --trusted-host symphonyvsts.pkgs.dev.azure.com --quiet

# COMMAND ----------

# DBTITLE 1,Restart Python Process
dbutils.library.restartPython()

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC ####Field Mapping Instructions
# MAGIC 1. Each line represents a column in the input table - "standard field": "actual input field".
# MAGIC 2. Update the value on the right side of colon (:) with the input column name.
# MAGIC 3. Do NOT remove or change the standard field names, the values before colon (:)
# MAGIC 4. If a column to map does not exist in the input, leave the value on the right as ""
# MAGIC 5. Examples - "entity_id": "Entity Number" or "extract_date": ""

@dataclass
class GLMapping:
    dict_general_ledger_mapping: dict
def get_gl_mapping() -> GLMapping:
    return GLMapping(
        dict_general_ledger_mapping = {
    ## Mapping mandatory fields
        "entity_id": "entity_id",
        "entity_name": "entity_name",
        "account_number": "account_number",
        "journal_number": "journal_number",
        "journal_header_description": "journal_header_description",

        "fiscal_period": "fiscal_period",
        "net_amount_oc": "net_amount_oc",

        "transaction_type_description": "transaction_type_description",

        "original_currency_oc": "original_currency_oc",
        "group_currency_gc": "group_currency_gc",
        "date_posted": "date_posted",
        "date_effective": "date_effective",
        "userid_entered": "userid_entered",
        "user_name_entered": "user_name_entered",
        "userid_approved": "userid_approved",
        "user_name_approved": "user_name_approved",
        "time_posted": "time_posted",
        "source": "source",
        "is_standard": "is_standard",
        "location": "location",
    ## Mapping optional fields 
        "account_description": "account_description",
        "date_updated": "date_updated",
        "updated_by": "updated_by",
        "name_of_user_updated": "name_of_user_updated",
        "time_updated": "time_updated",

        "time_approved": "time_approved",
        "is_manual": "is_manual",

    "financial_statement_line_num": "financial_statement_line_num",
    "chart_of_accounts": "chart_of_accounts",

    "revision_date": "revision_date",
    "financial_statement_subtotal_category_id": "financial_statement_subtotal_category_id", 
    "financial_statement_category_id": "financial_statement_category_id",

# MAGIC ## 3.1. Populating User Parameters
# MAGIC
# MAGIC #### Guidance on the Parameters
# MAGIC 1) **Fiscal Calendar**: If the fiscal year does not align with the calendar year, an additional input table with the fiscal calendar information containing the following 3 columns is required: ***fiscal_period***, ***fiscal_period_start*** and ***fiscal_period_end***.
# MAGIC 2) **Input file path**: Specify each input file by entering the file name and the Staging folder it is located in. The path must start with a slash (/).
# MAGIC <br>&ensp;&ensp;- E.g., **/User Imported Data/EFT_UP/file_name.csv**.
# MAGIC 3) **Output path**: Specify a Staging folder to which the outputs will be saved. The path must start with a slash (/).
# MAGIC <br>&ensp;&ensp;- E.g., **/SPEC-DATAPREP/folder name**.
# MAGIC <br>&ensp;&ensp;- WARNING: Any existing files in the output path will be overwritten. Create different folders for different runs (e.g., /SPEC-DATAPREP/**Q1Q3** and /SPEC-DATAPREP/**Q4**)
# MAGIC <br>&ensp;&ensp;- Do not use a root folder other than SPEC-DATAPREP.

# DBTITLE 1,Widgets Creation
#### Creating all the parameters used in the JE Reconciliation and Golden Check workpaper
#dbutils.widgets.removeAll()
dbutils.widgets.dropdown(name="delimiter",defaultValue=",",choices=[",",".",":",";","-","_","|","\t"],label="Delimiter of General Ledger input")
dbutils.widgets.dropdown(name="field_separator",defaultValue="Period",choices=["None","Comma","Period"],label="Decimal separator for numeric fields (period, comma, or none)")
dbutils.widgets.text(name="fiscal_year",defaultValue="",label="Fiscal Year (yyyy)")
dbutils.widgets.text(name="fiscal_year_end",defaultValue="",label="Fiscal Year-End (MM/dd/yyyy where MM=month, dd=day, yyyy=year)")
dbutils.widgets.text(name="first_day_testing_period",defaultValue="",label="Testing Period First Day (MM/dd/yyyy where MM=month, dd=day, yyyy=year)")
dbutils.widgets.text(name="last_day_testing_period",defaultValue="",label="Testing Period Last Day (MM/dd/yyyy where MM=month, dd=day, yyyy=year)")
dbutils.widgets.dropdown("currency",defaultValue="Group Currency",choices=["Group Currency","Entity Currency"],label="Select the currency to perform reconciliation on:")
dbutils.widgets.dropdown(name="include_prior_balance",defaultValue="Yes",choices=["Yes","No"],label="Include Prior Period Ending Balance in Final TB (Yes or No)?")
dbutils.widgets.dropdown(name="calendar_fiscal_year",defaultValue="Yes",choices=["Yes","No"],label="Does the fiscal year align with the calendar year (i.e., starts on January 1 and ends on December 31)?")
dbutils.widgets.dropdown(name="is_test_period_from_beginning",defaultValue="Yes",choices=["Yes","No"],label="Is the testing period from the beginning of the year (Yes or No)?")
dbutils.widgets.dropdown(name="autocorrect_multiple_is_standard",defaultValue="No",choices=["Yes","No"],label="When an entry contains both N and S for is_standard field, should it be auto-resolved – Yes/No?")
dbutils.widgets.dropdown(name="is_standard_when_multiple",defaultValue="N",choices =["N","S"],label="When an entry contains nonstandard (N) and standard (S) lines, indicate whether the overall entry should be designated as N or S.")
dbutils.widgets.dropdown(name="is_standard_when_null",defaultValue="Custom",choices=["Custom","N","S"],label="When an entry contains missing is_standard, indicate whether the overall entry should be designated as N, S, or a custom value.")
dbutils.widgets.dropdown(name="replace_missing_posted_date",defaultValue="No",choices=["Yes","No"],label="Replace missing Date Posted with Date Effective?")
dbutils.widgets.text(name="input_format_of_the_date_posted_field",defaultValue="yyyy-MM-dd",label="Input format of the DatePosted field (month=MM, day=dd, year=yyyy, hour=hh, minutes=mm, seconds=ss). Note the upper case MM for month.")
dbutils.widgets.text(name="input_format_of_the_date_effective_field",defaultValue="yyyy-MM-dd",label="Input format of the DateEffective field (month=MM, day=dd, year=yyyy, hour=hh, minutes=mm, seconds=ss). Note the upper case MM for month.")
dbutils.widgets.text(name="input_format_of_the_time_posted_field",defaultValue="yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss",label="Input format of the TimePosted field (month=MM, day=dd, year=yyyy, hour=hh, minutes=mm, seconds=ss). Note the upper case MM for month.")
dbutils.widgets.text(name="input_format_of_the_period_end_date_field",defaultValue="yyyy-MM-dd",label="Input format of the Period_End_Date field (month=MM, day=dd, year=yyyy, hour=hh, minutes=mm, seconds=ss). Note the upper case MM for month.")
dbutils.widgets.dropdown(name="exclude_data_integrity_check_for_observation",defaultValue="No",choices=["Yes","No"],label="Exclude all Data Quality Checks for observation?")
dbutils.widgets.dropdown(name="exclude_data_integrity_check_related_to_transactiontype",defaultValue="No",choices=["Yes","No"],label="Exclude Data Quality Checks related to Transaction Type (DQC 1e, 15 and 19)?")
dbutils.widgets.dropdown(name="exclude_data_integrity_check_related_to_userid",defaultValue="No",choices=["Yes","No"],label="Exclude Data Quality Checks related to User ID (DQC 1d, 17 and 20)?")
## Below are the parameters created to define the input file path for COA, TB, GL and Fiscal Calendar.
dbutils.widgets.text("input_file_coa", "/SPEC-DATAPREP/OUTPUT/COA_Final.parquet", label = "Enter the input file path for the Chart of Accounts")
dbutils.widgets.text("input_file_tb", "/SPEC-DATAPREP/OUTPUT/TB_Final.parquet", label = "Enter the input file path for the Trial Balance")
dbutils.widgets.text("input_file_gl", "/SPEC-DATAPREP/OUTPUT/JE_Final.parquet", label = "Enter the input file path for the General Ledger Detail")
dbutils.widgets.text("input_file_path_fiscal_calendar", "", label = "Enter the input file path for the Fiscal Calendar. Note - This is an optional field which will be used if the fiscal year does not align with the calendar year.")
## Below parameter is used to create output path label where data table outputs needs to be saved.
dbutils.widgets.text("output_path", "/SPEC-DATAPREP/OUTPUT", label = "Enter a path to save the data table outputs to.")
dbutils.widgets.dropdown(name="je_recon_dic_template",defaultValue="Yes",choices=["Yes","No"],label="Generate the JE Data Reconciliation and Integrity Check Excel template?")

# DBTITLE 1,Dataclasses 
 
@dataclass 
class GCWDetails: 
    delimiter: List[str] 
    currency: List[str] 
    fiscal_year_end: str 
    input_format_of_the_date_posted_field: str 
    input_format_of_the_date_effective_field: str 
    input_format_of_the_time_posted_field: str 
    input_format_of_the_period_end_date_field: str 
    input_file_path_fiscal_calendar: str 
    fiscal_year: str 
    calendar_fiscal_year: List[str] 
    first_day_testing_period: str 
    last_day_testing_period: str 
    include_prior_balance: List[str] 
    is_test_period_from_beginning: List[str] 
    autocorrect_multiple_is_standard: List[str] 
    is_standard_when_multiple: List[str] 
    is_standard_when_null: List[str] 
    input_file_coa: str 
    input_file_tb: str 
    input_file_gl: str 
    output_path: str 
    input_template_path: str 
    field_seperator: List[str] 
    replace_missing_posted_date: List[str] 
    gl_col_excluding_amount: List[str] 
    tb_col_excluding_amount: List[str] 
    tb_col_amount: List[str] 
    exclude_data_integrity_check_for_observation: List[str] 
    exclude_data_integrity_check_related_to_transactiontype: List[str] 
    exclude_data_integrity_check_related_to_userid: List[str] 
    data_integrity_check_summary_columns: List[str] 
    schema_fiscal_calendar: List[str] 
    je_recon_dic_template: List[str] 
    exclusion_list_exclude_entityid: List[str] 
    exclusion_list_include_entityid: List[str] 
    data_integrity_check_summary_cols:List[str] 
     
 
     
### ### The function below is used to save the parameter values displayed under the widgets in the variable created in the class GCWDetails, as well as other variables used in the notebook. 
#      
def get_gcw_details() -> GCWDetails: 
    return GCWDetails( 
    delimiter = dbutils.widgets.get('delimiter'), 
    currency = dbutils.widgets.get('currency'), 
    fiscal_year_end = dbutils.widgets.get('fiscal_year_end'), 
    je_recon_dic_template = dbutils.widgets.get('je_recon_dic_template'),   
    fiscal_year = dbutils.widgets.get('fiscal_year'), 
    calendar_fiscal_year=dbutils.widgets.get('calendar_fiscal_year'), 
    first_day_testing_period = dbutils.widgets.get('first_day_testing_period'), 
    last_day_testing_period = dbutils.widgets.get('last_day_testing_period'), 
    include_prior_balance = dbutils.widgets.get('include_prior_balance'), 
    is_test_period_from_beginning = dbutils.widgets.get('is_test_period_from_beginning'),  
    autocorrect_multiple_is_standard = dbutils.widgets.get('autocorrect_multiple_is_standard'), 
    is_standard_when_multiple = dbutils.widgets.get('is_standard_when_multiple'), 
    is_standard_when_null = dbutils.widgets.get('is_standard_when_null'), 
    exclude_data_integrity_check_for_observation = dbutils.widgets.get('exclude_data_integrity_check_for_observation'), 
    exclude_data_integrity_check_related_to_transactiontype = dbutils.widgets.get('exclude_data_integrity_check_related_to_transactiontype'), 
    exclude_data_integrity_check_related_to_userid = dbutils.widgets.get('exclude_data_integrity_check_related_to_userid'), 
    output_path = dbutils.widgets.get('output_path'), 
    input_file_coa = dbutils.widgets.get('input_file_coa'), 
    input_file_tb = dbutils.widgets.get('input_file_tb'), 
    input_file_gl = dbutils.widgets.get('input_file_gl'), 
    input_file_path_fiscal_calendar = dbutils.widgets.get('input_file_path_fiscal_calendar'), 
    field_seperator = dbutils.widgets.get('field_separator'), 
    replace_missing_posted_date = dbutils.widgets.get('replace_missing_posted_date'), 
    input_format_of_the_period_end_date_field = dbutils.widgets.get('input_format_of_the_period_end_date_field') ,  
    input_format_of_the_date_posted_field = dbutils.widgets.get('input_format_of_the_date_posted_field'),  
    input_format_of_the_date_effective_field = dbutils.widgets.get('input_format_of_the_date_effective_field'), 
    input_format_of_the_time_posted_field = dbutils.widgets.get('input_format_of_the_time_posted_field'), 
    gl_col_excluding_amount =['chart_of_accounts' , 'entity_id','entity_name' , 'journal_number','journal_header_description', 'dc_indicator', 'journal_line_number', 'journal_line_description', 'fiscal_year' , 'fiscal_period', 'transaction_type', 'transaction_type_description','entity_currency_ec', 'date_posted', 'date_effective', 'userid_entered', 'user_name_entered', 'time_posted', 'date_updated', 'updated_by', 	'name_of_user_updated', 'time_updated', 'userid_approved', 'user_name_approved', 'date_approved', 'time_approved', 'is_standard', 'is_manual', 'source'	, 'source_description', 'account_number', 'account_description', 'coa_account_key', 'exchange_rate', 'foreign_exchange_date', 	'forex_conversion_method', 'original_currency_oc' , 'group_currency_gc', 'business_area', 'controlling_area_for_cost_and_profit_center', 'cost_center', 'profit_center', 'project', 'location', 'custom_exclusion', 'extract_date', 'import_date', 'ledger_id', 'ledger_group', 'misc1', 'misc2', 'misc3', 'misc4', 'misc5'], 
    tb_col_excluding_amount =    ['chart_of_accounts','entity_id','entity_name','entity_currency_ec','fiscal_year','account_number','account_description', 
  'fiscal_period','period_type','period_end_date','group_currency_gc','extract_date','import_date','ledger_id','ledger_group','coa_account_key','misc1','misc2'],  
    tb_col_amount= ['beginning_balance_ec','adjusted_journal_entry_ec','adjusted_journal_entry_gc','beginning_balance_gc','ending_balance_gc','ending_balance_ec','period_activity_ec', 'period_activity_gc', 'consolidation_journal_entry_gc', 'reclassification_journal_entry_ec', 'reclassification_journal_entry_gc', 'adjusted_ec', 'adjusted_gc', 'preliminary_gc', 'preliminary_ec'],  
    data_integrity_check_summary_columns=StructType( 
            [ 
                StructField("entity_id", StringType(), True), 
                StructField("Number_of_Affected_Lines", DoubleType(), True), 
                StructField("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries", StringType(), True), 
                StructField("Debit_Amount_GC", StringType(), True), 
                StructField("Debit_Amount_EC", StringType(), True), 
                StructField("Debit_Amount_OC", StringType(), True), 
                StructField("Credit_Amount_EC", StringType(), True), 
                StructField("Credit_Amount_GC", StringType(), True), 
                StructField("Credit_Amount_OC", StringType(), True), 
                StructField("Related_Fields", StringType(), True), 
                StructField("Capability_Impacted", StringType(), True), 
                StructField("Error_Warning", StringType(), True), 
                StructField("Description", StringType(), True), 
                StructField("Data_Integrity_Check_Name", StringType(), True), 
            ] 
        ), 
    schema_fiscal_calendar = StructType([ 
    StructField("fiscal_period", IntegerType(), True), 
    StructField("fiscal_period_start", StringType(), True), 
    StructField("fiscal_period_end", StringType(), True) 
]), 
    data_integrity_check_summary_cols= ["entity_id","Number_of_Affected_Lines","Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries","Debit_Amount_GC","Credit_Amount_GC","Credit_Amount_EC","Debit_Amount_EC","Credit_Amount_OC","Debit_Amount_OC","Related_Fields","Capability_Impacted","Error_Warning","Description","Data_Integrity_Check_Name"], 
 
    exclusion_list_exclude_entityid = r"['\"\;\|]|!~!|!@!|\u00A0" , 
    exclusion_list_include_entityid = r"['\"\;\|\\\/\<\>]|!~!|!@!|\u00A0" , 
 
    input_template_path = f'{os.environ.get("PKGS_BASE_URL")}/content-library-notebooks/dww-templates/JE-Recon-and-DIC-Template.xlsx' 
 
    ) 
     
obj_gcw_details=get_gcw_details()

# DBTITLE 1,Inputs Read-In 
## This function is used to read the input files - General Legder, Trial Balance, Chart of Accounts and  Fiscal Calendar which is an optional input 
def read_file( 
    input_file_coa: str, 
    input_file_tb: str, 
    input_file_gl: str, 
    input_file_path_fiscal_calendar: str, 
    delimiter, 
    schema_fiscal_calendar 
): 
## multiLine: To parse records across multiple lines into a single line 
## quote: To quote fields/columns containing values where delimiter/separator can be part of the value. The default value is double quote. 
## escape: To set a single character as escaping character to override default escape character(\) 
    coa_file_type = get_file_type(input_file_coa) 
    tb_file_type = get_file_type(input_file_tb) 
    gl_file_type = get_file_type(input_file_gl) 
 
    if coa_file_type == FileType.EXCEL: 
        chart_of_accounts_df = cp.pandas.read(input_file_coa, header = 0, dtype = "string").to_spark() 
    else: 
        chart_of_accounts_df = cp.read(input_file_coa,delimiter=",",multiLine= False, quote="\"", escape="\"",header=True) 
 
    if tb_file_type == FileType.EXCEL: 
        trial_balance_df = cp.pandas.read(input_file_tb, header = 0, dtype = "string").to_spark() 
    else: 
        trial_balance_df = cp.read(input_file_tb,delimiter=",",multiLine= False, quote="\"", escape="\"",header=True) 
 
    if gl_file_type == FileType.EXCEL: 
        general_ledger_df = cp.pandas.read(input_file_gl, header = 0, dtype = "string").to_spark() 
    else: 
        general_ledger_df = cp.read(input_file_gl,delimiter=delimiter,multiLine= False, quote="\"", escape="\"",header=True) 
 
## Since the Fiscal Calendar input is optional, an empty dataframe is created in the below step, if no input file is provided. 
    if(input_file_path_fiscal_calendar != ''): 
        fiscal_calendar_file_type = get_file_type(input_file_path_fiscal_calendar) 
        if fiscal_calendar_file_type == FileType.EXCEL: 
            fiscal_calendar_df = cp.pandas.read(input_file_path_fiscal_calendar, header = 0, dtype = "string").to_spark() 
        else: 
            fiscal_calendar_df = cp.read(input_file_path_fiscal_calendar, delimiter=",", multiLine=True, quote="\"", 
                                         escape="\"", header=True) 
    else: 
        fiscal_calendar_df =  spark.createDataFrame([],schema_fiscal_calendar) 
 
    return ( 
        chart_of_accounts_df, 
        trial_balance_df, 
        general_ledger_df, 
        fiscal_calendar_df, 
    ) 
 
# COMMAND ---------- 
 
# DBTITLE 1,GL Mapping Function 
def map_input_fields_general_ledger(general_ledger_df,dict_general_ledger_mapping): 
    null_keys = [] 
    for key, value in dict_general_ledger_mapping.items(): 
        if ((value is None) | (value == "")): 
        # append the key to the null_keys list 
            null_keys.append(key) 
        general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.withColumnRenamed(value, key) 
    for new_col_gl in null_keys: 
        general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.withColumn(new_col_gl, lit(None).cast('string'))   
    return (general_ledger_df) 
 
# COMMAND ---------- 
 
# DBTITLE 1,TB Mapping Function 
 
def map_input_fields_trial_balance(trial_balance_df,dict_trial_balance_mapping): 
    null_keys = [] 
    for key, value in dict_trial_balance_mapping.items(): 
        if ((value is None) | (value == "")): 
        # append the key to the null_keys list 
            null_keys.append(key) 
        trial_balance_df = trial_balance_df.withColumnRenamed(value, key) 
    for new_col_tb in null_keys: 
        trial_balance_df = trial_balance_df.withColumn(new_col_tb, lit(None).cast('string'))   
    return (trial_balance_df) 
 
# COMMAND ---------- 
 
# DBTITLE 1,COA Mapping Function 
def map_input_fields_chart_of_accounts(chart_of_accounts_df,dict_chart_of_accounts_mapping): 
     
    null_keys = [] 
    for key, value in dict_chart_of_accounts_mapping.items(): 
        if ((value is None) | (value == "")): 
        # append the key to the null_keys list 
            null_keys.append(key) 
        chart_of_accounts_df = chart_of_accounts_df.withColumnRenamed(value, key) 
    for new_col_coa in null_keys: 
        chart_of_accounts_df = chart_of_accounts_df.withColumn(new_col_coa, lit(None).cast('string'))  
    return (chart_of_accounts_df) 
 
# COMMAND ---------- 
 
# DBTITLE 1,GL Numeric Fields Truncation 
 
## This function is used to truncate the monetary fields in the General Ledger data to 2 decimal places.  
def perform_trunc_gl_numeric_field(general_ledger_df_arg): 
    general_ledger_df = ( 
            general_ledger_df_arg.select(col("chart_of_accounts"),col("entity_id"),col("entity_name"),col("journal_number"),col("journal_header_description"),col("dc_indicator"),col("journal_line_number"),col("journal_line_description"),col("fiscal_year"),col("fiscal_period"),col("transaction_type"),col("transaction_type_description"),col("entity_currency_ec"),col("date_posted"),col("date_effective"),col("userid_entered"),col("user_name_entered"),col("time_posted"),col("date_updated"),col("updated_by"),col("name_of_user_updated"),col("time_updated"),col("userid_approved"),col("user_name_approved"),col("date_approved"),col("time_approved"),col("is_standard"),col("is_manual"),col("source"),col("source_description"),col("account_number"),col("account_description"),col("coa_account_key"),col("exchange_rate"),col("foreign_exchange_date"),col("forex_conversion_method"),col("original_currency_oc"),col("group_currency_gc"),col("business_area"),col("controlling_area_for_cost_and_profit_center"),col("cost_center"),col("profit_center"),col("project"),col("location"),col("custom_exclusion"),col("extract_date"),col("import_date"),col("ledger_id"),col("ledger_group"),col("misc1"),col("misc2"),col("misc3"),col("misc4"),col("misc5") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', net_amount_ec) > 0 then substring(cast(net_amount_ec as string), 1, locate('.', cast(net_amount_ec as string)) + 2) else net_amount_ec end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("net_amount_ec") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', net_amount_oc) > 0 then substring(cast(net_amount_oc as string), 1, locate('.', cast(net_amount_oc as string)) + 2) else net_amount_oc end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("net_amount_oc") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', net_amount_gc) > 0 then substring(cast(net_amount_gc as string), 1, locate('.', cast(net_amount_gc as string)) + 2) else net_amount_gc end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("net_amount_gc") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', credit_amount_gc) > 0 then substring(cast(credit_amount_gc as string), 1, locate('.', cast(credit_amount_gc as string)) + 2) else credit_amount_gc end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("credit_amount_gc") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', credit_amount_oc) > 0 then substring(cast(credit_amount_oc as string), 1, locate('.', cast(credit_amount_oc as string)) + 2) else credit_amount_oc end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("credit_amount_oc") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', credit_amount_ec) > 0 then substring(cast(credit_amount_ec as string), 1, locate('.', cast(credit_amount_ec as string)) + 2) else credit_amount_ec end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("credit_amount_ec") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', debit_amount_gc) > 0 then substring(cast(debit_amount_gc as string), 1, locate('.', cast(debit_amount_gc as string)) + 2) else debit_amount_gc end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("debit_amount_gc") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', debit_amount_ec) > 0 then substring(cast(debit_amount_ec as string), 1, locate('.', cast(debit_amount_ec as string)) + 2) else debit_amount_ec end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("debit_amount_ec") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', debit_amount_oc) > 0 then substring(cast(debit_amount_oc as string), 1, locate('.', cast(debit_amount_oc as string)) + 2) else debit_amount_oc end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("debit_amount_oc") 
             
        ) 
    ) 
    return general_ledger_df 
 
# COMMAND ---------- 
 
# DBTITLE 1,TB Numeric Fields Truncation 
## This function is used to truncate the monetary fields in the Trial Balance data to 2 decimal places.  
def perform_trunc_tb_numeric_field(trial_balance_df_arg): 
    trial_balance_df = ( 
        trial_balance_df_arg.select(col("chart_of_accounts"),col("entity_id"),col("entity_name"),col("entity_currency_ec"),col("fiscal_year"),col("account_number"),col("account_description"),col("fiscal_period"),col("period_type"),col("period_end_date"),col("group_currency_gc"),col("extract_date"),col("import_date"),col("ledger_id"),col("ledger_group"),col("coa_account_key"),col("misc1"),col("misc2") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', ending_balance_ec) > 0 then substring(cast(ending_balance_ec as string), 1, locate('.', cast(ending_balance_ec as string)) + 2) else ending_balance_ec end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("ending_balance_ec") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', ending_balance_gc) > 0 then substring(cast(ending_balance_gc as string), 1, locate('.', cast(ending_balance_gc as string)) + 2) else ending_balance_gc end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("ending_balance_gc") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', beginning_balance_ec) > 0 then substring(cast(beginning_balance_ec as string), 1, locate('.', cast(beginning_balance_ec as string)) + 2) else beginning_balance_ec end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("beginning_balance_ec") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', beginning_balance_gc) > 0 then substring(cast(beginning_balance_gc as string), 1, locate('.', cast(beginning_balance_gc as string)) + 2) else beginning_balance_gc end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("beginning_balance_gc") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', adjusted_journal_entry_ec) > 0 then substring(cast(adjusted_journal_entry_ec as string), 1, locate('.', cast(adjusted_journal_entry_ec as string)) + 2) else adjusted_journal_entry_ec end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("adjusted_journal_entry_ec") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', adjusted_journal_entry_gc) > 0 then substring(cast(adjusted_journal_entry_gc as string), 1, locate('.', cast(adjusted_journal_entry_gc as string)) + 2) else adjusted_journal_entry_gc end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("adjusted_journal_entry_gc") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', period_activity_ec) > 0 then substring(cast(period_activity_ec as string), 1, locate('.', cast(period_activity_ec as string)) + 2) else period_activity_ec end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("period_activity_ec") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', period_activity_gc) > 0 then substring(cast(period_activity_gc as string), 1, locate('.', cast(period_activity_gc as string)) + 2) else period_activity_gc end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("period_activity_gc") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', consolidation_journal_entry_gc) > 0 then substring(cast(consolidation_journal_entry_gc as string), 1, locate('.', cast(consolidation_journal_entry_gc as string)) + 2) else consolidation_journal_entry_gc end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("consolidation_journal_entry_gc") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', reclassification_journal_entry_ec) > 0 then substring(cast(reclassification_journal_entry_ec as string), 1, locate('.', cast(reclassification_journal_entry_ec as string)) + 2) else reclassification_journal_entry_ec end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("reclassification_journal_entry_ec") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', reclassification_journal_entry_gc) > 0 then substring(cast(reclassification_journal_entry_gc as string), 1, locate('.', cast(reclassification_journal_entry_gc as string)) + 2) else reclassification_journal_entry_gc end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("reclassification_journal_entry_gc") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', adjusted_ec) > 0 then substring(cast(adjusted_ec as string), 1, locate('.', cast(adjusted_ec as string)) + 2) else adjusted_ec end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("adjusted_ec") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', adjusted_gc) > 0 then substring(cast(adjusted_gc as string), 1, locate('.', cast(adjusted_gc as string)) + 2) else adjusted_gc end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("adjusted_gc") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', preliminary_gc) > 0 then substring(cast(preliminary_gc as string), 1, locate('.', cast(preliminary_gc as string)) + 2) else preliminary_gc end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("preliminary_gc") 
            ,(expr("case when locate('.', preliminary_ec) > 0 then substring(cast(preliminary_ec as string), 1, locate('.', cast(preliminary_ec as string)) + 2) else preliminary_ec end").cast("decimal(38,2)")).alias("preliminary_ec") 
 
             
        ) 
    ) 
    return trial_balance_df

# MAGIC %md 
# MAGIC # 5. Preparing General Ledger 
# MAGIC 
# MAGIC **IMPORTANT**:

# COMMAND ---------- 
 
# This function formats and creates all the journal entry fields required for the downstream analytics (Transactions Analysis, JE Insights, Journal Entry module etc.). It is also the function where the vast majority of the data prep logic occurs for the journals data, so if any logic requires updating for the journals, it is likely to be in this function. 
 
 
def prepare_general_ledger( 
    general_ledger_df, 
    fiscal_calendar_df, 
    field_seperator, 
    replace_missing_posted_date, 
    is_standard_when_multiple, 
    is_standard_when_null, 
    fiscal_year, 
    fiscal_year_end, 
    gl_col_excluding_amount, 
    input_format_of_the_date_posted_field, 
    input_format_of_the_date_effective_field, 
    input_format_of_the_time_posted_field, 
    exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, 
    exclusion_list_include_entityid, 
    calendar_fiscal_year, 
    autocorrect_multiple_is_standard, 
): 
    ### Converting amount fields to double(upto 2 decimal places) for both comma seperated/period seperated file. 
    if field_seperator == "Period": 
        general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.select( 
            *gl_col_excluding_amount, 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("net_amount_oc"), "[,\)]", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias("net_amount_oc"), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("net_amount_gc"), "[,\)]", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias("net_amount_gc"), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("net_amount_ec"), "[,\)]", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias("net_amount_ec"), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("debit_amount_oc"), "[,\)]", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias( 
                "debit_amount_oc" 
            ), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("debit_amount_gc"), "[,\)]", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias( 
                "debit_amount_gc" 
            ), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("debit_amount_ec"), "[,\)]", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias( 
                "debit_amount_ec" 
            ), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("credit_amount_oc"), "[,\)]", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias( 
                "credit_amount_oc" 
            ), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("credit_amount_gc"), "[,\)]", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias( 
                "credit_amount_gc" 
            ), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("credit_amount_ec"), "[,\)]", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias( 
                "credit_amount_ec" 
            ) 
        ) 
     
        general_ledger_df = perform_trunc_gl_numeric_field(general_ledger_df) 
    elif field_seperator == "Comma": 
        general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.select( 
            *gl_col_excluding_amount, 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("net_amount_oc"), "[.)]", ""),",", "."),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias("net_amount_oc"), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("net_amount_gc"), "[.\)]", ""),",", "."),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias("net_amount_gc"), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("net_amount_ec"), "[.)]", ""),",", "."),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias("net_amount_ec"), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("debit_amount_oc"), "[.\)]", ""),",", "."),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias( 
                "debit_amount_oc" 
            ), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("debit_amount_gc"), "[.\)]", ""),",", "."),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias( 
                "debit_amount_gc" 
            ), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("debit_amount_ec"), "[.\)]", ""),",", "."),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias( 
                "debit_amount_ec" 
            ), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("credit_amount_oc"), "[.\)]", ""),",", "."),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias( 
                "credit_amount_oc" 
            ), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("credit_amount_gc"), "[.\)]", ""),",", "."),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias( 
                "credit_amount_gc" 
            ), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("credit_amount_ec"), "[.\)]", ""),",", "."),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias( 
                "credit_amount_ec" 
            ) 
        ) 
         
        general_ledger_df = perform_trunc_gl_numeric_field(general_ledger_df) 
    else: 
        general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.select( 
            *gl_col_excluding_amount, 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("net_amount_oc"), "\)", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias("net_amount_oc"), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("net_amount_gc"), "\)", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias("net_amount_gc"), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("net_amount_ec"), "\)", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias("net_amount_ec"), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("debit_amount_oc"), "\)", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias( 
                "debit_amount_oc" 
            ), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("debit_amount_gc"), "\)", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias( 
                "debit_amount_gc" 
            ), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("debit_amount_ec"), "\)", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias( 
                "debit_amount_ec" 
            ), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("credit_amount_oc"), "\)", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias( 
                "credit_amount_oc" 
            ), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("credit_amount_gc"), "\)", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias( 
                "credit_amount_gc" 
            ), 
            regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("credit_amount_ec"), "\)", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)").alias( 
                "credit_amount_ec" 
            ) 
        ) 
     
        general_ledger_df = perform_trunc_gl_numeric_field(general_ledger_df) 
         
    ## ** Placeholder** 
    ## Optional Field Transformation  
    ## Use lines 112-122 below to appropriately identify the listed fields, if needed.  
    ## 1) Enable a line by removing ‘#’ at the start of the line.  
    ## 2) Update the last argument in withColumn() to add field transformations.  
    ## 3) Do not change the first argument (standard field name) in withColumn().  
 
    #general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.withColumn('journal_header_description', col('journal_header_description')) 
    #general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.withColumn('journal_number', col('journal_number')) 
    #general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.withColumn('journal_line_description', col('journal_line_description')) 
    #general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.withColumn('userid_entered', col('userid_entered')) 
    #general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.withColumn('entity_id', col('entity_id')) 
    #general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.withColumn('original_currency_oc', col('original_currency_oc')) 
    #general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.withColumn('group_currency_gc', col('group_currency_gc')) 
    #general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.withColumn('entity_currency_ec', col('entity_currency_ec')) 
    #general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.withColumn('location', col('location')) 
    #general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.withColumn('user_name_entered', col('user_name_entered')) 
    #general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.withColumn('custom_exclusion', col('custom_exclusion')) 
    
    general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.select( 
        general_ledger_df["*"],trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("entity_id"),exclusion_list_include_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("entity_id_1"),regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("journal_number"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3").alias("journal_number_1") 
    ) 
    gl_missing_entity_name = general_ledger_df.filter(isnull(col("entity_name"))|(col("entity_name") == "")) 
    missing_time_posted = general_ledger_df.filter(general_ledger_df.time_posted.isNotNull()).select("time_posted").first() 
    missing_fiscal_period = general_ledger_df.filter(general_ledger_df.fiscal_period.between(1,13)).select("fiscal_period").first() 
 
    ## Below step is used to convert the datatype of the date_posted and date_effectice column to date 
    general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.select(general_ledger_df["*"], 
                                                (to_date(col("date_effective"), input_format_of_the_date_effective_field) 
    ).alias('date_effective_1'), 
                                                (to_date(col("date_posted"), input_format_of_the_date_posted_field) 
    ).alias('date_posted_1')) 
    ## To improve the quality of the data, replacing the date_effective column with date_posted in the below step, if it is empty. 
    if replace_missing_posted_date == "Yes": 
        general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.withColumn( 
            "date_posted_1", 
            when(isnull("date_posted_1"), col("date_effective_1")).otherwise( 
                col("date_posted_1") 
            ), 
        ) 
     
    if missing_fiscal_period is None: 
        if calendar_fiscal_year == "Yes": 
            ## Below steps infer the Current and Prior FY dates(end and start) from the fiscal_year_end date provided in the parameters, so that the financial period for each row can be inferred using the DateEffective 
            general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.select( 
                general_ledger_df["*"], 
                to_date(lit(fiscal_year_end), "MM/dd/yyyy").alias("CurrentFYEnd"), 
            ) 
            general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.select( 
                general_ledger_df["*"], 
                add_months("CurrentFYEnd", -12).alias("PriorFYEnd"), 
            ) 
            general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.select( 
                general_ledger_df["*"], 
                date_add("PriorFYEnd", 1).alias("CurrentFYStart"), 
            ) 
            general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.select( 
                general_ledger_df["*"], 
                add_months("CurrentFYStart", -12).alias("PriorFYStart"), 
            ) 
            # Below steps infer the financial period from the DateEffective by starting from the prior FY start date and adding rolling calendar months to this date (i.e. if the prior FY start date is 01/01/2020, then if fin periods run in calendar months, the end date for period 1 is 01/31/2020, then end date for period 2 is 02/28/2020 and so on) 
            general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.select( 
                general_ledger_df["*"], 
                floor(months_between("date_effective_1", "CurrentFYStart")) 
                .cast("integer") 
                .alias("CurrentFYStart_minus_effective"), 
                floor(months_between("date_effective_1", "PriorFYStart")) 
                .cast("integer") 
                .alias("PriorFYStart_minus_effective") 
            ) 
             
            general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.withColumn( 
                "fiscal_period", 
                when( 
                    (col("CurrentFYStart_minus_effective") >= 0) 
                    & (col("CurrentFYStart_minus_effective") < 12), 
                    col("CurrentFYStart_minus_effective") + 1, 
                ) 
                .when( 
                    (col("PriorFYStart_minus_effective") >= 0) 
                    & (col("PriorFYStart_minus_effective") < 12), 
                    col("PriorFYStart_minus_effective") + 1, 
                ) 
                .otherwise(lit(13)), 
            ) 
        ## If the client does not have a calendar fiscal year, the fiscal period is read from the Fiscal Calendar, which is an optional input. 
 
        else: 
            fiscal_calendar_df = fiscal_calendar_df.select(col("fiscal_period"),  
                to_date(col("fiscal_period_start"), "MM/dd/yyyy").alias("fiscal_period_start"), 
                to_date(col("fiscal_period_end"), "MM/dd/yyyy").alias("fiscal_period_end") 
            ) 
            join_cond = ( 
                general_ledger_df.date_effective_1 
                >= fiscal_calendar_df.fiscal_period_start 
            ) & ( 
                general_ledger_df.date_effective_1 <= fiscal_calendar_df.fiscal_period_end 
            ) 
            general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.join( 
                broadcast(fiscal_calendar_df), join_cond, "left" 
            ).select( 
                general_ledger_df["*"], 
                fiscal_calendar_df.fiscal_period.alias("fiscal_period_calendar"), 
            ) 
            general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.withColumn( 
                "fiscal_period", col("fiscal_period_calendar") 
            ) 
 
    ## ** Placeholder ** 
    ## Optional Field Transformation for Net Amount Fields in GL  
    ## Use lines 220-227 below to appropriately identify the listed fields, if needed.  
    ## 1) Enable a line by removing ‘#’ at the start of the line.  
    ## 2) Update the last argument in withColumn() to add field transformations.  
    ## 3) Do not change the first argument (standard field name) in withColumn().  
 
    ## Net_Amount_EC field been appropriately identified in the below step? 
    # general_ledger_df=general_ledger_df.withColumn('net_amount_ec',col('net_amount_ec')) 
 
    ## Net_Amount_OC field been appropriately identified in the below step? 
    # general_ledger_df=general_ledger_df.withColumn('net_amount_oc',col('net_amount_oc')) 
 
    ## Net_Amount_GC field been appropriately identified in the below step? 
    # general_ledger_df=general_ledger_df.withColumn('net_amount_gc',col('net_amount_gc')) 
 
    ## If the fiscal period column is completely empty, then replacing it with the Fiscal Period inferred in the step above 
    general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.select( 
        general_ledger_df["*"], 
        lit(fiscal_year).alias("fiscal_year_1"), 
        when( 
            ( 
                (is_standard_when_null != lit("Custom")) 
                & (isnull(col("is_standard"))) 
            ), 
            lit(is_standard_when_null), 
        ) 
        ## ** Placeholder ** 
        ## Optional Field Transformation for Is_Standard  
        ## Use line 252 below to add conditions to assign “N” or “S” to missing Is_Standard, if needed.  
        ## Replace ‘lit(None)’ with user-defined formula.  
        ## Usable only when the response to the parameter “When an entry contains missing is_standard, indicate whether the overall entry should be designated as N, S, or a custom value.” is “Custom”.  
        ## Do not disable the section. 
 
        .when( 
            ( 
                (is_standard_when_null == lit("Custom")) 
                &(isnull(col("is_standard"))) 
            ), 
            lit(None), 
        ) 
        .otherwise(col("is_standard")) 
        .alias("is_standard_2"), 
                
        ## In the below steps, deriving the debit and credit amounts from the net amount columns 
        when(col("net_amount_ec") > 0.0, abs("net_amount_ec")) 
        .otherwise(lit(0)) 
        .alias("debit_amount_ec_1"), 
        when(col("net_amount_ec") < 0.0, abs("net_amount_ec")) 
        .otherwise(lit(0)) 
        .alias("credit_amount_ec_1"), 
        when(col("net_amount_oc") > 0.0, abs("net_amount_oc")) 
        .otherwise(lit(0)) 
        .alias("debit_amount_oc_1"), 
        when(col("net_amount_oc") < 0.0, abs("net_amount_oc")) 
        .otherwise(lit(0)) 
        .alias("credit_amount_oc_1"), 
        when(col("net_amount_gc") > 0.0, abs("net_amount_gc")) 
        .otherwise(lit(0)) 
        .alias("debit_amount_gc_1"), 
        when(col("net_amount_gc") < 0.0, abs("net_amount_gc")) 
        .otherwise(lit(0)) 
        .alias("credit_amount_gc_1"), 
        when(col("entity_name").isNull(),col("entity_id_1")).otherwise(col("entity_name")).alias("entity_name_1") 
        ) 
   
    ## In the below step, mapping the date posted value to the time posted field, if its empty 
    if missing_time_posted is None: 
        general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.withColumn("time_posted", date_format(('date_posted_1'), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss')) 
    else: 
        general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.withColumn( 
            "time_posted", 
            date_format( 
                to_timestamp(col("time_posted"), input_format_of_the_time_posted_field), 
                "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", 
            ), 
        ) 
 
    general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.select( 
        general_ledger_df["*"], col("is_standard_2").alias("copy_is_standard_2") 
    ) 
 
    ##The step below computes the number of distinct values for IsStandard for each journal entry ID, this is so that the multiple IsStandard DQC can be automatically resolved for any journals with more than one distinct IsStandard value later on in the recipe 
    general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.select( 
        general_ledger_df["*"], 
        size( 
            collect_set(when(col("is_standard_2").isNull(),lit('')).otherwise(col("is_standard_2"))).over(Window().partitionBy(col("journal_number_1"),col("entity_id_1"))) 
        ).alias("count_distinct_is_standard_2"), 
        count("*").over(Window().partitionBy(col("journal_number_1"),col("entity_id_1"))).alias("count_is_standard")) 
     
    gl_muliple_is_standard = general_ledger_df.select(col("journal_number_1"),col("entity_id_1"),col("count_distinct_is_standard_2"),col("count_is_standard")).distinct() 
 
    if(autocorrect_multiple_is_standard == 'Yes'): 
        general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.withColumn("is_standard_2", 
                                                         when( 
                                                             col("count_distinct_is_standard_2") > 1, lit(is_standard_when_multiple)).otherwise(col("is_standard_2")), 
                                                         ) 
    ## Replacing the special characters in the general ledger file     
    general_ledger_df = general_ledger_df.select( 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("chart_of_accounts"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("chart_of_accounts"), 
        col("entity_id_1").alias("entity_id"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("entity_name_1"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("entity_name"), 
        trim(col("journal_number_1")).alias("journal_number"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("journal_header_description"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("journal_header_description"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("dc_indicator"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("dc_indicator"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("journal_line_number"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("journal_line_number"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("journal_line_description"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("journal_line_description"), 
        col("fiscal_year_1").alias("fiscal_year"), 
        col("fiscal_period"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("transaction_type"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("transaction_type"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("transaction_type_description"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("transaction_type_description"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("entity_currency_ec"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("entity_currency_ec"), 
        col("date_posted_1").alias("date_posted"), 
        col("date_effective_1").alias("date_effective"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("userid_entered"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("userid_entered"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("user_name_entered"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("user_name_entered"), 
        col("time_posted"), 
        col("date_updated"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("updated_by"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("updated_by"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("name_of_user_updated"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("name_of_user_updated"), 
        col("time_updated"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("userid_approved"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("userid_approved"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("user_name_approved"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("user_name_approved"), 
        col("date_approved"), 
        col("time_approved"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("is_standard_2"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("is_standard"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("is_manual"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("is_manual"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("source"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("source"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("source_description"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("source_description"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("account_number"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("account_number"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("account_description"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("account_description"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("coa_account_key"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("coa_account_key"), 
        col("exchange_rate"), 
        col("foreign_exchange_date"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("forex_conversion_method"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("forex_conversion_method"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("original_currency_oc"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("original_currency_oc"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("group_currency_gc"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("group_currency_gc"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("business_area"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("business_area"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("controlling_area_for_cost_and_profit_center"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("controlling_area_for_cost_and_profit_center"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("cost_center"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("cost_center"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("profit_center"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("profit_center"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("project"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("project"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("location"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("location"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("custom_exclusion"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("custom_exclusion"), 
        col("extract_date"), 
        col("import_date"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("ledger_id"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("ledger_id"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("ledger_group"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("ledger_group"), 
        col("net_amount_oc"), 
        col("net_amount_gc"), 
        col("net_amount_ec"), 
        col("debit_amount_oc_1").alias("debit_amount_oc"), 
        col("debit_amount_gc_1").alias("debit_amount_gc"), 
        col("debit_amount_ec_1").alias("debit_amount_ec"), 
        col("credit_amount_oc_1").alias("credit_amount_oc"), 
        col("credit_amount_gc_1").alias("credit_amount_gc"), 
        col("credit_amount_ec_1").alias("credit_amount_ec"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("copy_is_standard_2"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("copy_is_standard_2"), 
    ) 
    number_of_records_gl=general_ledger_df.count() 
    return general_ledger_df,missing_time_posted,missing_fiscal_period,gl_missing_entity_name,gl_muliple_is_standard,number_of_records_gl

# MAGIC # 6. Preparing Trial Balance 
# MAGIC 
# MAGIC **IMPORTANT**: 
# MAGIC In this section, there are places where the user can enable optional transformation steps for specific fields. Please review the following lines in the cell below to appropriately define the fields as necessary: 
# MAGIC 1. Line 33: Replace net amount fields with another 
# MAGIC 
# MAGIC **Do not edit, remove or disable any part of the code in this section other than those specified above.**  
# MAGIC 
# MAGIC Once reviewed, go to section 7. 
 
# COMMAND ---------- 
 
# This function formats and creates all the trial balance fields required for the downstream analytics 
def prepare_trial_balance( 
    trial_balance_df, 
    field_seperator, 
    tb_col_excluding_amount, 
    tb_col_amount, 
    exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, 
    exclusion_list_include_entityid, 
    input_format_of_the_period_end_date_field 
): 
    if field_seperator == "Period": 
        ## Converting amount fields to double(upto 2 decimal places) for both comma seperated /period seperated file. 
        trial_balance_df = trial_balance_df.select(*tb_col_excluding_amount,*[(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col(col1), "[,\)]", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)")).alias(col1) for col1 in tb_col_amount]) 
         
         
        trial_balance_df = perform_trunc_tb_numeric_field(trial_balance_df) 
    elif field_seperator == "Comma": 
        trial_balance_df = trial_balance_df.select(*tb_col_excluding_amount,*[(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col(col1), "[.\)]", ""),",", "."),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)")).alias(col1) for col1 in tb_col_amount]) 
         
        trial_balance_df = perform_trunc_tb_numeric_field(trial_balance_df) 
    else: 
        trial_balance_df = trial_balance_df.select(*tb_col_excluding_amount,*[(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col(col1), "\)", ""),"\(", "-").cast("decimal(38,6)")).alias(col1) for col1 in tb_col_amount]) 
        
        trial_balance_df = perform_trunc_tb_numeric_field(trial_balance_df) 
 
    trial_balance_df = ( 
        trial_balance_df.select(trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("chart_of_accounts"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("chart_of_accounts"),trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("entity_currency_ec"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("entity_currency_ec"),col('period_end_date'),col("fiscal_year"),col("fiscal_period"),trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("period_type"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("period_type"),col("beginning_balance_ec"),col("period_activity_ec"),col("ending_balance_ec"),trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("group_currency_gc"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("group_currency_gc"),col("beginning_balance_gc"),col("period_activity_gc"),col("ending_balance_gc"),col("preliminary_ec"),col("adjusted_journal_entry_ec"),col("adjusted_ec"),col("reclassification_journal_entry_ec"),col("preliminary_gc"),col("adjusted_journal_entry_gc"),col("adjusted_gc"),col("reclassification_journal_entry_gc"),col("consolidation_journal_entry_gc"),col("extract_date"),col("import_date"),trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("ledger_id"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("ledger_id"),trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("ledger_group"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("ledger_group"),trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("coa_account_key"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("coa_account_key"),trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("entity_id"), 
        exclusion_list_include_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("entity_id"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("entity_name"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("entity_name"),trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("account_number"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("account_number"),trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("account_description"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("account_description"), 
        ) 
    ) 
 
    ## ** Placeholder ** 
    ## Optional Field Transformation for Net Amount Fields in TB  
    ## Use lines 40-44 below to appropriately identify the listed fields, if needed. 
    ## 1) Enable a line by removing ‘#’ at the start of the line.  
    ## 2) Update the last argument in withColumn() to add field transformations.  
    ## 3) Do not change the first argument (standard field name) in withColumn().  
 
    ## Ending_Balance_GC field been appropriately identified in the below step? 
    # trial_balance_df=trial_balance_df.withColumn('ending_balance_gc',col('ending_balance_gc')) 
 
    ## Ending_Balance_EC field been appropriately identified in the below step? 
    # trial_balance_df=trial_balance_df.withColumn('ending_balance_ec',col('ending_balance_ec')) 
 
    tb_incorrect_period_type = trial_balance_df.filter(~upper(col("period_type")).isin("MTD","YTD","QTD")| isnull(col("period_type"))|(col("period_type") == "")) 
    tb_missing_entity_name = trial_balance_df.filter(isnull(col("entity_name"))|(col("entity_name") == "")) 
    ## Below step is used to change the data type of period_end_date column to date and Period type to "YTD" incase of any unaccepted values in period type 
 
    trial_balance_df = ( 
        trial_balance_df.select(col("chart_of_accounts"),col("entity_currency_ec"),col("fiscal_year"),col("fiscal_period"),col("beginning_balance_ec"),col("period_activity_ec"),col("ending_balance_ec"),col("group_currency_gc"),col("beginning_balance_gc"),col("period_activity_gc"),col("ending_balance_gc"),col("preliminary_ec"),col("adjusted_journal_entry_ec"),col("adjusted_ec"),col("reclassification_journal_entry_ec"),col("preliminary_gc"),col("adjusted_journal_entry_gc"),col("adjusted_gc"),col("reclassification_journal_entry_gc"),col("consolidation_journal_entry_gc"),col("extract_date"),col("import_date"),col("ledger_id"),col("ledger_group"),col("coa_account_key"),col("entity_id"),when(col("entity_name").isNull(),col("entity_id")).otherwise(col("entity_name")).alias("entity_name"),col("account_description"), 
                                to_date(col("period_end_date"), 
                                        input_format_of_the_period_end_date_field).alias("period_end_date"), 
                                col("account_number"), 
                                (when((~upper(col("period_type")).isin("MTD","YTD","QTD")|isnull(col("period_type"))|(col("period_type") == "")),lit("YTD")) 
                                                       .otherwise(upper(col("period_type")))).alias("period_type")) 
    ) 
    return trial_balance_df, tb_incorrect_period_type,tb_missing_entity_name
