# MAGIC %md 
# MAGIC # 7. Preparing Chart of Accounts 
# MAGIC 
# MAGIC **IMPORTANT**: 
# MAGIC In this section, there are places where the user can enable optional transformation steps for specific fields. Please review the following lines in the cell below to appropriately define the fields as necessary: 
# MAGIC 1. Line 43: Add user-defined formula for Financial Statement Category 
# MAGIC 
# MAGIC **Do not edit, remove or disable any part of the code in this section other than those specified above.**  
# MAGIC 
# MAGIC Once reviewed, go to section 9. 
 
# COMMAND ---------- 
 
# This function formats and creates all the chart of account fields required for the downstream analytics 
def prepare_chart_of_accounts(chart_of_accounts_df,exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, exclusion_list_include_entityid): 
    # In the beow step, changing the financial_statement_category to plural form 
    chart_of_accounts_df = chart_of_accounts_df.withColumn( 
        "financial_statement_category", 
        when( 
            upper(col("financial_statement_category")).isin(["ASSETS", "ASSET"]), 
            "Assets", 
        ) 
        .when( 
            upper(col("financial_statement_category")).isin( 
                ["LIABILITIES", "LIABILITY"] 
            ), 
            "Liabilities", 
        ) 
        .when( 
            upper(col("financial_statement_category")).isin(["EXPENSES", "EXPENSE"]), 
            "Expenses", 
        ) 
        .when( 
            upper(col("financial_statement_category")).isin(["EQUITIES", "EQUITY"]), 
            "Equity", 
        ) 
        .when( 
            upper(col("financial_statement_category")).isin(["REVENUES", "REVENUE"]), 
            "Revenue", 
        ) 
        .otherwise(col("financial_statement_category")), 
    ) 
    chart_of_accounts_df = chart_of_accounts_df.withColumn("account_grouping_1_num", 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("account_grouping_1_num"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("account_grouping_1_num")) 
     
    chart_of_accounts_df = chart_of_accounts_df.withColumn("account_grouping_1_num", when(col("account_grouping_1_num")=="", None).otherwise(col("account_grouping_1_num"))) 
     
    missing_financial_statement_category = chart_of_accounts_df.filter(chart_of_accounts_df.financial_statement_category.isNotNull()).select("financial_statement_category").first() 
    missing_financial_statement_line = chart_of_accounts_df.filter(chart_of_accounts_df.financial_statement_line.isNotNull()).select("financial_statement_line").first() 
    missing_account_grouping_1 = chart_of_accounts_df.filter(chart_of_accounts_df.account_grouping_1.isNotNull()).select("account_grouping_1").first() 
    missing_revision_date = chart_of_accounts_df.filter(chart_of_accounts_df.revision_date.isNotNull()).select("revision_date").first() 
    missing_financial_statement_subtotal_category = chart_of_accounts_df.filter(chart_of_accounts_df.financial_statement_subtotal_category.isNotNull()).select("financial_statement_subtotal_category").first() 
     
    ## If the financial_statement_cateogry column is empty, deriving it from first digit of the account_number 
    if missing_financial_statement_category is None: 
        ## ** Placeholder ** 
        ## Optional Field Transformation for Financial_Statement_Category  
        ## Use lines 52 - 59 below to appropriately identify the field, if needed.  
        ## 1) Enable a line by removing ‘#’ at the start of the line and disable line 51 and 60. 
        ## 2) Update the formula to add field transformations.  
        ## 3) Do not change the 5 pre-defined FS category values.  
 
        ## Financial_Statement_Category field been appropriately defined in the below step? 
        ( 
        #chart_of_accounts_df = chart_of_accounts_df.withColumn( 
        #    "financial_statement_category", 
        #    when(substring(col("account_number"), 1, 1) == 1, "Assets") 
        #   .when(substring(col("account_number"), 1, 1) == 2, "Liabilities") 
        #   .when(substring(col("account_number"), 1, 1) == 3, "Equity") 
        #   .when(substring(col("account_number"), 1, 1) == 4, "Revenue") 
        #   .otherwise("Expenses"), 
        #) 
        ) 
    ## If the financial_statement_line column is empty, mapping the financial_statement_category to it 
    if missing_financial_statement_line is None: 
        chart_of_accounts_df = chart_of_accounts_df.withColumn( 
            "financial_statement_line", col("financial_statement_category") 
        ) 
    ## If the account_grouping_1 column is empty, mapping the financial_statement_line to it 
    if missing_account_grouping_1 is None: 
        chart_of_accounts_df = chart_of_accounts_df.withColumn( 
            "account_grouping_1", col("financial_statement_line") 
        ) 
    if missing_revision_date is None: 
        chart_of_accounts_df = chart_of_accounts_df.withColumn( 
            "revision_date", current_date() 
        ) 
     
    chart_of_accounts_df = chart_of_accounts_df.select(chart_of_accounts_df["*"],trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("entity_id"),exclusion_list_include_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("entity_id_1")) 
 
    coa_2_temp_df = chart_of_accounts_df.groupby("account_grouping_1").agg( 
        count_distinct("financial_statement_line") 
        .cast("int") 
        .alias("count_fsline_account_grouping"),count("*").alias("Number_of_ag1_fsline_corrected") 
    ) 
 
    coa_3_temp_df = chart_of_accounts_df.groupby("financial_statement_line").agg( 
        count_distinct("financial_statement_category") 
        .cast("int") 
        .alias("count_fs_category_fs_line"),count("*").alias("Number_of_fsline_corrected") 
    ) 
 
    chart_of_accounts_df = chart_of_accounts_df.join( 
        coa_2_temp_df, 
        chart_of_accounts_df.account_grouping_1 
        == coa_2_temp_df.account_grouping_1, 
        "left", 
    ).drop(coa_2_temp_df.account_grouping_1) 
 
    coa_missing_entity_name= chart_of_accounts_df.filter(isnull(col("entity_name"))|(col("entity_name") == "")) 
 
    chart_of_accounts_df = chart_of_accounts_df.select( 
        col("chart_of_accounts"), 
        col("entity_id_1"), 
        when(col("entity_name").isNull(),col("entity_id_1")).otherwise(col("entity_name")).alias("entity_name"), 
        col("revision_date"), 
        col("account_number"), 
        col("account_description"), 
        col("account_grouping_1_num"), 
        col("financial_statement_line_num"), 
        col("financial_statement_line"), 
        col("financial_statement_subtotal_category_id"), 
        col("financial_statement_subtotal_category"), 
        col("financial_statement_category_id"), 
        col("financial_statement_category"), 
        when( 
            col("financial_statement_category").isin( 
                ["Assets", "Equity", "Liabilities"] 
            ), 
            "Balance Sheet", 
        ).when( 
            col("financial_statement_category").isin( 
                ["Revenue","Expenses"] 
            ), 
            "Income Statement", 
        ).otherwise(lit("")).alias("financial_statement_type"), 
        col("abcotd_id"), 
        col("abcotd"), 
        col("abcotd_significance"), 
        col("content_area_id"), 
        col("content_area"), 
        col("extract_date"), 
        col("import_date"), 
        col("tags"), 
        col("coa_account_key"), 
        col("account_number"), 
        col("account_grouping_1"), 
        col("Number_of_ag1_fsline_corrected"), 
        col("count_fsline_account_grouping") 
    ) 
    # If the account_grouping is mapped to more than one financial_statement_line, concatenating the account_grouping and financial_statement_line fields in the following step. 
 
    chart_of_accounts_df = chart_of_accounts_df.select(chart_of_accounts_df["*"], 
        when( 
            col("count_fsline_account_grouping") > 1, 
            concat_ws( 
                "-", 
                col("account_grouping_1"), 
                col("financial_statement_line"), 
            ), 
        ).otherwise(col("account_grouping_1")).alias("ag1_multiple_fsline_with_correction"), 
    ) 
    coa_ag1_fsline_corrected = chart_of_accounts_df 
 
    coa_4_temp_df = chart_of_accounts_df.groupby("ag1_multiple_fsline_with_correction").agg( 
        count_distinct("financial_statement_category") 
        .cast("int") 
        .alias("count_fscategory_account_grouping"),count("*").alias("Number_of_account_grouping_1_corrected") 
    ) 
 
    chart_of_accounts_df = chart_of_accounts_df.join( 
        coa_4_temp_df, 
        chart_of_accounts_df.ag1_multiple_fsline_with_correction 
        == coa_4_temp_df.ag1_multiple_fsline_with_correction, 
        "left", 
    ).drop(coa_4_temp_df.ag1_multiple_fsline_with_correction) 
 
    # If the account_grouping is mapped to more than one financial_statement_category, concatenating the account_grouping and financial_statement_category fields in the following step 
 
    chart_of_accounts_df = chart_of_accounts_df.select(chart_of_accounts_df["*"], 
        when( 
            col("count_fscategory_account_grouping") > 1, 
            concat_ws( 
                "-", 
                col("ag1_multiple_fsline_with_correction"), 
                col("financial_statement_category"), 
            ), 
        ).otherwise(col("ag1_multiple_fsline_with_correction")).alias("ag1_multiple_fscategory_with_correction"), 
    ) 
    coa_ag1_fscategory_corrected = chart_of_accounts_df 
 
    chart_of_accounts_df = chart_of_accounts_df.join( 
        coa_3_temp_df, 
        chart_of_accounts_df.financial_statement_line 
        == coa_3_temp_df.financial_statement_line, 
        "left", 
    ).drop(coa_3_temp_df.financial_statement_line) 
 
    ## If the financial_statement_line is mapped to more than one financial_statement_category, concatenating the financial_statement_line and financial_statement_category fields in the following step. 
    chart_of_accounts_df = chart_of_accounts_df.select(chart_of_accounts_df["*"], 
        when( 
            col("count_fs_category_fs_line") > 1, 
            concat_ws( 
                "-", 
                col("financial_statement_line"), 
                col("financial_statement_category"), 
            ), 
        ).otherwise(col("financial_statement_line")).alias("fsline_multiple_fscategory_with_correction") 
    ) 
    coa_fsline_fscategory_corrected = chart_of_accounts_df 
 
    ## Replacing the special characters in the COA file 
    chart_of_accounts_df = chart_of_accounts_df.select( 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("chart_of_accounts"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("chart_of_accounts"), 
        col("entity_id_1").alias("entity_id"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("entity_name"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("entity_name"), 
        col("revision_date"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("account_description"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("account_description"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("account_grouping_1_num"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("account_grouping_1_num"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("financial_statement_line_num"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("financial_statement_line_num"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("financial_statement_subtotal_category_id"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("financial_statement_subtotal_category_id"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("financial_statement_subtotal_category"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("financial_statement_subtotal_category"), 
        trim(col("financial_statement_category_id")).alias("financial_statement_category_id"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("financial_statement_category"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("financial_statement_category"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("financial_statement_type"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("financial_statement_type"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("abcotd_id"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("abcotd_id"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("abcotd"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("abcotd"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("abcotd_significance"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("abcotd_significance"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("content_area_id"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("content_area_id"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("content_area"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("content_area"), 
        col("extract_date"), 
        col("import_date"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("tags"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("tags"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("coa_account_key"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("coa_account_key"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("account_number"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("account_number"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("ag1_multiple_fscategory_with_correction"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("account_grouping_1"), 
        trim(regexp_replace(regexp_replace(col("fsline_multiple_fscategory_with_correction"),exclusion_list_exclude_entityid, ""),"(?i)(N)(/)(?i)(A)","$1$3")).alias("financial_statement_line"), 
    ) 
 
    if missing_financial_statement_subtotal_category is None: 
        chart_of_accounts_df = chart_of_accounts_df.withColumn( 
            "financial_statement_subtotal_category", 
            when( 
                upper(col("financial_statement_category")).isin( 
                    ["REVENUE", "EXPENSES", "EQUITY"] 
                ), 
                "Equity", 
            ).otherwise(col("financial_statement_category")), 
        ) 
    number_of_records_coa=chart_of_accounts_df.count() 
    return chart_of_accounts_df,coa_ag1_fsline_corrected,coa_ag1_fscategory_corrected,coa_fsline_fscategory_corrected,missing_financial_statement_category,missing_financial_statement_line,missing_account_grouping_1,missing_revision_date,missing_financial_statement_subtotal_category,number_of_records_coa,coa_missing_entity_name 
 
# COMMAND ---------- 
 
# DBTITLE 1,GL and COA Output Function 
 
## This function is used to create general_ledger_detail and chart_of_accounts outputs 
def get_je_coa_outputs( 
    general_ledger_df, 
    trial_balance_df, 
    first_day_testing_period, 
    last_day_testing_period, 
    include_prior_balance, 
    chart_of_accounts_df 
): 
    ## Below step is used to have character limit up to 4000 for specific column in the general_ledger file to make sure the data flows through the Cortex JET without issue 
    general_ledger_detail = ( 
        general_ledger_df.select(col("chart_of_accounts"),col("dc_indicator"),col("journal_line_number"),col('date_posted'),col('date_effective'),col("fiscal_year"),col("fiscal_period"),col("transaction_type"),col("transaction_type_description"),col("entity_currency_ec"),col("userid_entered"),col("user_name_entered"),col("time_posted"),col("date_updated"),col("updated_by"),col("name_of_user_updated"),col("time_updated"),col("userid_approved"),col("user_name_approved"),col("date_approved"),col("time_approved"),col("is_standard"),col("is_manual"),col("source"),col("source_description"),col("account_description"),col("net_amount_gc"),col("net_amount_ec"),col("net_amount_oc"),col("debit_amount_gc"),col("debit_amount_ec"),col("debit_amount_oc"),col("credit_amount_gc"),col("credit_amount_ec"),col("credit_amount_oc"),col("coa_account_key"),col("exchange_rate"),col("foreign_exchange_date"),col("forex_conversion_method"),col("original_currency_oc"),col("group_currency_gc"),col("business_area"),col("controlling_area_for_cost_and_profit_center"),col("cost_center"),col("profit_center"),col("project"),col("location"),col("custom_exclusion"),col("extract_date"),col("import_date"),col("ledger_id"),col("ledger_group"), 
        substring(col("journal_number"), 1, 255).alias("journal_number"), 
        substring(col("journal_header_description"), 1, 4000).alias("journal_header_description"), 
        substring(col("journal_line_description"), 1, 4000).alias("journal_line_description"), 
        substring(col("account_number"), 1, 150).alias("account_number"), 
        substring(col("entity_id"), 1, 150).alias("entity_id"), 
        substring(col("entity_name"), 1, 150).alias("entity_name") 
        ) 
    ) 
    ## Below step is used to ensure that the date_posted and date_effectice column are formatted correctly, so converting date_effective and date_posted to MM/dd/yyyy 
    trial_balance_df = trial_balance_df.select( 
        trial_balance_df["*"], 
        to_date(lit(first_day_testing_period), "MM/dd/yyyy").alias("Prior_Period_End_Date_1"), 
        to_date(lit(last_day_testing_period), "MM/dd/yyyy").alias("Current_Period_End_Date") 
    ) 
    trial_balance_df = trial_balance_df.select( 
        trial_balance_df["*"], 
        date_add(col("Prior_Period_End_Date_1"), -1).alias("Prior_Period_End_Date"), 
    ) 
    trial_balance_df = trial_balance_df.select( 
        trial_balance_df["*"], 
        when( 
            col("period_end_date") == col("Current_Period_End_Date"), 
            "Current Period Ending", 
        ) 
        .when( 
            col("period_end_date") == col("Prior_Period_End_Date"), 
            "Current Period Beginning", 
        ) 
        .otherwise(None) 
        .alias("Fiscal_Year_Identifier"), 
        lit(include_prior_balance).alias("Include_Prior_Period_Ending_Balance"), 
    ) 
    trial_balance_df_for_reconciliation = trial_balance_df.select( 
        "entity_id", 
        "entity_name", 
        "account_number", 
        "account_description", 
        "ending_balance_ec", 
        "ending_balance_gc", 
        "period_end_date", 
        "entity_currency_ec", 
        "group_currency_gc", 
        "Fiscal_Year_Identifier" 
    ) 
    ## Below step is used to used to filter the prior period data in the trial balance file based on the parameter(Include_Prior_Period_Ending_Balance) 
    trial_balance_df = trial_balance_df.filter( 
        ( 
            (col("Include_Prior_Period_Ending_Balance") == "No") 
            & (col("Fiscal_Year_Identifier") == "Current Period Ending") 
        ) 
        | (col("Include_Prior_Period_Ending_Balance") == "Yes") 
    ) 
    trial_balance = ( 
        trial_balance_df.select( 
        col("chart_of_accounts"),col("entity_currency_ec"),col("fiscal_year"),col("account_description"),col('period_end_date'),col("fiscal_period"),col("period_type"),col("beginning_balance_ec"),col("period_activity_ec"),col("ending_balance_ec"),col("group_currency_gc"),col("beginning_balance_gc"),col("period_activity_gc"),col("ending_balance_gc"),col("preliminary_ec"),col("adjusted_journal_entry_ec"),col("adjusted_ec"),col("reclassification_journal_entry_ec"),col("preliminary_gc"),col("adjusted_journal_entry_gc"),col("adjusted_gc"),col("reclassification_journal_entry_gc"),col("consolidation_journal_entry_gc"),col("extract_date"),col("import_date"),col("ledger_id"),col("ledger_group"),col("coa_account_key"), 
        substring(col("account_number"), 1, 150).alias("account_number"), 
        substring(col("entity_id"), 1, 150).alias("entity_id"), 
        substring(col("entity_name"), 1, 150).alias("entity_name") 
        ) 
    ) 
         
    ## Below step is used to have character limit up to 4000 for specific column in the COA file to make sure the data flows through the Cortex JET without issue 
    chart_of_accounts = ( 
        chart_of_accounts_df.select( 
        col("chart_of_accounts"),col("revision_date"),col("account_grouping_1_num"),col("financial_statement_line_num"),col("financial_statement_subtotal_category_id"),col("financial_statement_subtotal_category"),col("financial_statement_category_id"),col("financial_statement_category"),col("financial_statement_type"),col("abcotd_id"),col("abcotd"),col("abcotd_significance"),col("content_area_id"),col("content_area"),col("extract_date"),col("import_date"),col("tags"),col("coa_account_key"), 
        substring(col("financial_statement_line"), 1, 4000).alias("financial_statement_line"), 
        substring(col("account_description"), 1, 4000).alias("account_description"), 
        substring(col("account_grouping_1"), 1, 4000).alias("account_grouping_1"), 
        substring(col("account_number"), 1, 150).alias("account_number"), 
        substring(col("entity_id"), 1, 150).alias("entity_id"), 
        substring(col("entity_name"), 1, 150).alias("entity_name") 
        ) 
    ) 
 
    return ( 
        general_ledger_detail, 
        trial_balance, 
        chart_of_accounts, 
        trial_balance_df_for_reconciliation, 
    ) 
 
# COMMAND ---------- 
 
# DBTITLE 1,Recon and Unreconciled Accounts 
## This function is used to capture reconciled and unreconciled accounts 
def perform_reconciliation( 
    chart_of_accounts_df, 
    trial_balance_df_for_reconciliation, 
    general_ledger_df, 
    is_test_period_from_beginning, 
    currency 
): 
    chart_of_accounts_df_for_reconciliation = chart_of_accounts_df.select( 
        col("entity_id").alias("entity_id_chart_of_accounts"), 
        col("entity_name"), 
        col("account_description"), 
        col("account_number").alias("account_number_chart_of_accounts"), 
        col("financial_statement_line"), 
        col("financial_statement_subtotal_category"), 
        col("financial_statement_category"), 
        col("abcotd"), 
        col("account_grouping_1"), 
    ) 
    trial_balance_df_for_reconciliation = ( 
        trial_balance_df_for_reconciliation.groupby("entity_id", "account_number") 
        .pivot("Fiscal_Year_Identifier") 
        .sum("ending_balance_gc", "ending_balance_ec") 
    ) 
    trial_balance_df_for_reconciliation = trial_balance_df_for_reconciliation.select( 
        col("entity_id"), 
        col("account_number"), 
        col("Current Period Beginning_sum(ending_balance_gc)").alias( 
            "beginning_balance_gc" 
        ), 
        col("Current Period Beginning_sum(ending_balance_ec)").alias( 
            "beginning_balance_ec" 
        ), 
        col("Current Period Ending_sum(ending_balance_gc)").alias("ending_balance_gc"), 
        col("Current Period Ending_sum(ending_balance_ec)").alias("ending_balance_ec"), 
    ) 
    tb_coa_joined = trial_balance_df_for_reconciliation.join( 
        chart_of_accounts_df_for_reconciliation, 
        ((trial_balance_df_for_reconciliation.entity_id 
        == chart_of_accounts_df_for_reconciliation.entity_id_chart_of_accounts) &(trial_balance_df_for_reconciliation.account_number 
        == chart_of_accounts_df_for_reconciliation.account_number_chart_of_accounts)), 
        "fullouter", 
    ).select(col("entity_id").alias("entity_id_trial_balance"), 
    col("account_number").alias("account_number_trial_balance"), 
    col("beginning_balance_gc"), 
    col("beginning_balance_ec"), 
    col("ending_balance_gc"), 
    col("ending_balance_ec"), 
    col("entity_id_chart_of_accounts"), 
    col("entity_name"), 
    col("account_description"), 
    col("account_number_chart_of_accounts"), 
    col("financial_statement_line"), 
    col("financial_statement_subtotal_category"), 
    col("financial_statement_category"), 
    col("abcotd"), 
    col("account_grouping_1")) 
     
    tb_coa_joined = tb_coa_joined.select( 
        tb_coa_joined["*"], 
        when( 
            ((isnull(col("account_number_trial_balance"))) | (col("account_number_trial_balance") == "")), 
            col("account_number_chart_of_accounts"), 
        ) 
        .otherwise(col("account_number_trial_balance")) 
        .alias("account_number"), 
        when( 
            ((isnull(col("entity_id_trial_balance"))) | (col("entity_id_trial_balance") == "")), 
            col("entity_id_chart_of_accounts"), 
        ) 
        .otherwise(col("entity_id_trial_balance")) 
        .alias("entity_id_1"), 
    ) 
    gl_tb_coa_joined = general_ledger_df.join( 
        (tb_coa_joined.hint('SHUFFLE_HASH')), 
        ((general_ledger_df.entity_id == tb_coa_joined.entity_id_1) & (general_ledger_df.account_number == tb_coa_joined.account_number)), 
        "outer", 
    ).select( 
        general_ledger_df.account_number, 
        tb_coa_joined.account_number.alias("account_number_1"), 
        general_ledger_df.entity_id, 
        general_ledger_df.entity_name, 
        general_ledger_df.journal_number, 
        general_ledger_df.transaction_type, 
        general_ledger_df.transaction_type_description, 
        general_ledger_df.date_posted, 
        general_ledger_df.date_effective, 
        general_ledger_df.account_description.alias("account_description_1"), 
        general_ledger_df.net_amount_oc, 
        general_ledger_df.net_amount_gc, 
        general_ledger_df.net_amount_ec, 
        general_ledger_df.credit_amount_gc, 
        general_ledger_df.debit_amount_gc, 
        general_ledger_df.credit_amount_ec, 
        general_ledger_df.debit_amount_ec, 
        general_ledger_df.credit_amount_oc, 
        general_ledger_df.debit_amount_oc, 
        tb_coa_joined.account_number_trial_balance, 
        tb_coa_joined.account_number_chart_of_accounts, 
        tb_coa_joined.beginning_balance_gc, 
        tb_coa_joined.beginning_balance_ec, 
        tb_coa_joined.ending_balance_gc, 
        tb_coa_joined.ending_balance_ec, 
        tb_coa_joined.account_description, 
        tb_coa_joined.financial_statement_subtotal_category, 
        tb_coa_joined.financial_statement_line, 
        tb_coa_joined.financial_statement_category, 
        tb_coa_joined.account_grouping_1, 
        tb_coa_joined.entity_id_1 
    ) 
    gl_tb_coa_joined = gl_tb_coa_joined.filter( 
        ((col("account_number").isNotNull()) | (col("account_number") != "")) 
        | ( 
            (col("account_number_1").isNotNull()) 
            | (col("account_number_1") != "") 
        ) 
    ) 
    reconciliation = gl_tb_coa_joined.select( 
        gl_tb_coa_joined["*"], 
        when( 
            ((col("account_number").isNull()) | (col("account_number") == "")), 
            col("account_number_1"), 
        ) 
        .otherwise(col("account_number")) 
        .alias("account_number_new"), 
        when( 
            ( 
                (col("account_description").isNull()) 
                | (col("account_description") == "") 
            ), 
            col("account_description_1"), 
        ) 
        .otherwise(col("account_description")) 
        .alias("account_description_new"), 
        when( 
            ( 
                (col("entity_id").isNull()) 
                | (col("entity_id") == "") 
            ), 
            col("entity_id_1"), 
        ) 
        .otherwise(col("entity_id")) 
        .alias("entity_id_new"), 
        when( 
            ((col("account_number").isNotNull()) | (col("account_number") != "")) 
            & ( 
                (col("account_number_trial_balance").isNotNull()) 
                | (col("account_number_trial_balance") != "") 
            ), 
            "Both", 
        ) 
        .when( 
            ( 
                (col("account_number_trial_balance").isNotNull()) 
                | (col("account_number_trial_balance") != "") 
            ), 
            "TB", 
        ) 
        .when( 
            ((col("account_number").isNotNull()) | (col("account_number") != "")), "JE" 
        ) 
        .otherwise(None) 
        .alias("found_in") 
    ) 
 
    reconciliation = reconciliation.filter(~(col("found_in")=='TB') | ~(col('ending_balance_gc')==0) | ~(col('beginning_balance_gc')==0) |  ~(col('ending_balance_ec')==0) | ~(col('beginning_balance_ec')==0) ) 
 
    if currency == "Group Currency": 
        reconciliation = reconciliation.select("account_number_new", "account_description_new","entity_id_new","financial_statement_subtotal_category","found_in","account_grouping_1",col("ending_balance_gc").alias("ending_balance"),col("beginning_balance_gc").alias("beginning_balance"),"financial_statement_line","financial_statement_category",col("debit_amount_gc").alias("debit_amount"),col("credit_amount_gc").alias("credit_amount"),col("net_amount_gc").alias("net_amount"),"journal_number") 
    else: 
        reconciliation = reconciliation.select("account_number_new", "account_description_new","entity_id_new","financial_statement_subtotal_category","found_in","account_grouping_1",col("ending_balance_ec").alias("ending_balance"),col("beginning_balance_ec").alias("beginning_balance"),"financial_statement_line","financial_statement_category",col("debit_amount_ec").alias("debit_amount"),col("credit_amount_ec").alias("credit_amount"),col("net_amount_ec").alias("net_amount"),"journal_number") 
 
     
    reconciliation = reconciliation.groupby( 
        "account_number_new", 
        "account_description_new", 
        "entity_id_new", 
        "financial_statement_subtotal_category", 
        "found_in", 
        "account_grouping_1", 
        "ending_balance", 
        "beginning_balance", 
        "financial_statement_line", 
        "financial_statement_category", 
    ).agg( 
        sum("debit_amount"), 
        sum("credit_amount"), 
        sum("net_amount"), 
        count("journal_number").alias("number_of_lines"), 
        count_distinct("journal_number").alias("number_of_entries") 
    ) 
 
    reconciliation = reconciliation.select(col("account_number_new"), 
        col("account_description_new"), 
        "entity_id_new", 
        "financial_statement_subtotal_category", 
        "found_in", 
        "account_grouping_1", 
        "financial_statement_line", 
        "financial_statement_category", 
        "number_of_lines", 
        "number_of_entries", 
        when(((col('beginning_balance').isNull()) | (col('beginning_balance') == '')) , lit(0)).otherwise(col('beginning_balance')).alias("beginning_balance"), 
        when(((col('ending_balance').isNull()) | (col('ending_balance') == '')) , lit(0)).otherwise(col('ending_balance')).alias("ending_balance"), 
        when(((col('sum(net_amount)').isNull()) | (col('sum(net_amount)') == '')) , lit(0)).otherwise(col('sum(net_amount)')).alias("sum(net_amount)"), 
        when(((col('sum(debit_amount)').isNull()) | (col('sum(debit_amount)') == '')) , lit(0)).otherwise(col('sum(debit_amount)')).alias("sum(debit_amount)"), 
        when(((col('sum(credit_amount)').isNull()) | (col('sum(credit_amount)') == '')) , lit(0)).otherwise(col('sum(credit_amount)')).alias("sum(credit_amount)"), 
        when( 
            col("financial_statement_category").isin( 
                ["Assets", "Equity", "Liabilities"] 
            ), 
            "BS", 
        ) 
        .otherwise("IS") 
        .alias("income_statement_balance_sheet"), 
         
        ) 
         
    reconciliation = reconciliation.select( 
        reconciliation["*"], 
        when( 
            ( 
                (lit(is_test_period_from_beginning) == "No") 
                | (col("income_statement_balance_sheet") == "BS") 
            ), 
            col("ending_balance") - col("beginning_balance"), 
        ) 
        .otherwise(col("ending_balance")) 
        .alias("trial_activity"), 
    ) 
    reconciliation = reconciliation.select( 
        reconciliation["*"], 
        when(col("found_in") == "JE", col("sum(net_amount)")) 
        .otherwise(col("sum(net_amount)") - col("trial_activity")) 
        .alias("variance"), 
    ) 
    reconciliation = reconciliation.select( 
        reconciliation["*"], abs(round(col('variance'), 2)).alias("abs_variance") 
    ) 
 
    reconciliation = reconciliation.select( 
        col("account_number_new").alias("account_number"), 
        col("account_description_new").alias("account_description"), 
        col("entity_id_new").alias("entity_id"), 
        "financial_statement_subtotal_category", 
        "found_in", 
        "account_grouping_1", 
        round(col('ending_balance'), 2).alias("ending_balance"), 
        round(col('beginning_balance'), 2).alias("beginning_balance"), 
        "financial_statement_line", 
        "financial_statement_category", 
        round(col('sum(debit_amount)'), 2).alias("debit_amount"), 
        round(col('sum(credit_amount)'), 2).alias("credit_amount"), 
        round(col('sum(net_amount)'), 2).alias("je_activity"), 
        round(col('trial_activity'), 2).alias("trial_activity"), 
        round(col('variance'), 2).alias("variance"), 
        "number_of_lines", 
        "number_of_entries", 
        "income_statement_balance_sheet", 
        "abs_variance", 
        when(((col("abs_variance") <= 1)) , "Reconciled") 
        .otherwise("Unreconciled") 
        .alias("reconciliation"),  
        lit(currency).alias("reconciliation_currency") 
    ) 
    unreconciled_account = reconciliation.filter( 
        col("reconciliation") == "Unreconciled" 
    ) 
     
 
    return ( 
        reconciliation, 
        unreconciled_account, 
        tb_coa_joined, 
        gl_tb_coa_joined 
    ) 
 
# COMMAND ---------- 
 
# DBTITLE 1,Unreconciled_Account_Detail 
def get_unrecon(unreconciled_account): 
    unreconciled_account_detail= unreconciled_account.select("account_number", 
        "account_description", 
        "entity_id", 
        "financial_statement_subtotal_category", 
        "found_in", 
        "account_grouping_1", 
        "ending_balance", 
        "beginning_balance", 
        "financial_statement_line", 
        "financial_statement_category", 
        "je_activity", 
        "number_of_lines", 
        "number_of_entries", 
        "income_statement_balance_sheet", 
        "trial_activity", 
        "variance", 
        "abs_variance", 
        "reconciliation" 
    )     
    general_ledger_temp = general_ledger_df.select( 
        "account_number", 
        "entity_id", 
        "fiscal_year", 
        "entity_name", 
        "journal_number", 
        "journal_header_description", 
        "dc_indicator", 
        "journal_line_number", 
        "journal_line_description", 
        "transaction_type", 
        "transaction_type_description", 
        "entity_currency_ec", 
        "date_posted", 
        "date_effective", 
        "fiscal_period", 
        "userid_entered", 
        "user_name_entered", 
        "time_posted", 
        "source", 
        "is_standard", 
        "net_amount_oc", 
        "credit_amount_oc", 
        "debit_amount_oc", 
        "net_amount_ec", 
        "credit_amount_ec", 
        "debit_amount_ec", 
        "net_amount_gc", 
        "credit_amount_gc", 
        "debit_amount_gc", 
        "group_currency_gc" 
    ) 
    unreconciled_account_detail_TB = unreconciled_account_detail.select(unreconciled_account_detail["*"],lit("").alias("fiscal_year"),lit("").alias("entity_name"),lit("").alias("journal_number"),lit("").alias("journal_header_description"),lit("").alias("dc_indicator"),lit("").alias("journal_line_number"),lit("").alias("journal_line_description"),lit("").alias("transaction_type"),lit("").alias("transaction_type_description"),lit("").alias("entity_currency_ec"),lit("").alias("date_posted"),lit("").alias("date_effective"),lit("").alias("fiscal_period"),lit("").alias("userid_entered"),lit("").alias("user_name_entered"),lit("").alias("time_posted"),lit("").alias("source"),lit("").alias("is_standard"),lit("").alias("net_amount_oc"),lit("").alias("credit_amount_oc"),lit("").alias("debit_amount_oc"),lit("").alias("net_amount_ec"),lit("").alias("credit_amount_ec"),lit("").alias("debit_amount_ec"),lit("").alias("net_amount_gc"),lit("").alias("credit_amount_gc"),lit("").alias("debit_amount_gc"),lit("").alias("group_currency_gc")).filter(col("found_in") == 'TB') 
    unreconciled_account_detail = general_ledger_temp.join( 
        broadcast(unreconciled_account_detail), 
        ((unreconciled_account_detail.entity_id 
        == general_ledger_temp.entity_id) & (unreconciled_account_detail.account_number 
        == general_ledger_temp.account_number)), 
        "inner", 
    ).drop(general_ledger_temp.account_number,general_ledger_temp.entity_id) 
 
    unreconciled_account_detail = unreconciled_account_detail.unionByName(unreconciled_account_detail_TB) 
    return (unreconciled_account_detail)

# DBTITLE 1,TB Output Function 
## This function is used to create trial_balance output 
def get_tb_output( trial_balance_full, reconciliation): 
    trial_balance = trial_balance_full.join( 
        reconciliation, 
        ((trial_balance_full.entity_id 
        == reconciliation.entity_id) & (trial_balance_full.account_number 
        == reconciliation.account_number)), 
        "inner", 
    ).select(trial_balance_full.chart_of_accounts,trial_balance_full.entity_id,trial_balance_full.entity_name,trial_balance_full.entity_currency_ec,trial_balance_full.fiscal_year,trial_balance_full.account_number,trial_balance_full.account_description,trial_balance_full.period_end_date,trial_balance_full.fiscal_period,trial_balance_full.period_type,trial_balance_full.beginning_balance_ec,trial_balance_full.period_activity_ec,trial_balance_full.ending_balance_ec,trial_balance_full.group_currency_gc,trial_balance_full.beginning_balance_gc,trial_balance_full.period_activity_gc,trial_balance_full.ending_balance_gc,trial_balance_full.preliminary_ec,trial_balance_full.adjusted_journal_entry_ec,trial_balance_full.adjusted_ec,trial_balance_full.reclassification_journal_entry_ec,trial_balance_full.preliminary_gc,trial_balance_full.adjusted_journal_entry_gc,trial_balance_full.adjusted_gc,trial_balance_full.reclassification_journal_entry_gc,trial_balance_full.consolidation_journal_entry_gc,trial_balance_full.extract_date,trial_balance_full.import_date,trial_balance_full.ledger_id,trial_balance_full.ledger_group,trial_balance_full.coa_account_key 
    ) 
    return(trial_balance)

# DBTITLE 1,JE Line Distribution Summary 
 
## Below function is used to populate the stratification summary report 
def get_stratification_summary(general_ledger_detail): 
    stratification_summary = general_ledger_detail.groupby("entity_id", 
        "is_standard", "journal_number" 
    ).agg(count("journal_number").alias("Number_of_Lines")) 
    stratification_summary = stratification_summary.select( 
        stratification_summary["*"], 
        when( 
            ((col("Number_of_Lines") == 1)), lit("Bucket 1: 1") 
        ) 
        .when( 
            ((col("Number_of_Lines") >= 2) & (col("Number_of_Lines") <= 20)), lit("Bucket 2: 2 - 20") 
        ) 
        .when( 
            ((col("Number_of_Lines") >= 21) & (col("Number_of_Lines") <= 100)), 
            lit("Bucket 3: 21 - 100"), 
        ) 
        .when( 
            ((col("Number_of_Lines") >= 101) & (col("Number_of_Lines") <= 1000)), 
            lit("Bucket 4: 101 - 1000"), 
        ) 
        .when( 
            (col("Number_of_Lines") > 1000), 
            lit("Bucket 5: > 1000"), 
        ) 
        .otherwise(lit("")) 
        .alias("Number_of_Lines_per_JE"), 
    ) 
    stratification_summary = stratification_summary.groupby("entity_id", 
        "is_standard", "Number_of_Lines_per_JE" 
    ).agg( 
        count("journal_number").alias("Number_of_JE"), 
        sum("Number_of_Lines").alias("Number_of_Lines"), 
    ) 
    total = stratification_summary.agg( 
        sum("Number_of_Lines").alias("Total_Lines"), sum("Number_of_JE").alias("Total_Entries") 
    ).first() 
    stratification_summary = stratification_summary.select( 
        stratification_summary["*"], 
        round(((col("Number_of_Lines") / total.Total_Lines) * 100),2).alias("Percentage_of_Lines"), 
        round(((col("Number_of_JE") / total.Total_Entries) * 100),2).alias("Percentage_of_Entries"), 
    ).orderBy(col("is_standard"), col("Number_of_Lines_per_JE")) 
    return stratification_summary 
 
# COMMAND ---------- 
 
# DBTITLE 1,Control Totals 
## This function is used to capture the control totals by period , standard_nonstandard, currency 
def get_control_total(general_ledger_df): 
    control_total = general_ledger_df.groupby( 
        "is_standard", 
        "fiscal_period", 
        "original_currency_oc", 
        "entity_currency_ec", 
        "group_currency_gc", 
        "entity_id", 
        "date_posted", 
        "date_effective", 
        "transaction_type", 
        "transaction_type_description", 
        "userid_entered", 
        "user_name_entered" 
    ).agg( 
        sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("sum_debit_amount_ec"), 
        sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("sum_credit_amount_ec"), 
        count("journal_number").alias("count_journal_number"), 
        sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("sum_debit_amount_oc"), 
        sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("sum_credit_amount_oc"), 
        sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("sum_debit_amount_gc"), 
        sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("sum_credit_amount_gc"), 
        sum("net_amount_ec").alias("sum_net_amount_ec"), 
        sum("net_amount_gc").alias("sum_net_amount_gc"), 
        sum("net_amount_oc").alias("sum_net_amount_oc") 
    ) 
    ## Control total by period 
    control_total_by_period = control_total.groupby("entity_id","fiscal_period").agg( 
        sum("sum_credit_amount_ec").alias("total_credit_amount_ec"), 
        sum("sum_credit_amount_oc").alias("total_credit_amount_oc"), 
        sum("sum_credit_amount_gc").alias("total_credit_amount_gc"), 
        sum("sum_debit_amount_ec").alias("total_debit_amount_ec"), 
        sum("sum_debit_amount_oc").alias("total_debit_amount_oc"), 
        sum("sum_debit_amount_gc").alias("total_debit_amount_gc"), 
        sum("sum_net_amount_ec").alias("total_net_amount_ec"), 
        sum("sum_net_amount_gc").alias("total_net_amount_gc"), 
        sum("sum_net_amount_oc").alias("total_net_amount_oc"), 
        sum("count_journal_number").alias("line_count"), 
        max("date_posted").alias("maxdate_date_posted"), 
        min("date_posted").alias("mindate_date_posted"), 
        max("date_effective").alias("maxdate_date_effective"), 
        min("date_effective").alias("mindate_date_effective"), 
    ) 
    ## Control total by standard_nonstandard 
    control_total_by_standard_nonstandard = control_total.groupby("entity_id","is_standard").agg( 
        sum("sum_credit_amount_ec").alias("total_credit_amount_ec"), 
        sum("sum_credit_amount_oc").alias("total_credit_amount_oc"), 
        sum("sum_credit_amount_gc").alias("total_credit_amount_gc"), 
        sum("sum_debit_amount_ec").alias("total_debit_amount_ec"), 
        sum("sum_debit_amount_oc").alias("total_debit_amount_oc"), 
        sum("sum_debit_amount_gc").alias("total_debit_amount_gc"), 
        sum("sum_net_amount_ec").alias("total_net_amount_ec"), 
        sum("sum_net_amount_gc").alias("total_net_amount_gc"), 
        sum("sum_net_amount_oc").alias("total_net_amount_oc"), 
        sum("count_journal_number").alias("line_count"), 
        max("date_posted").alias("maxdate_date_posted"), 
        min("date_posted").alias("mindate_date_posted"), 
        max("date_effective").alias("maxdate_date_effective"), 
        min("date_effective").alias("mindate_date_effective"), 
    ) 
    ## Control total by currency 
    control_total_by_currency = control_total.groupby("entity_id", 
        "original_currency_oc", "entity_currency_ec", "group_currency_gc" 
    ).agg( 
        sum("sum_credit_amount_ec").alias("total_credit_amount_ec"), 
        sum("sum_credit_amount_oc").alias("total_credit_amount_oc"), 
        sum("sum_credit_amount_gc").alias("total_credit_amount_gc"), 
        sum("sum_debit_amount_ec").alias("total_debit_amount_ec"), 
        sum("sum_debit_amount_oc").alias("total_debit_amount_oc"), 
        sum("sum_debit_amount_gc").alias("total_debit_amount_gc"), 
        sum("sum_net_amount_ec").alias("total_net_amount_ec"), 
        sum("sum_net_amount_gc").alias("total_net_amount_gc"), 
        sum("sum_net_amount_oc").alias("total_net_amount_oc"), 
        sum("count_journal_number").alias("line_count"), 
        max("date_posted").alias("maxdate_date_posted"), 
        min("date_posted").alias("mindate_date_posted"), 
        max("date_effective").alias("maxdate_date_effective"), 
        min("date_effective").alias("mindate_date_effective"), 
    ) 
    ## Control total by transaction type 
    control_total_by_transaction_type = control_total.groupby("entity_id","transaction_type","transaction_type_description").agg( 
        sum("sum_credit_amount_ec").alias("total_credit_amount_ec"), 
        sum("sum_credit_amount_oc").alias("total_credit_amount_oc"), 
        sum("sum_credit_amount_gc").alias("total_credit_amount_gc"), 
        sum("sum_debit_amount_ec").alias("total_debit_amount_ec"), 
        sum("sum_debit_amount_oc").alias("total_debit_amount_oc"), 
        sum("sum_debit_amount_gc").alias("total_debit_amount_gc"), 
        sum("sum_net_amount_ec").alias("total_net_amount_ec"), 
        sum("sum_net_amount_gc").alias("total_net_amount_gc"), 
        sum("sum_net_amount_oc").alias("total_net_amount_oc"), 
        sum("count_journal_number").alias("line_count"), 
        max("date_posted").alias("maxdate_date_posted"), 
        min("date_posted").alias("mindate_date_posted"), 
        max("date_effective").alias("maxdate_date_effective"), 
        min("date_effective").alias("mindate_date_effective"), 
    ) 
    ## Control total by user 
    control_total_by_user = control_total.groupby("entity_id","userid_entered","user_name_entered").agg( 
        sum("sum_credit_amount_ec").alias("total_credit_amount_ec"), 
        sum("sum_credit_amount_oc").alias("total_credit_amount_oc"), 
        sum("sum_credit_amount_gc").alias("total_credit_amount_gc"), 
        sum("sum_debit_amount_ec").alias("total_debit_amount_ec"), 
        sum("sum_debit_amount_oc").alias("total_debit_amount_oc"), 
        sum("sum_debit_amount_gc").alias("total_debit_amount_gc"), 
        sum("sum_net_amount_ec").alias("total_net_amount_ec"), 
        sum("sum_net_amount_gc").alias("total_net_amount_gc"), 
        sum("sum_net_amount_oc").alias("total_net_amount_oc"), 
        sum("count_journal_number").alias("line_count"), 
        max("date_posted").alias("maxdate_date_posted"), 
        min("date_posted").alias("mindate_date_posted"), 
        max("date_effective").alias("maxdate_date_effective"), 
        min("date_effective").alias("mindate_date_effective"), 
    ) 
    return ( 
        control_total_by_period, 
        control_total_by_standard_nonstandard, 
        control_total_by_currency, 
        control_total_by_transaction_type, 
        control_total_by_user 
    ) 
 
# COMMAND ---------- 
 
# DBTITLE 1,Function to catch error character in GL 
def find_error_character_gl(general_ledger_df): 
    """ 
    Filter general ledger dataframe to find records containing diamond character (�) 
    and return the filtered dataframe along with the count of records. 
 
    Parameters: 
    general_ledger_df (DataFrame): General ledger dataframe 
 
    Returns: 
    tuple: (filtered dataframe, count of records) 
    """ 
    columns_to_check = [ 
        "chart_of_accounts", "entity_id", "entity_name", "journal_number", 
        "journal_header_description", "journal_line_number", "journal_line_description", 
        "transaction_type", "transaction_type_description", "entity_currency_ec", 
        "userid_entered", "user_name_entered", "updated_by", "name_of_user_updated", 
        "userid_approved", "user_name_approved", "is_standard", "is_manual", 
        "source", "source_description", "account_number", "account_description", 
        "coa_account_key", "forex_conversion_method", "original_currency_oc", 
        "group_currency_gc", "business_area", "controlling_area_for_cost_and_profit_center", 
        "cost_center", "profit_center", "project", "location", "custom_exclusion", 
        "ledger_id", "ledger_group" 
    ] 
 
    condition = F.reduce(lambda x, y: x | y, [col(c).contains("�") for c in columns_to_check]) 
 
    general_ledger_error_char = general_ledger_df.filter(condition) 
    number_of_records_gl_error_char = general_ledger_error_char.count() 
 
    return general_ledger_error_char, number_of_records_gl_error_char 
 
# COMMAND ---------- 
 
# DBTITLE 1,Function to catch error character in TB 
def find_error_character_tb(trial_balance_df): 
    """ 
    Filter the trial balance dataframe to find rows containing the diamond character (�) 
    and return the filtered dataframe along with the count of records. 
 
    Parameters: 
    trial_balance_df (DataFrame): The input dataframe to be filtered. 
 
    Returns: 
    tuple: A tuple containing the filtered dataframe and the count of records. 
    """ 
    diamond_char = "�" 
    columns_to_check = [ 
        "chart_of_accounts", 
        "entity_currency_ec", 
        "period_type", 
        "group_currency_gc", 
        "ledger_id", 
        "ledger_group", 
        "coa_account_key", 
        "entity_id", 
        "entity_name", 
        "account_number", 
        "account_description" 
    ] 
 
    trial_balance_error_char = trial_balance_df.filter( 
        F.reduce(lambda x, y: x | y, [col(c).contains(diamond_char) for c in columns_to_check]) 
    ) 
 
    number_of_records_tb_error_char = trial_balance_error_char.count() 
 
    return trial_balance_error_char, number_of_records_tb_error_char 
 
# COMMAND ---------- 
 
# DBTITLE 1,Function to catch error character in COA 
def find_error_character_coa(chart_of_accounts_df): 
    """ 
    Filter the chart of accounts dataframe to find rows containing the diamond character (�) 
    and return the filtered dataframe along with the count of records. 
 
    Parameters: 
    chart_of_accounts_df (DataFrame): The input dataframe to be filtered. 
 
    Returns: 
    tuple: A tuple containing the filtered dataframe and the count of records. 
    """ 
    diamond_char = "�" 
    columns_to_check = [ 
        "chart_of_accounts", 
        "entity_id", 
        "entity_name", 
        "account_description", 
        "financial_statement_line_num", 
        "financial_statement_subtotal_category", 
        "financial_statement_category", 
        "financial_statement_type", 
        "abcotd", 
        "abcotd_significance", 
        "content_area", 
        "tags", 
        "coa_account_key", 
        "account_number", 
        "account_grouping_1", 
        "financial_statement_line" 
    ] 
 
    chart_of_accounts_error_char = chart_of_accounts_df.filter( 
        F.reduce(lambda x, y: x | y, [col(c).contains(diamond_char) for c in columns_to_check]) 
    ) 
 
    number_of_records_coa_error_char = chart_of_accounts_error_char.count() 
 
    return chart_of_accounts_error_char, number_of_records_coa_error_char

# DBTITLE 1,Transformation Summary
def get_transformation_summary(coa_ag1_fsline_corrected,coa_ag1_fscategory_corrected,coa_fsline_fscategory_corrected,missing_financial_statement_category,missing_financial_statement_line,missing_account_grouping_1,missing_revision_date,missing_financial_statement_subtotal_category,number_of_records_coa,tb_incorrect_period_type,tb_missing_entity_name,coa_missing_entity_name,missing_time_posted,missing_fiscal_period,number_of_records_gl,gl_missing_entity_name,gl_muliple_is_standard,autocorrect_multiple_is_standard,number_of_records_gl_error_char,number_of_records_tb_error_char,number_of_records_coa_error_char):
    coa_2_temp = coa_ag1_fsline_corrected.filter(col("count_fsline_account_grouping") > 1)
    coa_3_temp = coa_fsline_fscategory_corrected.filter(col("count_fs_category_fs_line") > 1)
    coa_4_temp = coa_ag1_fscategory_corrected.filter(col("count_fscategory_account_grouping") > 1)
    gl_muliple_is_standard = gl_muliple_is_standard.filter(col("count_distinct_is_standard_2") > 1)
    coa_missing_entity_name_count = coa_missing_entity_name.count()
    tb_missing_entity_name_count = tb_missing_entity_name.count()
    gl_missing_entity_name_count = gl_missing_entity_name.count()
    tb_incorrect_period_type_count = tb_incorrect_period_type.count()

    autocorrect_summary = StructType([
    StructField("Index", IntegerType(), True),
    StructField("Source File", StringType(), True),
    StructField("Field", StringType(), True),
    StructField("Rule Description", StringType(), True),
    StructField("Impact Area", StringType(), True),
    StructField("Number of Affected Records", StringType(), True),
    StructField("Original Value", ArrayType(StringType()), False),
    StructField("Corrected Value", ArrayType(StringType()), False),
    ])
    empty_df = spark.createDataFrame([], autocorrect_summary)
    data = [(1,"chart_of_accounts","financial_statement_category", "When the entire Population is empty, values are assigned based on the user-defined logic.","Population","",["n/a"],["n/a"]), (2,"chart_of_accounts","account_grouping_1", "When the entire Population is empty, it is replaced with Financial Statement Line.","Population","",["n/a"],["n/a"]),(3,"chart_of_accounts","account_grouping_1", "When account mapping relationship is incorrect, it is auto-resolved by concatenation with Financial Statement Line.","Record","",[""],[""]),(4,"chart_of_accounts","account_grouping_1", "When account mapping relationship is incorrect, it is auto-resolved by concatenation with Financial Statement Category.","Record","",[""],[""]),(5,"chart_of_accounts","financial_statement_line", "When account mapping relationship is incorrect, it is auto-resolved by concatenation with Financial Statement Category.","Record","",[""],[""]),(6,"chart_of_accounts","financial_statement_line", "When the entire Population is empty, it is replaced with Financial Statement Category.","Population","",["n/a"],["n/a"]),(7,"chart_of_accounts","revision_date", "When the entire Population is empty, it is filled with today's date.","Population","",["n/a"],["n/a"]),(8,"chart_of_accounts","financial_statement_subtotal_category", "When the entire Population is empty, values are assigned based on Financial Statement Category.","Population","",["n/a"],["n/a"]),(9,"chart_of_accounts","entity_name", "When the Entity Name Population is empty, it is replaced with Entity ID Population.","Record","",["n/a"],["n/a"]),(10,"trial_balance","period_type", "When the Period Type Population is empty or has an unaccepted value, replacing it with YTD.","Record","",["n/a"],["n/a"]),(11,"trial_balance","entity_name", "When the Entity Name Population is empty, it is replaced with Entity ID Population.","Record","",["n/a"],["n/a"]),(12,"journal_entry","time_posted", "When the entire Population is empty, it is replaced with Date Posted.","Population","",["n/a"],["n/a"]),(13,"journal_entry","fiscal_period","When the entire Population is empty, it is derived based on Fiscal Year End input parameter or Fiscal Calendar data table.","Population","",["n/a"],["n/a"]),(14,"journal_entry","entity_name","When the Entity Name Population is empty, it is replaced with Entity ID Population.","Record","",["n/a"],["n/a"]),(15,"journal_entry","is_standard","If more than one Is_Standard value is mapped to Journal Number, it is replaced with the value defined in the user parameter","Record","",["n/a"],["n/a"]),(16,"journal_entry","all string fields","Check for parsing failure or error character","Record","",["n/a"],["n/a"]),(17,"trial_balance","all string fields","Check for parsing failure or error character","Record","",["n/a"],["n/a"]),(18,"chart_of_accounts","all string fields","Check for parsing failure or error character","Record","",["n/a"],["n/a"])]
    populated_df = spark.createDataFrame(data, autocorrect_summary)
    if missing_financial_statement_category is None:
        populated_df=populated_df.select(col("Index"),col("Source File"),col("Field"),col("Rule Description"),col("Impact Area"),when(col("Index") == 1, number_of_records_coa).otherwise(lit(col("Number of Affected Records"))).alias("Number of Affected Records"),col("Original Value"),col("Corrected Value")
    )
    if missing_account_grouping_1 is None:
        populated_df=populated_df.select(col("Index"),col("Source File"),col("Field"),col("Rule Description"),col("Impact Area"),when(col("Index") == 2, number_of_records_coa).otherwise(lit(col("Number of Affected Records"))).alias("Number of Affected Records"),col("Original Value"),col("Corrected Value")
    )
    if missing_financial_statement_line is None:
        populated_df=populated_df.select(col("Index"),col("Source File"),col("Field"),col("Rule Description"),col("Impact Area"),when(col("Index") == 6, number_of_records_coa).otherwise(lit(col("Number of Affected Records"))).alias("Number of Affected Records"),col("Original Value"),col("Corrected Value")
    )
    if missing_revision_date is None:
        populated_df=populated_df.select(col("Index"),col("Source File"),col("Field"),col("Rule Description"),col("Impact Area"),when(col("Index") == 7, number_of_records_coa).otherwise(lit(col("Number of Affected Records"))).alias("Number of Affected Records"),col("Original Value"),col("Corrected Value")
    )

    if missing_financial_statement_subtotal_category is None:
        populated_df=populated_df.select(col("Index"),col("Source File"),col("Field"),col("Rule Description"),col("Impact Area"),when(col("Index") == 8, number_of_records_coa).otherwise(lit(col("Number of Affected Records"))).alias("Number of Affected Records"),col("Original Value"),col("Corrected Value")
    )

    if coa_2_temp.count()>0:
        coa_2=coa_2_temp.groupBy(lit(1).alias("Index")).agg(sum("Number_of_ag1_fsline_corrected").alias("Number_of_ag1_fsline_corrected"),collect_set("account_grouping_1").alias("original_value"),collect_set("ag1_multiple_fsline_with_correction").alias("corrected_value")).first()
        populated_df = populated_df.select(col("Index"),col("Source File"),col("Field"),col("Rule Description"),col("Impact Area"),
        when(col("Index") == 3, coa_2.Number_of_ag1_fsline_corrected).otherwise(lit(col("Number of Affected Records"))).alias("Number of Affected Records"),
        when(col("Index") == 3, lit(coa_2.original_value)).otherwise(lit(col("Original Value"))).alias("Original Value"),
        when(col("Index") == 3, lit(coa_2.corrected_value)).otherwise(lit(col("Corrected Value"))).alias("Corrected Value")
    )

    if coa_3_temp.count()>0:
        coa_3=coa_3_temp.groupBy(lit(1).alias("Index")).agg(sum("Number_of_fsline_corrected").alias("Number_of_fsline_corrected"),collect_set("financial_statement_line").alias("original_value"),collect_set("fsline_multiple_fscategory_with_correction").alias("corrected_value")).first()
        populated_df = populated_df.select(col("Index"),col("Source File"),col("Field"),col("Rule Description"),col("Impact Area"),
        when(col("Index") == 5, coa_3.Number_of_fsline_corrected).otherwise(lit(col("Number of Affected Records"))).alias("Number of Affected Records"),
        when(col("Index") == 5, lit(coa_3.original_value)).otherwise(lit(col("Original Value"))).alias("Original Value"),
        when(col("Index") == 5, lit(coa_3.corrected_value)).otherwise(lit(col("Corrected Value"))).alias("Corrected Value")
    )
    if coa_4_temp.count()>0:
        coa_4=coa_4_temp.groupBy(lit(1).alias("Index")).agg(sum("Number_of_account_grouping_1_corrected").alias("Number_of_account_grouping_1_corrected"),collect_set("account_grouping_1").alias("original_value"),collect_set("ag1_multiple_fscategory_with_correction").alias("corrected_value")).first()
        populated_df = populated_df.select(col("Index"),col("Source File"),col("Field"),col("Rule Description"),col("Impact Area"),
        when(col("Index") == 4, coa_4.Number_of_account_grouping_1_corrected).otherwise(lit(col("Number of Affected Records"))).alias("Number of Affected Records"),
        when(col("Index") == 4, lit(coa_4.original_value)).otherwise(lit(col("Original Value"))).alias("Original Value"),
        when(col("Index") == 4, lit(coa_4.corrected_value)).otherwise(lit(col("Corrected Value"))).alias("Corrected Value")
    )
    if coa_missing_entity_name_count >0:
        populated_df = populated_df.select(col("Index"),col("Source File"),col("Field"),col("Rule Description"),col("Impact Area"),
        when(col("Index") == 9, coa_missing_entity_name_count).otherwise(lit(col("Number of Affected Records"))).alias("Number of Affected Records"),col("Original Value"),col("Corrected Value")
    )
    if tb_incorrect_period_type_count >0:
        populated_df = populated_df.select(col("Index"),col("Source File"),col("Field"),col("Rule Description"),col("Impact Area"),when(col("Index") == 10, tb_incorrect_period_type_count).otherwise(lit(col("Number of Affected Records"))).alias("Number of Affected Records"),col("Original Value"),col("Corrected Value")
    )
    if tb_missing_entity_name_count>0:
        populated_df = populated_df.select(col("Index"),col("Source File"),col("Field"),col("Rule Description"),col("Impact Area"),
        when(col("Index") == 11,  tb_missing_entity_name_count).otherwise(lit(col("Number of Affected Records"))).alias("Number of Affected Records"),col("Original Value"),col("Corrected Value")
    )
    if missing_time_posted is None:
        populated_df = populated_df.select(col("Index"),col("Source File"),col("Field"),col("Rule Description"),col("Impact Area"),when(col("Index") == 12, number_of_records_gl).otherwise(lit(col("Number of Affected Records"))).alias("Number of Affected Records"),col("Original Value"),col("Corrected Value")
    )
    if missing_fiscal_period is None:
        populated_df = populated_df.select(col("Index"),col("Source File"),col("Field"),col("Rule Description"),col("Impact Area"),when(col("Index") == 13, number_of_records_gl).otherwise(lit(col("Number of Affected Records"))).alias("Number of Affected Records"),col("Original Value"),col("Corrected Value")
    )
    if gl_missing_entity_name_count>0:
        populated_df = populated_df.select(col("Index"),col("Source File"),col("Field"),col("Rule Description"),col("Impact Area"),
        when(col("Index") == 14,  gl_missing_entity_name_count).otherwise(lit(col("Number of Affected Records"))).alias("Number of Affected Records"),col("Original Value"),col("Corrected Value")
    )
    if ((gl_muliple_is_standard.count() > 0) & (autocorrect_multiple_is_standard == 'Yes')) :
        gl_muliple_is_standard=gl_muliple_is_standard.groupBy(lit(1).alias("Index")).agg(sum("count_is_standard").alias("Number_of_is_standard_corrected")).first()
        populated_df = populated_df.select(col("Index"),col("Source File"),col("Field"),col("Rule Description"),col("Impact Area"),
        when(col("Index") == 15, gl_muliple_is_standard.Number_of_is_standard_corrected).otherwise(lit(col("Number of Affected Records"))).alias("Number of Affected Records"),col("Original Value"),col("Corrected Value")
        )
    if (number_of_records_gl_error_char>0):
        populated_df = populated_df.select(col("Index"),col("Source File"),col("Field"),col("Rule Description"),col("Impact Area"),
        when(col("Index") == 16,  number_of_records_gl_error_char).otherwise(lit(col("Number of Affected Records"))).alias("Number of Affected Records"),col("Original Value"),col("Corrected Value")
    )
    if (number_of_records_tb_error_char>0):
        populated_df = populated_df.select(col("Index"),col("Source File"),col("Field"),col("Rule Description"),col("Impact Area"),
        when(col("Index") == 17,  number_of_records_tb_error_char).otherwise(lit(col("Number of Affected Records"))).alias("Number of Affected Records"),col("Original Value"),col("Corrected Value")
    )
    if (number_of_records_coa_error_char>0):
        populated_df = populated_df.select(col("Index"),col("Source File"),col("Field"),col("Rule Description"),col("Impact Area"),
        when(col("Index") == 18,  number_of_records_coa_error_char).otherwise(lit(col("Number of Affected Records"))).alias("Number of Affected Records"),col("Original Value"),col("Corrected Value")
    )
        

    populated_df=populated_df.select(col("Index"),col("Source File"),col("Field"),col("Rule Description"),col("Impact Area"),col("Number of Affected Records"),concat_ws(",", col("Original Value")).alias("Original Value"),concat_ws(",", col("Corrected Value")).alias("Corrected Value"))
    return populated_df

        obj_gcw_details.exclusion_list_include_entityid)
