# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC # 8. Data Integrity Check

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_01a_Error_COA_Blank_Values

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies blank values in critical fields within the chart of accounts (COA): Account_Number, Account_Description, Financial_Statement_Line, Financial_Statement_Line_Num, Account_Grouping_1, Account_Grouping_1_Num, Financial_Statement_Category (Assets, Liabilities, Equity, Revenue, Expenses), Chart_of_Accounts and Entity_ID
def perform_dqc_coa_blank_values(chart_of_accounts_df):
    data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values = chart_of_accounts_df.select(
        chart_of_accounts_df["*"],
        when(
            (
                (col("financial_statement_line").isNull())|(col("financial_statement_line") == "")
            ),
            "Financial_Statement_Line missing in COA File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("errormessage1"),
        when(
            (
                (col("financial_statement_category").isNull())|(col("financial_statement_category") == "")
            ),
            "Financial_Statement_Category missing in COA File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("errormessage2"),
        when(
            (col("entity_id").isNull())|(col("entity_id") == ""), 
            "EntityID missing in COA File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("errormessage3"),
        when(
            (
                (col("account_description").isNull())|(col("account_description") == "")
            ),
            "Account_Description missing in COA File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("errormessage4"),
        when(
            (col("account_grouping_1").isNull())|(col("account_grouping_1") == ""),
            "Account_Grouping_1 missing in COA File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("errormessage5"),
        when(
            (col("account_grouping_1_num").isNull())|(col("account_grouping_1_num") == ""),
            "Account_Grouping_1_Num missing in COA File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("errormessage6"),
        when(
            (col("financial_statement_line_num").isNull())|(col("financial_statement_line_num") == ""),
            "Financial_Statement_Line_Num missing in COA File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("errormessage7"),
        when(
            (
                (col("account_number").isNull())|(col("account_number") == "")
            ),
            "Account_Number missing in COA File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("errormessage8"),
        when(
            (col("chart_of_accounts").isNull())|(col("chart_of_accounts") == ""),
            "Chart_of_Accounts missing in COA File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("errormessage9")
    )
    data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values = data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values.select(
        data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values["*"],
        concat_ws(
            ",",
            col("errormessage1"),
            col("errormessage2"),
            col("errormessage3"),
            col("errormessage4"),
            col("errormessage5"),
            col("errormessage6"),
            col("errormessage7"),
            col("errormessage8"),
            col("errormessage9")
        ).alias("error_detail"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values = data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values.withColumn(
        "error_detail", regexp_replace(col("error_detail"), "(X_,)|(,X_)|(X_)", "")
    )
    data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values = data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values.filter(
        (col("error_detail") != "")
    )
    data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values_1 = data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values.select(col("chart_of_accounts"),
    col("entity_id"),
    col("entity_name"),
    col("revision_date"),
    col("account_description"),
    col("account_grouping_1_num"),
    col("financial_statement_line_num"),
    col("financial_statement_subtotal_category_id"),
    col("financial_statement_subtotal_category"),
    col("financial_statement_category_id"),
    col("financial_statement_category"),
    col("financial_statement_type"),
    col("abcotd_id"),
    col("abcotd"),
    col("abcotd_significance"),
    col("content_area_id"),
    col("content_area"),
    col("extract_date"),
    col("import_date"),
    col("tags"),
    col("coa_account_key"),
    col("account_number"),
    col("account_grouping_1"),
    col("financial_statement_line"),
    split(col("error_detail"), ",").alias("error_detail") 
    )
    data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values = data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values_1.withColumn(
        "error_detail", explode(col("error_detail"))
    )
    data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values_1.groupby("entity_id").agg(count(col("*")).alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"))
    )
    data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values_summary = data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values_summary["*"],
        lit("").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
        lit(
            "Financial_Statement_Line, Financial_Statement_Line_Num, Financial_Statement_Category, Account_Grouping_1, Account_Grouping_1_Num, Account_Number, Account_Description, Entity_ID, Chart_of_Accounts"
        ).alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit("All").alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Error").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies blank values in critical fields within the chart of accounts (COA): Account_Number, Account_Description, Financial_Statement_Line, Financial_Statement_Line_Num, Account_Grouping_1, Account_Grouping_1_Num, Financial_Statement_Category (Assets, Liabilities, Equity, Revenue, Expenses), Chart_of_Accounts and Entity_ID. You will not be able to process the data in the platform if there are missing values in the critical fields."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_01a_Error_COA_Blank_Values").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )

    data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )
    return (data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values, data_integrity_check_01a_error_coa_blank_values_summary)

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_01b_Error_TB_Blank_Values

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies blank values in critical fields within the trial balance (TB): Account_Number, Account_Description, Entity_ID, Period_End_Date, Ending_Balance_GC, Ending_Balance_EC, Group_Currency_GC, Entity_Currency_EC, Chart_of_Accounts
def perform_dqc_trial_balance_blank_values(trial_balance_df):
    data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values = trial_balance_df.select(
        trial_balance_df["*"],
        when(
            col("period_end_date").isNull(),
            "Period_End_Date missing in Trial Balance File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("errormessage1"),
        when(
            (col("entity_id").isNull())|(col("entity_id") == ""),
            "EntityID missing in Trial Balance File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("errormessage2"),
        when(
            col("ending_balance_gc").isNull(),
            "Ending_Balance_GC missing in Trial Balance File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("errormessage3"),
        when(
            (
                (col("account_number").isNull())|(col("account_number") == "")
            ),
            "Account_Number missing in Trial Balance File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("errormessage4"),
        when(
            col("ending_balance_ec").isNull(),
            "Ending_Balance_EC missing in Trial Balance File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("errormessage5"),
        when(
            (col("group_currency_gc").isNull())|(col("group_currency_gc") == ""),
            "Group_Currency_GC missing in Trial Balance File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("errormessage6"),
        when(
            (col("entity_currency_ec").isNull())|(col("entity_currency_ec") == ""),
            "Entity_Currency_EC missing in Trial Balance File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("errormessage7"),
        when(
            (col("chart_of_accounts").isNull())|(col("chart_of_accounts") == ""),
            "Chart_of_Accounts missing in Trial Balance File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("errormessage8"),
        when(
            (col("account_description").isNull())|(col("account_description") == ""),
            "Account_Description missing in Trial Balance File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("errormessage9")
        
    )
    
    data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values = data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values.select(
        data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values["*"],
        concat_ws(
            ",",
            col("errormessage1"),
            col("errormessage2"),
            col("errormessage3"),
            col("errormessage4"),
            col("errormessage5"),
            col("errormessage6"),
            col("errormessage7"),
            col("errormessage8"),
            col("errormessage9")
        ).alias("error_detail"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values = data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values.withColumn(
        "error_detail", regexp_replace(col("error_detail"), "(X_,)|(,X_)|(X_)", "")
    )
    data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values = data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values.filter(
        (col("error_detail") != "")
    )
    data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values = data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values.withColumn(
        "error_detail", split(col("error_detail"), ",")
    )
    data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values_1 = data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values.select(
        col("chart_of_accounts"),col("entity_currency_ec"),col('period_end_date'),col("fiscal_year"),col("fiscal_period"),col("period_type"),col("beginning_balance_ec"),col("period_activity_ec"),col("ending_balance_ec"),col("group_currency_gc"),col("beginning_balance_gc"),col("period_activity_gc"),col("ending_balance_gc"),col("preliminary_ec"),col("adjusted_journal_entry_ec"),col("adjusted_ec"),col("reclassification_journal_entry_ec"),col("preliminary_gc"),col("adjusted_journal_entry_gc"),col("adjusted_gc"),col("reclassification_journal_entry_gc"),col("consolidation_journal_entry_gc"),col("extract_date"),col("import_date"),col("ledger_id"),col("ledger_group"),col("coa_account_key"),col("entity_id"),
        col("entity_name"),col("account_number"),col("account_description"),col("error_detail")
    )
    data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values = data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values_1.withColumn(
        "error_detail", explode(col("error_detail"))
    )
    data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values_1.groupby("entity_id").agg(count(col("*")).alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"))
    )
    data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values_summary = data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values_summary["*"],
        lit("").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
        lit("Account_Number, Account_Description, Entity_ID, Ending_balance_GC/EC, Period_End_Date, Group_Currency_GC, Entity_Currency_EC, Chart_of_Accounts").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit("All").alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Error").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies blank values in critical fields within the trial balance (TB): Account_Number, Account_Description, Entity_ID, Period_End_Date, Ending_Balance_GC, Ending_Balance_EC, Group_Currency_GC, Entity_Currency_EC, Chart_of_Accounts. You will not be able to process the data in the platform if there are missing values in the critical fields."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_01b_Error_TB_Blank_Values").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )

    data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )
    return (data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values, data_integrity_check_01b_error_tb_blank_values_summary)

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_01c_Error_JE_Blank_Values

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies blank values in critical fields within the journal entry (JE) data: Account_Number, Journal Entry ID (Journal_Number), Transaction_Type, Date_Posted, Date_Effective, Entity_ID, UserID_Entered, and NetAmounts (Net_Amount_EC, Net_Amount_GC, Net_Amount_OC)
def perform_dqc_journal_entry_blank_values(general_ledger_df):
    data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values = general_ledger_df.select(
        general_ledger_df["*"],
        when(
            (col("journal_number").isNull())|(col("journal_number") == ""),
            "Journal_Number missing in Journal Entry File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage1"),
        when(
            col("date_effective").isNull(),
            "Date_Effective missing in Journal Entry File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage2"),
        when(
            (col("entity_id").isNull())|(col("entity_id") == ""),
            "Entity_ID missing in Journal Entry  File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage3"),
        when(
            col("date_posted").isNull(),
            "Date_Posted missing in Journal Entry File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage4"),
        when(
            (
                (col("account_number").isNull())|(col("account_number") == "")
            ),
            "Account Number missing in Journal Entry  File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage5"),
        when(
            col("net_amount_ec").isNull(),
            "Net_Amount_EC missing in Journal Entry File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage6"),
        when(
            col("net_amount_gc").isNull(),
            "Net_Amount_GC missing in Journal Entry File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage7"),
        when(
            col("net_amount_oc").isNull(),
            "Net_Amount_OC missing in Journal Entry File",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage8"),
        
    )
    
    data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values = data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values.select(
        data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values["*"],
        concat_ws(
            ",",
            col("ErrorMessage1"),
            col("ErrorMessage2"),
            col("ErrorMessage3"),
            col("ErrorMessage4"),
            col("ErrorMessage5"),
            col("ErrorMessage6"),
            col("ErrorMessage7"),
            col("ErrorMessage8")
        ).alias("error_detail"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values = data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values.withColumn(
        "error_detail", regexp_replace(col("error_detail"), "(X_,)|(,X_)|(X_)", "")
    )
    data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values = data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values.filter(
        (col("error_detail") != "")
    )
    data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values_1 = data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values.select(col("chart_of_accounts"),
    col("entity_id"),
    col("entity_name"),
    col("journal_number"),
    col("journal_header_description"),
    col("dc_indicator"),
    col("journal_line_number"),
    col("journal_line_description"),
    col("fiscal_year"),
    col("fiscal_period"),
    col("transaction_type"),
    col("transaction_type_description"),
    col("entity_currency_ec"),
    col("date_posted"),
    col("date_effective"),
    col("userid_entered"),
    col("user_name_entered"),
    col("time_posted"),
    col("date_updated"),
    col("updated_by"),
    col("name_of_user_updated"),
    col("time_updated"),
    col("userid_approved"),
    col("user_name_approved"),
    col("date_approved"),
    col("time_approved"),
    col("is_standard"),
    col("is_manual"),
    col("source"),
    col("source_description"),
    col("account_number"),
    col("account_description"),
    col("coa_account_key"),
    col("exchange_rate"),
    col("foreign_exchange_date"),
    col("forex_conversion_method"),
    col("original_currency_oc"),
    col("group_currency_gc"),
    col("business_area"),
    col("controlling_area_for_cost_and_profit_center"),
    col("cost_center"),
    col("profit_center"),
    col("project"),
    col("location"),
    col("custom_exclusion"),
    col("extract_date"),
    col("import_date"),
    col("ledger_id"),
    col("ledger_group"),
    col("net_amount_oc"),
    col("net_amount_gc"),
    col("net_amount_ec"),
    col("debit_amount_oc"),
    col("debit_amount_gc"),
    col("debit_amount_ec"),
    col("credit_amount_oc"),
    col("credit_amount_gc"),
    col("credit_amount_ec"),
    split(col("error_detail"), ",").alias("error_detail")
    )
    data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values = data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values_1.withColumn(
        "error_detail", explode(col("error_detail"))
     )
    data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values_summary = data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values_1.groupby(
        "entity_id", "journal_number"
    ).agg(
        count("*").alias("number_of_affected_lines"),
        sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
        sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
        sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
        sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
        sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
        sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values_summary.groupby("entity_id").agg(
            count("*").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
            sum("number_of_affected_lines").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values_summary = data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values_summary["*"],
        lit(
            "Account_Number, Date_Effective, Date_Posted, Journal_Number, Entity_ID, Net_Amount_EC, Net_Amount_GC, Net_Amount_OC"
        ).alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit("All").alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Error").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies blank values in critical fields within the journal entry (JE) data: Account_Number, Journal Entry ID (Journal_Number), Date_Posted, Date_Effective, Entity_ID, and NetAmounts (Net_Amount_EC, Net_Amount_GC, Net_Amount_OC). You will not be able to process the data in the platform if there are missing values in the critical fields."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_01c_Error_JE_Blank_Values").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )

    data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values, data_integrity_check_01c_error_je_blank_values_summary)

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_01d_Warning_JE_Blank_UserID

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies blank values in the UserID_Entered field within the journal entry (JE) data'


def perform_dqc_journal_entry_blank_userid(general_ledger_df):
    data_integrity_check_01d_warning_je_blank_userid = general_ledger_df.filter(
        (col("userid_entered").isNull())|(col("userid_entered") == "")
    )

    data_integrity_check_01d_warning_je_blank_userid_summary = data_integrity_check_01d_warning_je_blank_userid.groupby(
        "entity_id", "journal_number"
    ).agg(
        count("*").alias("number_of_affected_lines"),
        sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
        sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
        sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
        sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
        sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
        sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_01d_warning_je_blank_userid_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_01d_warning_je_blank_userid_summary.groupby("entity_id").agg(
            count("*").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
            sum("Number_of_Affected_Lines").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_01d_warning_je_blank_userid_summary = data_integrity_check_01d_warning_je_blank_userid_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_01d_warning_je_blank_userid_summary["*"],
        lit("UserID_Entered").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit(
            "Management Override - JE Testing, Management Override - JE Analytics, Cortex JE Insights"
        ).alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Warning").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies blank values in the UserID_Entered field within the journal entry (JE) data. Consider investigating these journal entries and determining whether data provided is appropriate for use or needs to be evaluated separately."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_01d_Warning_JE_Blank_UserID").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )

    data_integrity_check_01d_warning_je_blank_userid_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_01d_warning_je_blank_userid_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (data_integrity_check_01d_warning_je_blank_userid, data_integrity_check_01d_warning_je_blank_userid_summary)

# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_01e_Warning_JE_Blank_TransactionType

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies blank values in the UserID_Entered field within the journal entry (JE) data'


def perform_dqc_journal_entry_blank_transactiontype(general_ledger_df):
    data_integrity_check_01e_warning_je_blank_transactiontype = general_ledger_df.filter(
        (col("transaction_type").isNull())|(col("transaction_type") == "")
    )

    data_integrity_check_01e_warning_je_blank_transactiontype_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_01e_warning_je_blank_transactiontype.groupby(
            "entity_id", "journal_number"
        ).agg(
            count("*").alias("number_of_affected_lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_01e_warning_je_blank_transactiontype_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_01e_warning_je_blank_transactiontype_summary.groupby("entity_id").agg(
            count("*").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
            sum("Number_of_Affected_Lines").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_01e_warning_je_blank_transactiontype_summary = data_integrity_check_01e_warning_je_blank_transactiontype_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_01e_warning_je_blank_transactiontype_summary["*"],
        lit("Transaction_Type").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit(
            "Management Override - JE Analytics, Transaction Analysis - JE Analytics"
        ).alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Warning").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies blank values in the transaction_type field within the journal entry (JE) data. Consider investigating these journal entries and determining whether data provided is appropriate for use or needs to be evaluated separately. Note: If performing Transaction Analysis - JE Analytics for cycle-based procedures, this data integrity check is critical and must be resolved prior to interpreting and documenting results of the analytic."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_01e_Warning_JE_Blank_Transaction_Type").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )

    data_integrity_check_01e_warning_je_blank_transactiontype_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_01e_warning_je_blank_transactiontype_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_01e_warning_je_blank_transactiontype,
        data_integrity_check_01e_warning_je_blank_transactiontype_summary,
    )

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_02a_Error_TB_Accounts_Not_In_COA

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies Account_Numbers found only in the trial balance (TB) and not in the chart of accounts (COA).
def perform_dqc_trial_balance_accounts_not_in_coa(tb_coa_joined):
    data_integrity_check_02a_error_tb_accounts_not_in_coa = tb_coa_joined.filter(
        (col("account_number_chart_of_accounts").isNull())
    ).select(
        col("account_number_trial_balance").alias("account_number"),
        col("entity_id_trial_balance").alias("entity_id"),
        "beginning_balance_gc",
        "beginning_balance_ec",
        "ending_balance_gc",
        "ending_balance_ec",
    )

    data_integrity_check_02a_error_tb_accounts_not_in_coa_Summary = (
        data_integrity_check_02a_error_tb_accounts_not_in_coa.groupby("entity_id")
        .agg(count('*').alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"))
    )
    data_integrity_check_02a_error_tb_accounts_not_in_coa_Summary = data_integrity_check_02a_error_tb_accounts_not_in_coa_Summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_02a_error_tb_accounts_not_in_coa_Summary["*"],
        lit("").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
        lit("Account_Number").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit("All").alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Error").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies Account_Numbers found only in the trial balance (TB) and not in the chart of accounts (COA). You will not be able to process the data in the platform if there are any accounts found in the trial balance but not in the chart of accounts."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_02a_Error_TB_Accounts_Not_In_COA").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )

    data_integrity_check_02a_error_tb_accounts_not_in_coa_Summary = (
        data_integrity_check_02a_error_tb_accounts_not_in_coa_Summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_02a_error_tb_accounts_not_in_coa,
        data_integrity_check_02a_error_tb_accounts_not_in_coa_Summary,
    )

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_02b_Error_JE_Accounts_Not_In_COA

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies Accounts found only in the journal entry data and not in the chart of accounts (COA)
def perform_dqc_je_accounts_not_in_coa(gl_tb_coa_joined):
    data_integrity_check_02b_error_je_accounts_not_in_coa = gl_tb_coa_joined.filter(
        (
            (col("account_number_chart_of_accounts").isNull())
            & (col("account_number").isNotNull())
        )
    ).select(
        "account_number",
        "entity_id",
        "entity_name",
        "journal_number",
        "transaction_type",
        "transaction_type_description",
        "date_posted",
        "date_effective",
        col("account_description_1").alias("account_description"),
        "net_amount_oc",
        "net_amount_ec",
        "net_amount_gc",
        "credit_amount_gc",
        "debit_amount_gc",
        "credit_amount_ec",
        "debit_amount_ec",
        "credit_amount_oc",
        "debit_amount_oc",
    )
    data_integrity_check_02b_error_je_accounts_not_in_coa_Summary = (
        data_integrity_check_02b_error_je_accounts_not_in_coa.groupby("entity_id", "journal_number").agg(
            count("*").alias("number_of_affected_lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_02b_error_je_accounts_not_in_coa_Summary = (
        data_integrity_check_02b_error_je_accounts_not_in_coa_Summary.groupby("entity_id").agg(
            count("*").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
            sum("number_of_affected_lines").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_02b_error_je_accounts_not_in_coa_Summary = data_integrity_check_02b_error_je_accounts_not_in_coa_Summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_02b_error_je_accounts_not_in_coa_Summary["*"],
        lit("Account_Number, Account_Description").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit("All").alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Error").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies Accounts found only in the journal entry data and not in the chart of accounts (COA). You will not be able to process the data in the platform if there are any accounts found in the journal entry data but not in the trial balance."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_02b_Error_JE_Accounts_Not_In_COA").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_02b_error_je_accounts_not_in_coa_Summary = (
        data_integrity_check_02b_error_je_accounts_not_in_coa_Summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_02b_error_je_accounts_not_in_coa,
        data_integrity_check_02b_error_je_accounts_not_in_coa_Summary,
    )

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_03a_Error_TB_Amount_Digits_TooLong

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies amount values within the trial balance exceeding 15 digits
def perform_dqc_tb_amount_digits_too_long(trial_balance_df):
    data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong = trial_balance_df.select(
        trial_balance_df["*"],
        when(
            length(abs(col("ending_balance_gc")).cast("Decimal(38,0)")) > 15,
            "Ending_Balance_GC Greater than 15 digits",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage1"),
        when(
            length(abs(col("ending_balance_ec")).cast("Decimal(38,0)")) > 15,
            "Ending_Balance_EC Greater than 15 digits",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage2"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong = (
        data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong.select(
            data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong["*"],
            concat_ws(
                ",",
                data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong["ErrorMessage1"],
                data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong["ErrorMessage2"],
            ).alias("error_detail"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong = (
        data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong.withColumn(
            "error_detail", regexp_replace(col("error_detail"), "(X_,)|(,X_)|(X_)", "")
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong = (
        data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong.filter((col("error_detail") != ""))
    )
    data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong = (
        data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong.withColumn(
            "error_detail", split(col("error_detail"), ",")
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong_1 = (
        data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong.select(
            "account_number",
            "entity_name",
            "entity_id",
            "account_description",
            "ending_balance_gc",
            "ending_balance_ec",
            "period_end_date",
            col("error_detail")
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong = (
        data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong_1.withColumn(
            "error_detail", explode(col("error_detail"))
         )
     )
    data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong_Summary = (
        data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong_1.groupby("entity_id")
        .agg(count('*').alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"))
    )
    data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong_Summary = data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong_Summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong_Summary["*"],
        lit("").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
        lit("Ending_balance_GC/EC").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit("All").alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Error").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies amount values within the trial balance exceeding 15 digits. You will not be able to process the data in the platform if there are any trial balance amount values longer than 15 digits."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_03a_Error_TB_Amount_Digits_TooLong").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong_Summary = (
        data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong_Summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong,
        data_integrity_check_03a_error_tb_amount_digits_toolong_Summary,
    )

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_03b_Error_JE_Amount_Digits_TooLong

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies amount values within the journal entry data exceeding 15 digits.
def perform_dqc_je_amount_digits_too_long(general_ledger_df):
    data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong = general_ledger_df.select(
        general_ledger_df["*"],
        when(
            length(abs(col("net_amount_gc")).cast("Decimal(38,0)")) > 15,
            "Net_Amount_GC Greater than 15 digit",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage1"),
        when(
            length(abs(col("net_amount_ec")).cast("Decimal(38,0)")) > 15,
            "Net_Amount_EC Greater than 15 digits",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage2"),
        when(
            length(abs(col("net_amount_oc")).cast("Decimal(38,0)")) > 15,
            "Net_Amount_OC Greater than 15 digits",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage3"),
        when(
            length(abs(col("debit_amount_oc")).cast("Decimal(38,0)")) > 15,
            "Debit_Amount_OC Greater than 15 digits",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage4"),
        when(
            length(abs(col("debit_amount_ec")).cast("Decimal(38,0)")) > 15,
            "Debit_Amount_EC Greater than 15 digits",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage5"),
        when(
            length(abs(col("debit_amount_gc")).cast("Decimal(38,0)")) > 15,
            "Debit_Amount_GC Greater than 15 digits",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage6"),
        when(
            length(abs(col("credit_amount_gc")).cast("Decimal(38,0)")) > 15,
            "Credit_Amount_GC Greater than 15 digits",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage7"),
        when(
            length(abs(col("credit_amount_oc")).cast("Decimal(38,0)")) > 15,
            "Credit_Amount_OC Greater than 15 digits",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage8"),
        when(
            length(abs(col("credit_amount_ec")).cast("Decimal(38,0)")) > 15,
            "Credit_Amount_EC Greater than 15 digits",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage9"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong = (
        data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong.select(
            data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong["*"],
            concat_ws(
                ",",
                col("ErrorMessage1"),
                col("ErrorMessage2"),
                col("ErrorMessage3"),
                col("ErrorMessage4"),
                col("ErrorMessage5"),
                col("ErrorMessage6"),
                col("ErrorMessage7"),
                col("ErrorMessage8"),
                col("ErrorMessage9"),
            ).alias("error_detail"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong = (
        data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong.withColumn(
            "error_detail", regexp_replace(col("error_detail"), "(X_,)|(,X_)|(X_)", "")
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong = (
        data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong.filter((col("error_detail") != ""))
    )
    
    data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong_1 = (
        data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong.select(col("chart_of_accounts"),
    col("entity_id"),
    col("entity_name"),
    col("journal_number"),
    col("journal_header_description"),
    col("dc_indicator"),
    col("journal_line_number"),
    col("journal_line_description"),
    col("fiscal_year"),
    col("fiscal_period"),
    col("transaction_type"),
    col("transaction_type_description"),
    col("entity_currency_ec"),
    col("date_posted"),
    col("date_effective"),
    col("userid_entered"),
    col("user_name_entered"),
    col("time_posted"),
    col("date_updated"),
    col("updated_by"),
    col("name_of_user_updated"),
    col("time_updated"),
    col("userid_approved"),
    col("user_name_approved"),
    col("date_approved"),
    col("time_approved"),
    col("is_standard"),
    col("is_manual"),
    col("source"),
    col("source_description"),
    col("account_number"),
    col("account_description"),
    col("coa_account_key"),
    col("exchange_rate"),
    col("foreign_exchange_date"),
    col("forex_conversion_method"),
    col("original_currency_oc"),
    col("group_currency_gc"),
    col("business_area"),
    col("controlling_area_for_cost_and_profit_center"),
    col("cost_center"),
    col("profit_center"),
    col("project"),
    col("location"),
    col("custom_exclusion"),
    col("extract_date"),
    col("import_date"),
    col("ledger_id"),
    col("ledger_group"),
    col("net_amount_oc"),
    col("net_amount_gc"),
    col("net_amount_ec"),
    col("debit_amount_oc"),
    col("debit_amount_gc"),
    col("debit_amount_ec"),
    col("credit_amount_oc"),
    col("credit_amount_gc"),
    col("credit_amount_ec"),
    split(col("error_detail"), ",").alias("error_detail")
    )
    )
    data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong = (
        data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong_1.withColumn(
            "error_detail", explode(col("error_detail"))
         )
     )
    data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong_Summary = (
        data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong_1.groupby(
            "entity_id", "journal_number"
        ).agg(
            count("*").alias("number_of_affected_lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong_Summary = (
        data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong_Summary.groupby("entity_id").agg(
            count("*").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
            sum("number_of_affected_lines").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong_Summary = data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong_Summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong_Summary["*"],
        lit("Net_Amount_EC/GC/OC, Debit_Amount_EC/GC/OC, Credit_Amount_EC/GC/OC").alias(
            "Related_Fields"
        ),
        lit("All").alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Error").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies amount values within the journal entry data exceeding 15 digits. You will not be able to process the data in the platform if there are any journal entry amount values longer than 15 digits."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_03b_Error_JE_Amount_Digits_TooLong").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong_Summary = (
        data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong_Summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong,
        data_integrity_check_03b_error_je_amount_digits_toolong_Summary,
    )

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_04a_Error_COA_Duplicate_Account_Values

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies duplicate account numbers (Account_Number) in the chart of accounts (COA)
def perform_dqc_coa_duplicate_account_values(chart_of_accounts_df):
    data_integrity_check_04a_error_coa_duplicate_account_values = (
        chart_of_accounts_df.groupby("account_number", "entity_id")
        .agg(count('*').alias("number_of_occurences"))
    )
    data_integrity_check_04a_error_coa_duplicate_account_values = (
        data_integrity_check_04a_error_coa_duplicate_account_values.filter(
            col("number_of_occurences") > 1
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_04a_error_coa_duplicate_account_values_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_04a_error_coa_duplicate_account_values.groupby("entity_id")
        .agg(sum('number_of_occurences').alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"))
    )

    data_integrity_check_04a_error_coa_duplicate_account_values_summary = data_integrity_check_04a_error_coa_duplicate_account_values_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_04a_error_coa_duplicate_account_values_summary["*"],
        lit("").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
        lit("Account_Number").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit("All").alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Error").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies duplicate account numbers (Account_Number) in the chart of accounts (COA). You will not be able to process the data in the platform if there are duplicate account numbers in the chart of accounts."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_04a_Error_COA_Duplicate_Account_Values").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_04a_error_coa_duplicate_account_values_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_04a_error_coa_duplicate_account_values_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_04a_error_coa_duplicate_account_values,
        data_integrity_check_04a_error_coa_duplicate_account_values_summary,
    )

## This function identifies duplicate account numbers (Account_Number) in the trial balance (TB).
def perform_dqc_tb_duplicate_account_values(trial_balance_df):

        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_04b_error_tb_duplicate_account_values_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_04b_error_tb_duplicate_account_values.groupby("entity_id")
        .agg(sum('number_of_occurences').alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"))
    )
    data_integrity_check_04b_error_tb_duplicate_account_values_summary = data_integrity_check_04b_error_tb_duplicate_account_values_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_04b_error_tb_duplicate_account_values_summary["*"],
        lit("").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
        lit("Account_Number, Period_End_Date").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit("All").alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Error").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies duplicate account numbers (Account_Number) in the trial balance (TB). You will not be able to process the data in the platform if there are duplicate account numbers in the chart of accounts."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_04b_Error_TB_Duplicate_Account_Values").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_04b_error_tb_duplicate_account_values_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_04b_error_tb_duplicate_account_values_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )
    return (
        data_integrity_check_04b_error_tb_duplicate_account_values,
        data_integrity_check_04b_error_tb_duplicate_account_values_summary,
    )

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_05_Error_JE_Unknown_Classification

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies values which are not classified as N (nonstandard) or S (standard) within the classification (Is_Standard) field.
def prepare_dqc_je_unknown_classification(general_ledger_df):
    data_integrity_check_05_error_je_unknown_classification = general_ledger_df.filter(
        (~col("is_standard").isin(["N", "S"]) | isnull(col("is_standard")))
    ).drop("copy_is_standard_2")
    data_integrity_check_05_error_je_unknown_classification_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_05_error_je_unknown_classification.groupby(
            "entity_id", "journal_number"
        ).agg(
            count("*").alias("number_of_affected_lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_05_error_je_unknown_classification_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_05_error_je_unknown_classification_summary.groupby("entity_id").agg(
            count("*").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
            sum("number_of_affected_lines").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_05_error_je_unknown_classification_summary = data_integrity_check_05_error_je_unknown_classification_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_05_error_je_unknown_classification_summary["*"],
        lit("Is_Standard, Journal_Number").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit(
            "Management Override - JE Testing, Management Override - JE Analytics, Cortex JE Insights"
        ).alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Error").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies values which are not classified as N (nonstandard) or S (standard) within the classification (Is_Standard) field. You will not be able to process the data in the platform if there is any text other than N or S within the classification (IsStandard) field."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_05_Error_JE_Unknown_Classification").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_05_error_je_unknown_classification_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_05_error_je_unknown_classification_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_05_error_je_unknown_classification,
        data_integrity_check_05_error_je_unknown_classification_summary,
    )

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_06_Error_JE_Multiple_Classification

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies journal entries (Journal_Number) with journal entry lines mapped to multiple classification (IsStandard) values (N and S)
def perform_dqc_je_multiple_classification(gl_null_to_blank):
    data_integrity_check_06_error_je_multiple_classification = (
        gl_null_to_blank.groupby(
            "journal_number", "entity_id", "copy_is_standard_2"
        ).agg(
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("sum_debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("sum_credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("sum_debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("sum_credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("sum_debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("sum_credit_amount_oc"),
            sum("net_amount_gc").alias("sum_net_amount_gc"),
            sum("net_amount_ec").alias("sum_net_amount_ec"),
            sum("net_amount_oc").alias("sum_net_amount_oc"),
            count("*").alias("row_count"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_06_error_je_multiple_classification = (
        data_integrity_check_06_error_je_multiple_classification.groupby(
            "journal_number", "entity_id"
        ).agg(count("*").alias("count_distinct_is_standard"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_gc").alias("sum_debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_gc").alias("sum_credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_ec").alias("sum_debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_ec").alias("sum_credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_oc").alias("sum_debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_oc").alias("sum_credit_amount_oc"),
            sum("sum_net_amount_gc").alias("sum_net_amount_gc"),
            sum("sum_net_amount_ec").alias("sum_net_amount_ec"),
            sum("sum_net_amount_oc").alias("sum_net_amount_oc"),
            sum("row_count").alias("number_of_journal_lines"))
    )
    data_integrity_check_06_error_je_multiple_classification = (
        data_integrity_check_06_error_je_multiple_classification.filter(
            col("count_distinct_is_standard") > 1
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_06_error_je_multiple_classification_1 = (
        data_integrity_check_06_error_je_multiple_classification.alias("a")
        .join(
            gl_null_to_blank.alias("b"),
            (col("a.journal_number") == col("b.journal_number")) & (col("a.entity_id") == col("b.entity_id")),
            "inner",
        )
        .select(
            col("b.account_number"),col("a.entity_id"),col("b.entity_name"),col("a.journal_number"),col("b.journal_header_description"),col("b.dc_indicator"),col("b.journal_line_number"),col("b.journal_line_description"),col("b.fiscal_year"),col("b.fiscal_period"),col("b.transaction_type"),col("b.transaction_type_description"),col("b.date_posted"),col("b.date_effective"),col("b.userid_entered"),col("b.user_name_entered"),col("b.userid_approved"),col("b.user_name_approved"),col("b.time_posted"),col("b.source"),col("b.copy_is_standard_2").alias("is_standard"),col("b.location"),col("b.net_amount_ec"),col("b.net_amount_gc"),col("b.net_amount_oc"),col("b.debit_amount_ec"),col("b.debit_amount_gc"),col("b.debit_amount_oc"),col("b.credit_amount_ec"),col("b.credit_amount_gc"),col("b.credit_amount_oc"),col("b.group_currency_gc"),col("b.entity_currency_ec"),col("b.original_currency_oc"),col("a.count_distinct_is_standard"),col("a.sum_credit_amount_gc"),col("a.sum_debit_amount_gc"),col("a.sum_credit_amount_ec"),col("a.sum_debit_amount_ec"),col("a.sum_credit_amount_oc"),col("a.sum_debit_amount_oc"),col("a.sum_net_amount_ec"),col("a.sum_net_amount_gc"),col("a.sum_net_amount_oc"),col("a.number_of_journal_lines")
        )
    )

    data_integrity_check_06_error_je_multiple_classification_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_06_error_je_multiple_classification.groupby("entity_id").agg(
            sum("number_of_journal_lines").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
            countDistinct("journal_number").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_gc").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_gc").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_ec").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_ec").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_oc").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_oc").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_06_error_je_multiple_classification_summary = data_integrity_check_06_error_je_multiple_classification_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_06_error_je_multiple_classification_summary["*"],
        lit("Is_Standard, Journal_Number").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit(
            "Management Override - JE Testing, Management Override - JE Analytics, Cortex JE Insights"
        ).alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Error").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies journal entries (Journal_Number) with journal entry lines mapped to multiple classification (IsStandard) values (N and S). You will not be able to process the data in the platform if there are inconsistent classifications of N (nonstandard) or S (standard) within the same journal entry."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_06_Error_JE_Multiple_Classification").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_06_error_je_multiple_classification_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_06_error_je_multiple_classification_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_06_error_je_multiple_classification_1,
        data_integrity_check_06_error_je_multiple_classification_summary,
    )

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_07_Error_COA_Unknown_Financial_Statement_Category

# COMMAND ----------

##This function identifies any Financial_Statement_Category that does not match Assets, Liabilities, Equity, Revenue, or Expenses.
def perform_dqc_coa_unknown_financial_statement_category(chart_of_accounts_df):
    data_integrity_check_07_error_coa_unknown_financial_statement_category = chart_of_accounts_df.withColumn("financial_statement_category",
    when(col("financial_statement_category").isNull(), "").otherwise(col("financial_statement_category")))
    data_integrity_check_07_error_coa_unknown_financial_statement_category = chart_of_accounts_df.filter(
        ~col("financial_statement_category").isin(
            ["Assets", "Expenses", "Liabilities", "Revenue", "Equity"]
        ) | isnull(col("financial_statement_category"))
    )
    data_integrity_check_07_error_coa_unknown_financial_statement_category_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_07_error_coa_unknown_financial_statement_category.groupby("entity_id")
        .agg(count('*').alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"))

    )
    data_integrity_check_07_error_coa_unknown_financial_statement_category_summary = data_integrity_check_07_error_coa_unknown_financial_statement_category_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_07_error_coa_unknown_financial_statement_category_summary["*"],
        lit("").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
        lit("").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        lit("").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
        lit("Financial_Statement_Category").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit(
            "Management Override - JE Testing, Management Override - JE Analytics, Transaction Analysis - JE Analytics, Cortex JE Insights"
        ).alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Error").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies any Financial_Statement_Category that does not match Assets, Liabilities, Equity, Revenue, or Expenses. You will not be able to process the data in the platform if the trial balance financial statement account subtypes have any value aside from Assets, Liabilities, Equity, Revenue, or Expenses."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_07_Error_COA_Unknown_Financial_Statement_Category").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_07_error_coa_unknown_financial_statement_category_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_07_error_coa_unknown_financial_statement_category_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_07_error_coa_unknown_financial_statement_category,
        data_integrity_check_07_error_coa_unknown_financial_statement_category_summary,
    )

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_08_Warning_JE_Sum_of_Amount_by_Entry_Not_Net_Zero

# COMMAND ----------

# DBTITLE 0,o
## This fuction identifies journal entries where the Net_Amount_EC, Net_Amount_GC or Net_Amount_OC does not net to zero
def perform_dqc_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero(general_ledger_df):
    summarized_journal_number = general_ledger_df.groupby(
        "journal_number", "entity_id"
    ).agg(
        sum("net_amount_gc").alias("sum_net_amount_gc"),
        sum("net_amount_oc").alias("sum_net_amount_oc"),
        sum("net_amount_ec").alias("sum_net_amount_ec"),
        sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("sum_debit_amount_gc"),
        sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("sum_credit_amount_gc"),
        sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("sum_debit_amount_ec"),
        sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("sum_credit_amount_ec"),
        sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("sum_debit_amount_oc"),
        sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("sum_credit_amount_oc"),
        count("*").alias("count_journal_number"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero = (
        summarized_journal_number.filter(col("count_journal_number") > 1)
    )
    data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero = (
        data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero.select(
            summarized_journal_number["*"],
            when(
                (round(col("sum_net_amount_ec"), 2) > 1.00)
                | (round(col("sum_net_amount_ec"), 2) < -1.00),
                "Net_Amount_EC does not net to 0 at journal entry level",
            )
            .otherwise("X_")
            .alias("ErrorMessage1"),
            when(
                (round(col("sum_net_amount_oc"), 2) > 1.00)
                | (round(col("sum_net_amount_oc"), 2) < -1.00),
                "Net_Amount_OC does not net to 0 at journal entry level",
            )
            .otherwise("X_")
            .alias("ErrorMessage2"),
            when(
                (round(col("sum_net_amount_gc"), 2) > 1.00)
                | (round(col("sum_net_amount_gc"), 2) < -1.00),
                "Net_Amount_GC does not net to 0 at journal entry level",
            )
            .otherwise("X_")
            .alias("ErrorMessage3"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero = (
        data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero.select(
            data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero["*"],
            concat_ws(
                ",", col("ErrorMessage1"), col("ErrorMessage2"), col("ErrorMessage3")
            ).alias("error_detail"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero = (
        data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero.withColumn(
            "error_detail", regexp_replace(col("error_detail"), "(X_,)|(,X_)|(X_)", "")
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero = (
        data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero.filter(
            (col("error_detail") != "")
        )
    )
    
    data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero = (
        data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero.select(col("count_journal_number").alias("row_count"),col("entity_id"),col("journal_number"),col("sum_debit_amount_gc").alias("Total_Debit_Amount_GC"),col("sum_debit_amount_ec").alias("Total_Debit_Amount_EC"),col("sum_debit_amount_oc").alias("Total_Debit_Amount_OC"),col("sum_credit_amount_gc").alias("Total_Credit_Amount_GC"),col("sum_credit_amount_ec").alias("Total_Credit_Amount_EC"),col("sum_credit_amount_oc").alias("Total_Credit_Amount_OC"),col("sum_net_amount_ec").alias("Total_Net_Amount_EC"),col("sum_net_amount_oc").alias("Total_Net_Amount_OC"),col("sum_net_amount_gc").alias("Total_Net_Amount_GC"),split(col("error_detail"), ",").alias("error_detail")
        )
    )
    
    data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero = data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero.alias("a").join(general_ledger_df.alias("b"),
    (col("a.journal_number") == col("b.journal_number")) & (col("a.entity_id") == col("b.entity_id")),
        "inner",
    ).select(col("b.account_number"),col("a.entity_id"),col("b.entity_name"),col("a.journal_number"),col("b.journal_header_description"),col("b.dc_indicator"),col("b.journal_line_number"),col("b.journal_line_description"),col("b.fiscal_year"),col("b.fiscal_period"),col("b.transaction_type"),col("b.transaction_type_description"),col("b.date_posted"),col("b.date_effective"),col("b.userid_entered"),col("b.user_name_entered"),col("b.userid_approved"),col("b.user_name_approved"),col("b.time_posted"),col("b.source"),col("b.is_standard"),col("b.location"),col("b.net_amount_ec"),col("b.net_amount_gc"),col("b.net_amount_oc"),col("b.debit_amount_ec"),col("b.debit_amount_gc"),col("b.debit_amount_oc"),col("b.credit_amount_ec"),col("b.credit_amount_gc"),col("b.credit_amount_oc"),col("b.group_currency_gc"),col("b.entity_currency_ec"),col("b.original_currency_oc"),col("a.Total_Debit_Amount_GC"),col("a.Total_Debit_Amount_EC"),col("a.Total_Debit_Amount_OC"),col("a.Total_Credit_Amount_GC"),col("a.Total_Credit_Amount_EC"),col("a.Total_Credit_Amount_OC"),col("a.Total_Net_Amount_EC"),col("a.Total_Net_Amount_OC"),col("a.Total_Net_Amount_GC"),col("a.row_count"),col("a.error_detail"))
    
    data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero = (
        data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero.withColumn(
            "error_detail", explode(col("error_detail"))
         )
     )

    data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero.select(
            "row_count",
            "journal_number",
            "entity_id",
            "Total_Debit_Amount_GC",
            "Total_Credit_Amount_GC",
            "Total_Debit_Amount_EC",
            "Total_Credit_Amount_EC",
            "Total_Debit_Amount_OC",
            "Total_Credit_Amount_OC",
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero_summary.distinct()
    )
    data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero_summary.groupby(
            "entity_id", "journal_number"
        ).agg(
            sum("row_count").alias("number_of_affected_lines"),
            sum("Total_Debit_Amount_GC").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("Total_Credit_Amount_GC").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("Total_Debit_Amount_EC").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("Total_Credit_Amount_EC").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("Total_Debit_Amount_OC").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("Total_Credit_Amount_OC").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero_summary.groupby(
            "entity_id"
        ).agg(
            count("*").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
            sum("number_of_affected_lines").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero_summary = data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero_summary["*"],
        lit("Net_Amount_EC/GC/OC, Journal_Number").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit("All").alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Warning").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies journal entries where the Net_Amount_EC, Net_Amount_GC or Net_Amount_OC does not net to zero. One line entries are not evaluated in this check. Refer to data_integrity_check_09 for entries with only one line. Consider investigating these journal entries and determining whether data provided is appropriate for use or needs to be evaluated separately. Note: If performing Transaction Analysis - JE Analytics for cycle-based procedures, this data integrity check is critical and must be resolved prior to interpreting and documenting results of the analytic."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_08_Warning_JE_Sum_of_Amount_by_Entry_Not_Net_Zero").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero,
        data_integrity_check_08_warning_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero_summary,
        summarized_journal_number,
    )
