# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_09_Warning_JE_One_Line_Entries

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies any journal entries (Journal_Number) with only a single record
def perform_dqc_je_one_line_entries(summarized_journal_number):
    data_integrity_check_09_warning_je_one_line_entries = summarized_journal_number.filter(
        col("count_journal_number") == 1
    )
    data_integrity_check_09_warning_je_one_line_entries_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_09_warning_je_one_line_entries.groupby("entity_id", "journal_number").agg(
            sum("count_journal_number").alias("number_of_affected_lines"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_gc").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_gc").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_ec").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_ec").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_oc").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_oc").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_09_warning_je_one_line_entries_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_09_warning_je_one_line_entries_summary.groupby("entity_id").agg(
            count("*").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
            sum("number_of_affected_lines").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_09_warning_je_one_line_entries_summary = data_integrity_check_09_warning_je_one_line_entries_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_09_warning_je_one_line_entries_summary["*"],
        lit("Journal_Number").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit(
            "Management Override - JE Testing, Management Override - JE Analytics, Cortex JE Insights"
        ).alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Warning").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies any journal entries (Journal_Number) with only a single record. Consider investigating these journal entries and determining whether data provided is appropriate for use or needs to be evaluated separately."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_09_Warning_JE_One_Line_Entries").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_09_warning_je_one_line_entries_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_09_warning_je_one_line_entries_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_09_warning_je_one_line_entries,
        data_integrity_check_09_warning_je_one_line_entries_summary,
    )

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_10_Warning_JE_Entry_Amount_Consistency

# COMMAND ----------

# This function identifies any journal entry lines (Journal_Line_Number) where the NetAmount for each entry equals the difference between the debit amount (Debit_Amount) and credit amount (Credit_Amount)
def perform_dqc_je_entry_amount_consistency(gl_null_to_blank):
    general_ledger_null_amount_to_0 = gl_null_to_blank.select(
        gl_null_to_blank["*"],
        when((col("net_amount_gc").isNull()), 0)
        .otherwise(col("net_amount_gc"))
        .alias("net_amount_gc_1"),
        when((col("net_amount_oc").isNull()), 0)
        .otherwise(col("net_amount_oc"))
        .alias("net_amount_oc_1"),
        when((col("net_amount_ec").isNull()), 0)
        .otherwise(col("net_amount_ec"))
        .alias("net_amount_ec_1"),
        when((col("debit_amount_ec").isNull()), 0)
        .otherwise(col("debit_amount_ec"))
        .alias("debit_amount_ec_1"),
        when((col("debit_amount_oc").isNull()), 0)
        .otherwise(col("debit_amount_oc"))
        .alias("debit_amount_oc_1"),
        when((col("debit_amount_gc").isNull()), 0)
        .otherwise(col("debit_amount_gc"))
        .alias("debit_amount_gc_1"),
        when((col("credit_amount_gc").isNull()), 0)
        .otherwise(col("credit_amount_gc"))
        .alias("credit_amount_gc_1"),
        when((col("credit_amount_ec").isNull()), 0)
        .otherwise(col("credit_amount_ec"))
        .alias("credit_amount_ec_1"),
        when((col("credit_amount_oc").isNull()), 0)
        .otherwise(col("credit_amount_oc"))
        .alias("credit_amount_oc_1"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency = general_ledger_null_amount_to_0.select(
        general_ledger_null_amount_to_0["*"],
        when(
            col("debit_amount_ec_1").cast("decimal(38,0)")
            - col("credit_amount_ec_1").cast("decimal(38,0)")
            != col("net_amount_ec_1").cast("decimal(38,0)"),
            "Debit_Amount_EC minus Credit_Amount_EC is not equal to Net_Amount_EC",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage1"),
        when(
            col("debit_amount_gc_1").cast("decimal(38,0)")
            - col("credit_amount_gc_1").cast("decimal(38,0)")
            != col("net_amount_gc_1").cast("decimal(38,0)"),
            "Debit_Amount_GC minus Credit_Amount_GC is not equal to Net_Amount_GC",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage2"),
        when(
            col("debit_amount_oc_1").cast("decimal(38,0)")
            - col("credit_amount_oc_1").cast("decimal(38,0)")
            != col("net_amount_oc_1").cast("decimal(38,0)"),
            "Debit_Amount_OC minus Credit_Amount_OC is not equal to Net_Amount_OC",
        )
        .otherwise("X_")
        .alias("ErrorMessage3"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency = (
        data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency.select(
            data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency["*"],
            concat_ws(",", col("ErrorMessage1"), col("ErrorMessage2"),col("ErrorMessage3")).alias(
                "error_detail"
            ),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency = (
        data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency.withColumn(
            "error_detail", regexp_replace(col("error_detail"), "(X_,)|(,X_)|(X_)", "")
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency = (
        data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency.filter((col("error_detail") != ""))
    )
    data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency_1 = (
        data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency.select(
            col("net_amount_gc_1").alias("net_amount_gc"),
            col("net_amount_oc_1").alias("net_amount_oc"),
            col("net_amount_ec_1").alias("net_amount_ec"),
            col("debit_amount_gc_1").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
            col("debit_amount_oc_1").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
            col("debit_amount_ec_1").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
            col("credit_amount_gc_1").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
            col("credit_amount_ec_1").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
            col("credit_amount_oc_1").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
            "entity_id",
            "entity_name",
            "account_number",
            "account_description",
            "fiscal_year",
            "journal_number",
            "journal_line_number",
            "transaction_type",
            "date_effective",
            "date_posted",
            "fiscal_period",
            split(col("error_detail"), ",").alias("error_detail")
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency = (
        data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency_1.withColumn(
            "error_detail", explode(col("error_detail"))
         )
    )
    data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency_1.groupby(
            "entity_id", "journal_number"
        ).agg(
            count("*").alias("number_of_affected_lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency_summary.groupby("entity_id").agg(
            count("*").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
            sum("number_of_affected_lines").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency_summary = data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency_summary["*"],
        lit("Debit_Amount_EC/GC/OC, Credit_Amount_EC/GC/OC, Net_Amount_EC/GC/OC").alias(
            "Related_Fields"
        ),
        lit(
            "Management Override - JE Testing, Management Override - JE Analytics, Cortex JE Insights"
        ).alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Warning").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies any journal entry lines (Journal_Line_Number) where the NetAmount for each entry equals the difference between the debit amount (Debit_Amount) and credit amount (Credit_Amount). Consider investigating these journal entries and inquiring with management regarding the inconsistencies within the journal entry data."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_10_Warning_JE_Entry_Amount_Consistency").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency,
        data_integrity_check_10_warning_je_entry_amount_consistency_summary,
        general_ledger_null_amount_to_0,
    )

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_11_Warning_JE_Debit_Credit_Same_Line

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies any records that have both a debit (Debit_Amount) and a credit (Credit_Amount) value in the same line
def perform_dqc_je_debit_credit_same_line(general_ledger_null_amount_to_0):
    data_integrity_check_11_warning_je_debit_credit_same_line = general_ledger_null_amount_to_0.select(
        general_ledger_null_amount_to_0["*"],
        when(
            ((col("debit_amount_ec_1") != 0) & (col("credit_amount_ec_1") != 0))
            | ((col("debit_amount_oc_1") != 0) & (col("credit_amount_oc_1") != 0))
            | ((col("debit_amount_gc_1") != 0) & (col("credit_amount_gc_1") != 0)),
            "Checks if a journal line has both a Debit Amount and a Credit Amount greater than zero (a line may only be Debit or Credit but not both)",
        )
        .otherwise(None)
        .alias("error_detail"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_11_warning_je_debit_credit_same_line = (
        data_integrity_check_11_warning_je_debit_credit_same_line.filter(
            col("error_detail") != ""
        ).select(
            "error_detail",
            col("debit_amount_oc_1").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
            col("debit_amount_ec_1").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
            col("debit_amount_gc_1").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
            col("credit_amount_oc_1").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
            col("credit_amount_gc_1").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
            col("credit_amount_ec_1").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
            "journal_number",
            "entity_id",
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_11_warning_je_debit_credit_same_line_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_11_warning_je_debit_credit_same_line.groupby(
            "entity_id", "journal_number"
        ).agg(
            count("*").alias("number_of_affected_lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_11_warning_je_debit_credit_same_line_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_11_warning_je_debit_credit_same_line_summary.groupby("entity_id").agg(
            count("*").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
            sum("number_of_affected_lines").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_11_warning_je_debit_credit_same_line_summary = data_integrity_check_11_warning_je_debit_credit_same_line_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_11_warning_je_debit_credit_same_line_summary["*"],
        lit("Debit_Amount_EC/GC/OC, Credit_Amount_EC/GC/OC").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit(
            "Management Override - JE Testing, Management Override - JE Analytics, Cortex JE Insights"
        ).alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Warning").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies any records that have both a debit (Debit_Amount) and a credit (Credit_Amount) value in the same line. Consider whether journal entry lines need to be split into separate records or netted to represent one amount per journal entry line. Remember to map to the appropriate debit (DebAmount), credit (CreAmount), or net (Net_Amount) amount columns."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_11_Warning_JE_Debit_Credit_Same_Line").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_11_warning_je_debit_credit_same_line_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_11_warning_je_debit_credit_same_line_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_11_warning_je_debit_credit_same_line,
        data_integrity_check_11_warning_je_debit_credit_same_line_summary,
    )

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_12_Warning_JE_Amount_Currency_Inconsistency

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies records where Net_Amount, in any currency (original, entity, group) equals 0 while any other currency value does not equal zero or vice versa.
def perform_dqc_je_amount_currency_inconsistency(general_ledger_null_amount_to_0):
    data_integrity_check_12_warning_je_amount_currency_inconsistency = (
        general_ledger_null_amount_to_0.filter(
            (
                (col("net_amount_ec_1") == 0)
                & (
                    (col("net_amount_gc_1") != 0)
                    | (col("net_amount_oc_1") != 0)
                )
            )
            | (
                (col("net_amount_gc_1") == 0)
                & (
                    (col("net_amount_ec_1") != 0)
                    | (col("net_amount_oc_1") != 0)
                )
            )
            | (
                (col("net_amount_oc_1") == 0)
                & (
                    (col("net_amount_gc_1") != 0)
                    | (col("net_amount_ec_1") != 0)
                )
            )
        ).select(
            col("net_amount_gc_1").alias("net_amount_gc"),
            col("net_amount_oc_1").alias("net_amount_oc"),
            col("net_amount_ec_1").alias("net_amount_ec"),
            col("debit_amount_gc_1").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
            col("debit_amount_oc_1").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
            col("debit_amount_ec_1").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
            col("credit_amount_gc_1").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
            col("credit_amount_ec_1").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
            col("credit_amount_oc_1").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
            "group_currency_gc",
            "entity_currency_ec",
            "original_currency_oc",
            "entity_id",
            "entity_name",
            "account_number",
            "account_description",
            "fiscal_year",
            "journal_number",
            "journal_line_number",
            "transaction_type",
            "date_effective",
            "date_posted",
            "fiscal_period",
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_12_warning_je_amount_currency_inconsistency_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_12_warning_je_amount_currency_inconsistency.groupby(
            "entity_id", "journal_number"
        ).agg(
            count("*").alias("number_of_affected_lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_12_warning_je_amount_currency_inconsistency_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_12_warning_je_amount_currency_inconsistency_summary.groupby(
            "entity_id"
        ).agg(
            count("*").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
            sum("number_of_affected_lines").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_12_warning_je_amount_currency_inconsistency_summary = data_integrity_check_12_warning_je_amount_currency_inconsistency_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_12_warning_je_amount_currency_inconsistency_summary["*"],
        lit("NetAmount EC/GC/OC").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit(
            "Management Override - JE Testing, Management Override - JE Analytics, Cortex JE Insights"
        ).alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Warning").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies records where Net_Amount, in any currency (original, entity, group) equals 0 while any other currency value does not equal zero or vice versa. Within Management Override - JE Testing, all tests use entity/group currency expect for Test 10a, 10b and 11 which use original currency. If you have a zero value in original currency while entity/group currency does not equal zero (or vice versa), you will not be able to perform certain tests on these records. Consider investigating these journal entries and inquiring with management regarding the inconsistencies within the journal entry data."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_12_Warning_JE_Amount_Currency_Inconsistency").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_12_warning_je_amount_currency_inconsistency_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_12_warning_je_amount_currency_inconsistency_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_12_warning_je_amount_currency_inconsistency,
        data_integrity_check_12_warning_je_amount_currency_inconsistency_summary,
    )

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_13a_Warning_JE_Entity_Multiple_Currency

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies Entity_IDs mapped to multiple entity currencies (EntityCurrency)
def perform_dqc_je_entity_multiple_currency(general_ledger_df):
    gl_null_to_blank = general_ledger_df.na.fill("", subset=["entity_currency_ec","journal_number","group_currency_gc","date_posted","date_effective","fiscal_period", "transaction_type","user_name_entered","userid_entered","journal_header_description"])

    data_integrity_check_13a_warning_je_entity_multiple_currency = (
        gl_null_to_blank.groupby(
            "entity_id", "journal_number", "entity_currency_ec"
        ).agg(
            count("*").alias("number_of_lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("sum_debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("sum_credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("sum_debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("sum_credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("sum_debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("sum_credit_amount_oc"),
        )
    )

    data_integrity_check_13a_warning_je_entity_multiple_currency = (
        data_integrity_check_13a_warning_je_entity_multiple_currency.groupby("entity_id").agg(
            countDistinct("journal_number").alias("count_distinct_journal_number"),
            sum("number_of_lines").alias("number_of_lines"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_gc").alias("sum_debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_gc").alias("sum_credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_ec").alias("sum_debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_ec").alias("sum_credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_oc").alias("sum_debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_oc").alias("sum_credit_amount_oc"),
            countDistinct("entity_currency_ec").alias("count_distinct_entitycurrency"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_13a_warning_je_entity_multiple_currency = (
        data_integrity_check_13a_warning_je_entity_multiple_currency.filter(
            col("count_distinct_entitycurrency") > 1
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_13a_warning_je_entity_multiple_currency_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_13a_warning_je_entity_multiple_currency.groupby("entity_id").agg(
            sum("number_of_lines").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
            sum("count_distinct_journal_number").alias(
                "Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"
            ),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_gc").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_gc").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_ec").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_ec").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_oc").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_oc").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        )
    )

    data_integrity_check_13a_warning_je_entity_multiple_currency_summary = data_integrity_check_13a_warning_je_entity_multiple_currency_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_13a_warning_je_entity_multiple_currency_summary["*"],
        lit("Entity_ID, Entity_Currency_EC").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit(
            "Management Override - JE Testing, Quantitative Risk Factors - JE Analytics, Cortex JE Insights"
        ).alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Warning").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies Entity_IDs mapped to multiple entity currencies (Entity_Currency). Each entity is expected to have only one EC. Consider investigating and inquiring with management on what the entity currency should be and why there are inconsistencies within the journal entry data. If an Entity_ID has more than one EC, all records for that Entity_ID will be identified as data integrity matters. For a summary report by currency, contact your Data Specialist to download the report included in the Data Table Outputs within the Specialist Tools workflow."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_13a_Warning_JE_Entity_Multiple_Currency").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_13a_warning_je_entity_multiple_currency_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_13a_warning_je_entity_multiple_currency_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_13a_warning_je_entity_multiple_currency,
        data_integrity_check_13a_warning_je_entity_multiple_currency_summary,
        gl_null_to_blank
    )

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_13b_Warning_JE_Group_Multiple_Currency

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies multiple currencies in the Group Currency field (Group_Currency_GC)
def perform_dqc_je_group_multiple_currency(gl_null_to_blank):
    data_integrity_check_13b_warning_je_group_multiple_currency = (
        gl_null_to_blank.groupby("group_currency_gc", "journal_number")
        .agg(
            count("*").alias("Number_of_Lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("sum_debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("sum_credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("sum_debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("sum_credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("sum_debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("sum_credit_amount_oc")
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_13b_warning_je_group_multiple_currency = (
        data_integrity_check_13b_warning_je_group_multiple_currency.groupby(lit("1"))
        .agg(
            sum("Number_of_Lines").alias("Number_of_Lines"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_gc").alias("sum_debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_gc").alias("sum_credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_ec").alias("sum_debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_ec").alias("sum_credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_oc").alias("sum_debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_oc").alias("sum_credit_amount_oc"),
            countDistinct("journal_number").alias("Count_Distinct_Journal_Number"),
            countDistinct("group_currency_gc").alias("Count_Distinct_GroupCurrency")
        )
    )
    
    data_integrity_check_13b_warning_je_group_multiple_currency = (
        data_integrity_check_13b_warning_je_group_multiple_currency.filter(
            col("Count_Distinct_GroupCurrency") > 1
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_13b_warning_je_group_multiple_currency = (
        data_integrity_check_13b_warning_je_group_multiple_currency.select(col("Count_Distinct_GroupCurrency"),col("Number_of_Lines"), col("Count_Distinct_Journal_Number"), col("sum_debit_amount_gc"),col("sum_debit_amount_oc"),col("sum_debit_amount_ec"),col("sum_credit_amount_gc"),col("sum_credit_amount_oc"),col("sum_credit_amount_ec"))
    )
    data_integrity_check_13b_warning_je_group_multiple_currency_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_13b_warning_je_group_multiple_currency.select(col("Number_of_Lines").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"), col("Count_Distinct_Journal_Number").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"), col("sum_debit_amount_gc").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),col("sum_debit_amount_oc").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),col("sum_debit_amount_ec").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),col("sum_credit_amount_gc").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),col("sum_credit_amount_oc").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),col("sum_credit_amount_ec").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"))
    )

    data_integrity_check_13b_warning_je_group_multiple_currency_summary = data_integrity_check_13b_warning_je_group_multiple_currency_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_13b_warning_je_group_multiple_currency_summary["*"],
        lit("Group_Currency_GC").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit(
            "Management Override - JE Testing, Quantitative Risk Factors - JE Analytics, Cortex JE Insights"
        ).alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Warning").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies multiple currencies in the Group Currency field (Group_Currency_GC). A dataset is expected to have only one GC. Consider investigating and inquiring with management on what the group currency should be and why there are inconsistencies within the journal entry data. If more than one GC is present, all records will be identified as data integrity matters. For a summary report by currency, contact your Data Specialist to download the report included in the Data Table Outputs within the Specialist Tools workflow."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_13b_Warning_JE_Group_Multiple_Currency").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
        lit("").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Toggled_Off")
    )
    data_integrity_check_13b_warning_je_group_multiple_currency_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_13b_warning_je_group_multiple_currency_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_13b_warning_je_group_multiple_currency,
        data_integrity_check_13b_warning_je_group_multiple_currency_summary,
    )

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_14_Warning_JE_Multiple_Date_Values

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies journal entries (Journal_Number) mapped to more than one value for Date_Effective, Date_Posted, and\/or financial period (Fiscal_Period)
def perform_dqc_je_multiple_date_values(gl_null_to_blank):
    data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values = (
        gl_null_to_blank.groupby(
            "journal_number",
            "entity_id",
            when(col("date_effective").isNull(),lit("")).otherwise(col("date_effective")).alias("date_effective"),
            when(col("date_posted").isNull(),lit("")).otherwise(col("date_posted")).alias("date_posted"),
            "fiscal_period",
        ).agg(
            count("*").alias("number_of_journal_lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
            sum("net_amount_gc").alias("net_amount_gc"),
            sum("net_amount_ec").alias("net_amount_ec"),
            sum("net_amount_oc").alias("net_amount_oc")
        )
    )

    data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values = (
        data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values.groupby(
            "journal_number", "entity_id"
        ).agg(
            countDistinct("date_effective").alias("countdistinct_date_effective"),
            countDistinct("date_posted").alias("countdistinct_date_posted"),
            countDistinct("fiscal_period").alias("countdistinct_fiscal_period"),
            sum("number_of_journal_lines").alias("number_of_journal_lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("sum_debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("sum_credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("sum_debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("sum_credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("sum_debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("sum_credit_amount_oc"),
            sum("net_amount_gc").alias("sum_net_amount_gc"),
            sum("net_amount_ec").alias("sum_net_amount_ec"),
            sum("net_amount_oc").alias("sum_net_amount_oc")
        )
    )

    data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values = (
        data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values.filter(
            (col("countdistinct_fiscal_period") > 1)
            | (col("countdistinct_date_posted") > 1)
            | (col("countdistinct_date_effective") > 1)
        )
    )

    data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values = (
        data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values.select(
            data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values["*"],
            when(
                col("countdistinct_date_effective") > 1,
                "Journal_Number mapped to more than one value for Date_Effective",
            )
            .otherwise("X_")
            .alias("Description1"),
            when(
                col("countdistinct_date_posted") > 1,
                "Journal_Number mapped to more than one value for Date_Posted",
            )
            .otherwise("X_")
            .alias("Description2"),
            when(
                col("countdistinct_fiscal_period") > 1,
                "Journal_Number mapped to more than one value for Fiscal_Period",
            )
            .otherwise("X_")
            .alias("Description3"),
        )
    )

    data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values = (
        data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values.select(
            data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values["*"],
            concat_ws(
                ",", col("Description1"), col("Description2"), col("Description3")
            ).alias("error_detail"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values = (
        data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values.withColumn(
            "error_detail", regexp_replace(col("error_detail"), "(X_,)|(,X_)|(X_)", "")
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values = (
        data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values.filter((col("error_detail") != ""))
    )


    
    data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values_1 = (
        data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values.select(col("journal_number"),
        col("entity_id"),
        col("countdistinct_date_effective"),
        col("countdistinct_date_posted"),
        col("countdistinct_fiscal_period"),
        col("number_of_journal_lines"),
        col("sum_debit_amount_gc"),
        col("sum_credit_amount_gc"),
        col("sum_debit_amount_ec"),
        col("sum_credit_amount_ec"),
        col("sum_debit_amount_oc"),
        col("sum_credit_amount_oc"),
        col("sum_net_amount_gc"),
        col("sum_net_amount_ec"),
        col("sum_net_amount_oc"),
        split(col("error_detail"), ",").alias("error_detail")
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values = data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values_1.alias("a").join(gl_null_to_blank.alias("b"),
    (col("a.journal_number") == col("b.journal_number")) & (col("a.entity_id") == col("b.entity_id")),
        "inner",
    ).select(col("b.account_number"),col("a.entity_id"),col("b.entity_name"),col("a.journal_number"),col("b.journal_header_description"),col("b.dc_indicator"),col("b.journal_line_number"),col("b.journal_line_description"),col("b.fiscal_year"),col("b.fiscal_period"),col("b.transaction_type"),col("b.transaction_type_description"),col("b.date_posted"),col("b.date_effective"),col("b.userid_entered"),col("b.user_name_entered"),col("b.userid_approved"),col("b.user_name_approved"),col("b.time_posted"),col("b.source"),col("b.is_standard"),col("b.location"),col("b.net_amount_ec"),col("b.net_amount_gc"),col("b.net_amount_oc"),col("b.debit_amount_ec"),col("b.debit_amount_gc"),col("b.debit_amount_oc"),col("b.credit_amount_ec"),col("b.credit_amount_gc"),col("b.credit_amount_oc"),col("b.group_currency_gc"),col("b.entity_currency_ec"),col("b.original_currency_oc"),col("a.countdistinct_date_effective"),col("a.countdistinct_date_posted"),col("a.countdistinct_fiscal_period"),col("a.number_of_journal_lines"),col("sum_net_amount_gc"),col("sum_net_amount_ec"),col("sum_net_amount_oc"),col("a.sum_debit_amount_gc"),col("a.sum_credit_amount_gc"),col("a.sum_debit_amount_ec"),col("a.sum_credit_amount_ec"),col("a.sum_debit_amount_oc"),col("a.sum_credit_amount_oc"),col("a.error_detail"))

    data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values = (
        data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values.withColumn(
            "error_detail", explode(col("error_detail"))
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values_1.groupby("entity_id").agg(
            sum("number_of_journal_lines").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
            count("*").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_gc").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_gc").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_ec").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_ec").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_oc").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_oc").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        )
    )

    data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values_summary = data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values_summary["*"],
        lit("Date_Effective, Date_Posted, Fiscal_Period, Journal_Number").alias(
            "Related_Fields"
        ),
        lit(
            "Management Override - JE Testing, Management Override - JE Analytics, Quantitative Risk Factors - JE Analytics, Cortex JE Insights"
        ).alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Warning").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies journal entries (Journal_Number) mapped to more than one value for Date_Effective, Date_Posted, and/or financial period (Fiscal_Period). Consider investigating and inquiring with management to understand why dates may vary within individual journal entries."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_14_Warning_JE_Multiple_Date_Values").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values,
        data_integrity_check_14_warning_je_multiple_date_values_summary,
    )

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_15_Warning_JE_Multiple_Transaction_Type

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies journal entries (Journal_Number) mapped to multiple transaction types (Transaction_Type)
def perform_dqc_je_multiple_transaction_type(gl_null_to_blank):
    data_integrity_check_15_warning_je_multiple_transaction_type = (
        gl_null_to_blank.groupby(
            "journal_number", "entity_id", "transaction_type"
        ).agg(
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
            sum("net_amount_gc").alias("net_amount_gc"),
            sum("net_amount_ec").alias("net_amount_ec"),
            sum("net_amount_oc").alias("net_amount_oc"),
            count("*").alias("row_count"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_15_warning_je_multiple_transaction_type = (
        data_integrity_check_15_warning_je_multiple_transaction_type.groupby(
            "journal_number", "entity_id"
        ).agg(
            count("transaction_type").alias("count_distinct_type"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("sum_debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("sum_credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("sum_debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("sum_credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("sum_debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("sum_credit_amount_oc"),
            sum("net_amount_gc").alias("sum_net_amount_gc"),
            sum("net_amount_ec").alias("sum_net_amount_ec"),
            sum("net_amount_oc").alias("sum_net_amount_oc"),
            sum("row_count").alias("number_of_journal_lines"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_15_warning_je_multiple_transaction_type = (
        data_integrity_check_15_warning_je_multiple_transaction_type.filter(
            col("count_distinct_type") > 1
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_15_warning_je_multiple_transaction_type_1 = (
        data_integrity_check_15_warning_je_multiple_transaction_type.alias("a")
        .join(
            gl_null_to_blank.alias("b"),
            (col("a.journal_number") == col("b.journal_number")) & (col("a.entity_id") == col("b.entity_id")),
            "inner",
        )
        .select(
            col("b.account_number"),col("a.entity_id"),col("b.entity_name"),col("a.journal_number"),col("b.journal_header_description"),col("b.dc_indicator"),col("b.journal_line_number"),col("b.journal_line_description"),col("b.fiscal_year"),col("b.fiscal_period"),col("b.transaction_type"),col("b.transaction_type_description"),col("b.date_posted"),col("b.date_effective"),col("b.userid_entered"),col("b.user_name_entered"),col("b.userid_approved"),col("b.user_name_approved"),col("b.time_posted"),col("b.source"),col("b.is_standard"),col("b.location"),col("b.net_amount_ec"),col("b.net_amount_gc"),col("b.net_amount_oc"),col("b.debit_amount_ec"),col("b.debit_amount_gc"),col("b.debit_amount_oc"),col("b.credit_amount_ec"),col("b.credit_amount_gc"),col("b.credit_amount_oc"),col("b.group_currency_gc"),col("b.entity_currency_ec"),col("b.original_currency_oc"),col("a.count_distinct_type").alias("count_distinct_transaction_type"),col("a.sum_debit_amount_gc"),col("a.sum_credit_amount_gc"),col("a.sum_debit_amount_ec"),col("a.sum_credit_amount_ec"),col("a.sum_debit_amount_oc"),
            col("a.sum_credit_amount_oc"),col("a.sum_net_amount_ec"),col("a.sum_net_amount_gc"),col("a.sum_net_amount_oc"),col("a.number_of_journal_lines")
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_15_warning_je_multiple_transaction_type_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_15_warning_je_multiple_transaction_type.groupby("entity_id").agg(
            sum("number_of_journal_lines").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
            countDistinct("journal_number").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_gc").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_gc").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_ec").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_ec").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_oc").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_oc").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_15_warning_je_multiple_transaction_type_summary = data_integrity_check_15_warning_je_multiple_transaction_type_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_15_warning_je_multiple_transaction_type_summary["*"],
        lit("Transaction_Type, Journal_Number").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit(
            "Management Override - JE Analytics, Transaction Analysis - JE Analytics"
        ).alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Warning").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies journal entries (Journal_Number) mapped to multiple transaction types (Transaction_Type). Consider investigating these journal entries to understand why journal entries may have multiple transaction types and whether those journal entries are unique. Consider inquiring with management to understand why journal entries are mapped to multiple transaction types. Note: If performing Transaction Analysis - JE Analytics for cycle-based procedures, this data integrity check is critical and must be resolved prior to interpreting and documenting results of the analytic."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_15_Warning_JE_Multiple_Transaction_Type").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )

    data_integrity_check_15_warning_je_multiple_transaction_type_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_15_warning_je_multiple_transaction_type_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )
    return (
        data_integrity_check_15_warning_je_multiple_transaction_type_1,
        data_integrity_check_15_warning_je_multiple_transaction_type_summary,
    )

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_16_Warning_JE_Out_Of_Range_Effective_Date

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies Date_Effective values that are before and after the testing period
def perform_dqc_je_out_of_range_effective_date(
    general_ledger_df, first_day_testing_period, last_day_testing_period
):
    data_integrity_check_16_warning_je_prior_post_effective_date = general_ledger_df.filter(
        (col("date_effective") < to_date(lit(first_day_testing_period), "MM/dd/yyyy"))
        | ((col("date_effective").isNull()) | (col("date_effective") == ""))
        | (col("date_effective") > to_date(lit(last_day_testing_period), "MM/dd/yyyy"))
    )
    data_integrity_check_16_warning_je_prior_post_effective_date_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_16_warning_je_prior_post_effective_date.groupby(
            "entity_id", "journal_number"
        ).agg(
            count("*").alias("number_of_affected_lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
        )
    )

    data_integrity_check_16_warning_je_prior_post_effective_date_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_16_warning_je_prior_post_effective_date_summary.groupby("entity_id").agg(
            count("*").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
            sum("number_of_affected_lines").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_16_warning_je_prior_post_effective_date_summary = data_integrity_check_16_warning_je_prior_post_effective_date_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_16_warning_je_prior_post_effective_date_summary["*"],
        lit("Date_Effective").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit("All").alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Warning").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies Date_Effective values that are prior or after the testing period. Consider investigating these journal entries and determining whether data provided is appropriate for use or needs to be evaluated separately. Cortex Capabilities will include journal entry data based on the testing period parameter. Any entries with effective dates before or after the testing period will be excluded from the analysis, but likely included in the data set reconciliation. Additionally, the excluded journal entry data will be included in prior or post testing period analyses. For example, entries with effective dates after the testing period, will be included in the subsequent testing period analysis."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_16_Warning_JE_Prior_Post_Effective_Date").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_16_warning_je_prior_post_effective_date_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_16_warning_je_prior_post_effective_date_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_16_warning_je_prior_post_effective_date,
        data_integrity_check_16_warning_je_prior_post_effective_date_summary,
    )

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_17_Observation_JE_Multiple_User_ID

# COMMAND ----------

# This function identifies journal entries (Journal_Number) mapped to more than one UserID_Entered value
def perform_dqc_je_multiple_user_id(gl_null_to_blank):
    data_integrity_check_17_observation_je_multiple_user_id = (
        gl_null_to_blank.groupby(
            "journal_number", "entity_id", "userid_entered"
        ).agg(
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
            count("*").alias("row_count"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_17_observation_je_multiple_user_id = (
        data_integrity_check_17_observation_je_multiple_user_id.groupby(
            "journal_number", "entity_id"
        ).agg(
            count("userid_entered").alias("Count_Distinct_User"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("Total_Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("Total_Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("Total_Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("Total_Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("Total_Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("Total_Credit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("row_count").alias("Number_of_Journal_Lines"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_17_observation_je_multiple_user_id = (
        data_integrity_check_17_observation_je_multiple_user_id.filter(
            col("Count_Distinct_User") > 1
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_17_observation_je_multiple_user_id_1 = data_integrity_check_17_observation_je_multiple_user_id.alias("a").join(gl_null_to_blank.alias("b"),
    (col("a.journal_number") == col("b.journal_number")) & (col("a.entity_id") == col("b.entity_id")),
        "inner",
    ).select(col("b.account_number"),col("a.entity_id"),col("b.entity_name"),col("a.journal_number"),col("b.journal_header_description"),col("b.dc_indicator"),col("b.journal_line_number"),col("b.journal_line_description"),col("b.fiscal_year"),col("b.fiscal_period"),col("b.transaction_type"),col("b.transaction_type_description"),col("b.date_posted"),col("b.date_effective"),col("b.userid_entered"),col("b.user_name_entered"),col("b.userid_approved"),col("b.user_name_approved"),col("b.time_posted"),col("b.source"),col("b.is_standard"),col("b.location"),col("b.net_amount_ec"),col("b.net_amount_gc"),col("b.net_amount_oc"),col("b.debit_amount_ec"),col("b.debit_amount_gc"),col("b.debit_amount_oc"),col("b.credit_amount_ec"),col("b.credit_amount_gc"),col("b.credit_amount_oc"),col("b.group_currency_gc"),col("b.entity_currency_ec"),col("b.original_currency_oc"),col("a.Count_Distinct_User"),col("a.Total_Debit_Amount_GC"),col("a.Total_Credit_Amount_GC"),col("a.Total_Debit_Amount_EC"),col("a.Total_Credit_Amount_EC"),col("a.Total_Debit_Amount_OC"),col("a.Total_Credit_Amount_OC"),col("a.Number_of_Journal_Lines"))

    data_integrity_check_17_observation_je_multiple_user_id_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_17_observation_je_multiple_user_id.groupby("entity_id").agg(
            sum("Number_of_Journal_Lines").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
            count("*").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
            sum("Total_Debit_Amount_GC").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("Total_Credit_Amount_GC").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("Total_Debit_Amount_EC").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("Total_Credit_Amount_EC").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("Total_Debit_Amount_OC").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("Total_Credit_Amount_OC").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_17_observation_je_multiple_user_id_summary = data_integrity_check_17_observation_je_multiple_user_id_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_17_observation_je_multiple_user_id_summary["*"],
        lit("UserID_Entered, Journal_Number").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit(
            "Management Override - JE Testing, Management Override - JE Analytics, Cortex JE Insights"
        ).alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Observation").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies journal entries (Journal_Number) mapped to more than one UserID_Entered value. Consider investigating these journal entries and determining whether data provided is appropriate for use and inquiring with management on why multiple User IDs were included within individual journal entries."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_17_Observation_JE_Multiple_User_ID").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_17_observation_je_multiple_user_id_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_17_observation_je_multiple_user_id_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_17_observation_je_multiple_user_id_1,
        data_integrity_check_17_observation_je_multiple_user_id_summary,
    )

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_18_Observation_JE_Multiple_Entry_Description

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies Journal_Number that are identical to the row level Journal_Header_Description values
def perform_dqc_je_multiple_entry_description(gl_null_to_blank):
    data_integrity_check_18_observation_je_multiple_entry_description = (
        gl_null_to_blank.groupby(
            "journal_number", "entity_id", "journal_header_description"
        ).agg(
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
            count("*").alias("row_count")
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_18_observation_je_multiple_entry_description = (
        data_integrity_check_18_observation_je_multiple_entry_description.groupby(
            "journal_number", "entity_id"
        ).agg(
            count("*").alias("distinct_header_description"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
            sum("row_count").alias("row_count"),
        )
    )

    data_integrity_check_18_observation_je_multiple_entry_description = (
        data_integrity_check_18_observation_je_multiple_entry_description.filter(
            col("distinct_header_description") > 1
        )
    )

    data_integrity_check_18_observation_je_multiple_entry_description_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_18_observation_je_multiple_entry_description.groupby("entity_id").agg(
            count("*").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
            sum("row_count").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_18_observation_je_multiple_entry_description_summary = data_integrity_check_18_observation_je_multiple_entry_description_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_18_observation_je_multiple_entry_description_summary["*"],
        lit(
            "Journal_Number, Journal_Header_Description, Journal_Line_Description"
        ).alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit(
            "Management Override - JE Testing, Management Override - JE Analytics, Cortex JE Insights"
        ).alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Observation").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies Journal_Number that contains multiple Journal_Header_Description values. Each journal entry number is expected to have one journal entry description. Consider investigating these journal entries and determining whether the lines represent multiple journal entries. While Test 09 in Management Override - JE Testing performs analysis on all Journal_Header_Description values, the JE Testing deliverable output will only include one Journal_Header_Description value."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_18_Observation_JE_Multiple_Entry_Description").alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_18_observation_je_multiple_entry_description_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_18_observation_je_multiple_entry_description_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_18_observation_je_multiple_entry_description,
        data_integrity_check_18_observation_je_multiple_entry_description_summary,
    )

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_19_Observation_JE_Sum_of_Amount_by_Transaction_Type_Not_Net_Zero

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies transaction types (Transaction_Type) where NetAmount does not net to zero
def perform_dqc_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero(gl_null_to_blank):
    data_integrity_check_19_je_type_list = gl_null_to_blank.groupby(
        "transaction_type", "journal_number" , "entity_id"
    ).agg(count("*"))
    data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero = (
        gl_null_to_blank.groupby("transaction_type","entity_id").agg(
            sum("net_amount_gc").alias("sum_net_amount_gc"),
            sum("net_amount_oc").alias("sum_net_amount_oc"),
            sum("net_amount_ec").alias("sum_net_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("sum_debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("sum_debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("sum_debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("sum_credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("sum_credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("sum_credit_amount_oc"),
            count("*").alias("count_journal_number"),
            count_distinct("journal_number").alias("count_distinct_journal_number"),
        )
    )

    data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero = (
        data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero.select(
            data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero["*"],
            when(
                (round(col("sum_net_amount_ec"),2) > 1.00) | (round(col("sum_net_amount_ec"),2) < -1.00),
                "Net_Amount_EC does not net to 0 at transaction type level",
            )
            .otherwise("X_")
            .alias("ErrorMessage1"),
            when(
                (round(col("sum_net_amount_oc"),2) > 1.00) | (round(col("sum_net_amount_oc"),2) < -1.00),
                "Net_Amount_OC does not net to 0 at transaction type level",
            )
            .otherwise("X_")
            .alias("ErrorMessage2"),
            when(
                (round(col("sum_net_amount_gc"),2) > 1.00) | (round(col("sum_net_amount_gc"),2) < -1.00),
                "Net_Amount_GC does not net to 0 at transaction type level",
            )
            .otherwise("X_")
            .alias("ErrorMessage3"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero = (
        data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero.select(
            data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero["*"],
            concat_ws(
                ",", col("ErrorMessage1"), col("ErrorMessage2"), col("ErrorMessage3")
            ).alias("error_detail"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero = (
        data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero.withColumn(
            "error_detail", regexp_replace(col("error_detail"), "(X_,)|(,X_)|(X_)", "")
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero = (
        data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero.filter(
            (col("error_detail") != "")
        )
    )

    data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero = (
        data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero.select(col("entity_id"),col("sum_debit_amount_gc").alias("Total_Debit_Amount_GC"),col("sum_debit_amount_ec").alias("Total_Debit_Amount_EC"),col("sum_debit_amount_oc").alias("Total_Debit_Amount_OC"),col("sum_credit_amount_gc").alias("Total_Credit_Amount_GC"),col("sum_credit_amount_ec").alias("Total_Credit_Amount_EC"),col("sum_credit_amount_oc").alias("Total_Credit_Amount_OC"),col("sum_net_amount_ec").alias("Total_Net_Amount_EC"),col("sum_net_amount_oc").alias("Total_Net_Amount_OC"),col("sum_net_amount_gc").alias("Total_Net_Amount_GC"),col("count_journal_number").alias("row_count"),col("count_distinct_journal_number"),col("transaction_type"),split(col("error_detail"), ",").alias("error_detail")
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero = (
        data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero.withColumn(
            "error_detail", explode(col("error_detail"))
         )
     )

    data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero.select(col("entity_id"),col("Total_Debit_Amount_GC"),col("Total_Debit_Amount_EC"),col("Total_Debit_Amount_OC"),col("Total_Credit_Amount_GC"),col("Total_Credit_Amount_EC"),col("Total_Credit_Amount_OC"),col("Total_Net_Amount_EC"),col("Total_Net_Amount_OC"),col("Total_Net_Amount_GC"),col("row_count"),col("transaction_type"))
    )
    data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary.distinct()
     )

    data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary = data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary.alias("a").join(
        data_integrity_check_19_je_type_list.alias("b"),
        (
        (col("a.transaction_type") == col("b.transaction_type")) & (col("a.entity_id") == col("b.entity_id"))
        ),
        "inner",
    ).select(
        col("a.transaction_type") , col("a.entity_id") , col("a.Total_Debit_Amount_GC") , col("a.Total_Credit_Amount_GC") , col("a.Total_Credit_Amount_EC"), col("a.Total_Credit_Amount_OC"), col("a.Total_Debit_Amount_EC"), col("a.Total_Debit_Amount_OC"),col("b.journal_number"), col("a.row_count")
    )
    data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary_temp = data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary.groupBy("entity_id").agg(countDistinct("journal_number").alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"))

    data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary = data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary.alias("c").join(
        data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary_temp.alias("d"),
        (
        (col("c.entity_id") == col("d.entity_id"))
       ),
        "inner",
    ).select(
        col("c.transaction_type") , col("c.entity_id") , col("c.Total_Debit_Amount_GC") , col("c.Total_Credit_Amount_GC") , col("c.Total_Credit_Amount_EC"), col("c.Total_Credit_Amount_OC"), col("c.Total_Debit_Amount_EC"), col("c.Total_Debit_Amount_OC"), col("c.row_count"),col("d.Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries")
    )

    data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary.distinct()
    )

    data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary = data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary.groupby("entity_id").agg(
        sum("row_count").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
        (sum("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries")/count("*")).alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
        sum("Total_Credit_Amount_GC").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
        sum("Total_Debit_Amount_GC").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
        sum("Total_Credit_Amount_EC").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
        sum("Total_Credit_Amount_OC").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        sum("Total_Debit_Amount_EC").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
        sum("Total_Debit_Amount_OC").alias("Debit_Amount_OC")
    )

    data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary = data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary[
            "*"
        ],
        lit("Net_Amount_EC/GC/OC, Transaction_Type").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit(
            "Management Override - JE Analytics, Transaction Analysis - JE Analytics"
        ).alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Observation").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies transaction types (Transaction_Type) where NetAmount does not net to zero. This may also help resolve data_integrity_check_15_Warning_JE_Multiple_Transaction_Type. Consider investigating these journal entries to determine whether information provided is appropriate for use and inquiring with management on why transaction types do not net to zero. For a summary report by transaction type, contact your Data Specialist to download the report included in the Data Table Outputs within the Specialist Tools workflow."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit(
            "Data_Integrity_Check_19_Observation_JE_Sum_of_Amount_by_Transaction_Type_Not_Net_Zero"
        ).alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),
    )
    data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary = data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary.filter(
        col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero,
        data_integrity_check_19_observation_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero_summary,
    )

# COMMAND ----------

# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ## Data_Integrity_Check_20_Observation_UserID_Entered_Multiple_User_Name_Entered

# COMMAND ----------

## This function identifies UserID_Entered mapped to more than one User_Name_Entered value
def perform_dqc_user_id_entered_multiple_user_name_entered(gl_null_to_blank):
    data_integrity_check_20_observation_userid_entered_multiple_user_name_entered = (
        gl_null_to_blank.groupby(
            "userid_entered", "entity_id", "journal_number", "user_name_entered"
        ).agg(
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("credit_amount_oc"),
            count("*").alias("row_count"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_20_observation_userid_entered_multiple_user_name_entered = (
        data_integrity_check_20_observation_userid_entered_multiple_user_name_entered.groupby(
            "userid_entered", "entity_id"
        ).agg(
            countDistinct("user_name_entered").alias("count_distinct_user_name"),
            countDistinct("journal_number").alias("number_of_journal_entries"),
            sum("debit_amount_gc").alias("sum_debit_amount_gc"),
            sum("credit_amount_gc").alias("sum_credit_amount_gc"),
            sum("debit_amount_ec").alias("sum_debit_amount_ec"),
            sum("credit_amount_ec").alias("sum_credit_amount_ec"),
            sum("debit_amount_oc").alias("sum_debit_amount_oc"),
            sum("credit_amount_oc").alias("sum_credit_amount_oc"),
            sum("row_count").alias("row_count"),
        )
    )

    data_integrity_check_20_observation_userid_entered_multiple_user_name_entered = (
        data_integrity_check_20_observation_userid_entered_multiple_user_name_entered.filter(
            col("count_distinct_user_name") > 1
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_20_observation_userid_entered_multiple_user_name_entered_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_20_observation_userid_entered_multiple_user_name_entered.groupby(
            "entity_id"
        ).agg(
            sum("row_count").alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines"),
            sum("number_of_journal_entries").alias(
                "Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"
            ),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_gc").alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_gc").alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_ec").alias("Debit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_ec").alias("Credit_Amount_EC"),
            sum("sum_debit_amount_oc").alias("Debit_Amount_OC"),
            sum("sum_credit_amount_oc").alias("Credit_Amount_OC"),
        )
    )
    data_integrity_check_20_observation_userid_entered_multiple_user_name_entered_summary = data_integrity_check_20_observation_userid_entered_multiple_user_name_entered_summary.select(
        data_integrity_check_20_observation_userid_entered_multiple_user_name_entered_summary["*"],
        lit("UserID_Entered, User_Name_Entered").alias("Related_Fields"),
        lit(
            "Management Override - JE Testing, Management Override - JE Analytics, Cortex JE Insights"
        ).alias("Capability_Impacted"),
        lit("Observation").alias("Error_Warning"),
        lit(
            "Identifies UserID_Entered mapped to more than one User_Name_Entered value. Consider investigating these user IDs and determining whether data provided is appropriate for use and inquiring with management on why multiple user names were assigned to individual user IDs."
        ).alias("Description"),
        lit("Data_Integrity_Check_20_Observation_UserID_Entered_Multiple_User_Name_Entered").alias(
            "Data_Integrity_Check_Name"
        ),
    )
    data_integrity_check_20_observation_userid_entered_multiple_user_name_entered_summary = (
        data_integrity_check_20_observation_userid_entered_multiple_user_name_entered_summary.filter(
            col("Number_of_Affected_Lines") > 0
        )
    )

    return (
        data_integrity_check_20_observation_userid_entered_multiple_user_name_entered,
        data_integrity_check_20_observation_userid_entered_multiple_user_name_entered_summary,
    )

## Storing the data quality checks output and its individual summary returned by the functions
cp.logging.set_command('cmd 101: Storing Data_Integrity_Check_ Outputs')
try:
    logger.info('Populating Data_Integrity_Check_ Outputs')
    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_13a_Warning_JE_Entity_Multiple_Currency,
        Data_Integrity_Check_13a_Warning_JE_Entity_Multiple_Currency_Summary,
        gl_null_to_blank
    ) = perform_dqc_je_entity_multiple_currency(general_ledger_df)
    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_01a_Error_COA_Blank_Values,
        Data_Integrity_Check_01a_Error_COA_Blank_Values_Summary,
    ) = perform_dqc_coa_blank_values(chart_of_accounts_df)
    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_01b_Error_TB_Blank_Values,
        Data_Integrity_Check_01b_Error_TB_Blank_Values_Summary,
    ) = perform_dqc_trial_balance_blank_values(trial_balance_df)
    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_01c_Error_JE_Blank_Values,
        Data_Integrity_Check_01c_Error_JE_Blank_Values_Summary,
    ) = perform_dqc_journal_entry_blank_values(general_ledger_df)
    if obj_gcw_details.exclude_data_integrity_check_related_to_userid == "No":
        (
            Data_Integrity_Check_01d_Warning_JE_Blank_UserID,
            Data_Integrity_Check_01d_Warning_JE_Blank_UserID_Summary,
        ) = perform_dqc_journal_entry_blank_userid(general_ledger_df)
    else:
        Data_Integrity_Check_01d_Warning_JE_Blank_UserID_Summary = spark.createDataFrame(
            [], schema=obj_gcw_details.data_integrity_check_summary_columns
        )
    if obj_gcw_details.exclude_data_integrity_check_related_to_transactiontype == "No":
        (
            Data_Integrity_Check_01e_Warning_JE_Blank_TransactionType,
