    else:
        Data_Integrity_Check_01e_Warning_JE_Blank_TransactionType_Summary = spark.createDataFrame(
            [], schema=obj_gcw_details.data_integrity_check_summary_columns
        )
        Data_Integrity_Check_15_Warning_JE_Multiple_Transaction_Type_Summary = spark.createDataFrame(
            [], schema=obj_gcw_details.data_integrity_check_summary_columns
        )
    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_02a_Error_TB_Accounts_Not_In_COA,
        Data_Integrity_Check_02a_Error_TB_Accounts_Not_In_COA_Summary,
    ) = perform_dqc_trial_balance_accounts_not_in_coa(tb_coa_joined)
    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_02b_Error_JE_Accounts_Not_In_COA,
        Data_Integrity_Check_02b_Error_JE_Accounts_Not_In_COA_Summary,
    ) = perform_dqc_je_accounts_not_in_coa(gl_tb_coa_joined)
    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_03a_Error_TB_Amount_Digits_TooLong,
        Data_Integrity_Check_03a_Error_TB_Amount_Digits_TooLong_Summary,
    ) = perform_dqc_tb_amount_digits_too_long(trial_balance_df)
    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_03b_Error_JE_Amount_Digits_TooLong,
        Data_Integrity_Check_03b_Error_JE_Amount_Digits_TooLong_Summary,
    ) = perform_dqc_je_amount_digits_too_long(general_ledger_df)
    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_04a_Error_COA_Duplicate_Account_Values,
        Data_Integrity_Check_04a_Error_COA_Duplicate_Account_Values_Summary,
    ) = perform_dqc_coa_duplicate_account_values(chart_of_accounts_df)
    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_04b_Error_TB_Duplicate_Account_Values,
        Data_Integrity_Check_04b_Error_TB_Duplicate_Account_Values_Summary,
    ) = perform_dqc_tb_duplicate_account_values(trial_balance_df)

    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_05_Error_JE_Unknown_Classification,
        Data_Integrity_Check_05_Error_JE_Unknown_Classification_Summary,
    ) = prepare_dqc_je_unknown_classification(general_ledger_df)
    if obj_gcw_details.autocorrect_multiple_is_standard == "No":
        (
            Data_Integrity_Check_06_Error_JE_Multiple_Classification,
            Data_Integrity_Check_06_Error_JE_Multiple_Classification_Summary,
        ) = perform_dqc_je_multiple_classification(general_ledger_df)
    else:
        Data_Integrity_Check_06_Error_JE_Multiple_Classification_Summary = spark.createDataFrame(
            [], schema=obj_gcw_details.data_integrity_check_summary_columns
        )
    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_07_Error_COA_Unknown_Financial_Statement_Category,
        Data_Integrity_Check_07_Error_COA_Unknown_Financial_Statement_Category_Summary,
    ) = perform_dqc_coa_unknown_financial_statement_category(chart_of_accounts_df)
    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_08_Warning_JE_Sum_of_Amount_by_Entry_Not_Net_Zero,
        Data_Integrity_Check_08_Warning_JE_Sum_of_Amount_by_Entry_Not_Net_Zero_Summary,
        summarized_journal_number,
    ) = perform_dqc_je_sum_of_amount_by_entry_not_net_zero(general_ledger_df)
    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_09_Warning_JE_One_Line_Entries,
        Data_Integrity_Check_09_Warning_JE_One_Line_Entries_Summary,
    ) = perform_dqc_je_one_line_entries(summarized_journal_number)
    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_10_Warning_JE_Entry_Amount_Consistency,
        Data_Integrity_Check_10_Warning_JE_Entry_Amount_Consistency_Summary,
        general_ledger_null_amount_to_0,
    ) = perform_dqc_je_entry_amount_consistency(gl_null_to_blank)
    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_11_Warning_JE_Debit_Credit_Same_Line,
        Data_Integrity_Check_11_Warning_JE_Debit_Credit_Same_Line_Summary,
    ) = perform_dqc_je_debit_credit_same_line(general_ledger_null_amount_to_0)
    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_12_Warning_JE_Amount_Currency_Inconsistency,
        Data_Integrity_Check_12_Warning_JE_Amount_Currency_Inconsistency_Summary,
    ) = perform_dqc_je_amount_currency_inconsistency(general_ledger_null_amount_to_0)

    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_13b_Warning_JE_Group_Multiple_Currency,
        Data_Integrity_Check_13b_Warning_JE_Group_Multiple_Currency_Summary,
    ) = perform_dqc_je_group_multiple_currency(gl_null_to_blank)
    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_14_Warning_JE_Multiple_Date_Values,
        Data_Integrity_Check_14_Warning_JE_Multiple_Date_Values_Summary,
    ) = perform_dqc_je_multiple_date_values(gl_null_to_blank)
    (
        Data_Integrity_Check_16_Warning_JE_Prior_Post_Effective_Date,
        Data_Integrity_Check_16_Warning_JE_Prior_Post_Effective_Date_Summary,
    ) = perform_dqc_je_out_of_range_effective_date(
        general_ledger_df,
        obj_gcw_details.first_day_testing_period,
        obj_gcw_details.last_day_testing_period,
    )
    if (obj_gcw_details.exclude_data_integrity_check_related_to_userid == "No") & (
        obj_gcw_details.exclude_data_integrity_check_for_observation == "No"
    ):
        (
            Data_Integrity_Check_17_Observation_JE_Multiple_User_ID,
            Data_Integrity_Check_17_Observation_JE_Multiple_User_ID_Summary,
        ) = perform_dqc_je_multiple_user_id(gl_null_to_blank)
        (
            Data_Integrity_Check_20_Observation_UserID_Entered_Multiple_User_Name_Entered,
            Data_Integrity_Check_20_Observation_UserID_Entered_Multiple_User_Name_Entered_Summary,
        ) = perform_dqc_user_id_entered_multiple_user_name_entered(gl_null_to_blank)
    else:
        Data_Integrity_Check_17_Observation_JE_Multiple_User_ID_Summary = spark.createDataFrame(
            [], schema=obj_gcw_details.data_integrity_check_summary_columns
        )
        Data_Integrity_Check_20_Observation_UserID_Entered_Multiple_User_Name_Entered_Summary = (
            spark.createDataFrame([], schema=obj_gcw_details.data_integrity_check_summary_columns)
        )
    if (obj_gcw_details.exclude_data_integrity_check_related_to_transactiontype == "No") & (
        obj_gcw_details.exclude_data_integrity_check_for_observation == "No"
    ):
        (
            Data_Integrity_Check_19_Observation_JE_Sum_of_Amount_by_Transaction_Type_Not_Net_Zero,
            Data_Integrity_Check_19_Observation_JE_Sum_of_Amount_by_Transaction_Type_Not_Net_Zero_Summary,
        ) = perform_dqc_je_sum_of_amount_by_transaction_type_not_net_zero(gl_null_to_blank)
    else:
        Data_Integrity_Check_19_Observation_JE_Sum_of_Amount_by_Transaction_Type_Not_Net_Zero_Summary = spark.createDataFrame(
            [], schema=obj_gcw_details.data_integrity_check_summary_columns
        )
    if obj_gcw_details.exclude_data_integrity_check_for_observation == "No":
        (
            Data_Integrity_Check_18_Observation_JE_Multiple_Entry_Description,
            Data_Integrity_Check_18_Observation_JE_Multiple_Entry_Description_Summary,
        ) = perform_dqc_je_multiple_entry_description(gl_null_to_blank)
    else:
        Data_Integrity_Check_18_Observation_JE_Multiple_Entry_Description_Summary = spark.createDataFrame(
            [], schema=obj_gcw_details.data_integrity_check_summary_columns
        )
except Exception as e:
    logger.exception(e)
    # re-raise the exception
    raise Exception from e

    Data_Integrity_Check_00_Summary_Entity_ID = Data_Integrity_Check_00_Summary_Entity_ID.unionByName(Data_Integrity_Checks_toggle_parameter)
    window_spec_summary = Window.partitionBy("Data_Integrity_Check_Name").orderBy(desc("Data_Integrity_Check_Name"),desc("Description"))
    Data_Integrity_Check_00_Summary_Entity_ID = Data_Integrity_Check_00_Summary_Entity_ID.select(Data_Integrity_Check_00_Summary_Entity_ID["*"], (row_number().over(window_spec_summary)).alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Name_1"))

    ## The below step is used to add an extra column to Data_Integrity_Check_Toggled_Off that shows whether the Data_Integrity_Checks on which the toggled parameter is applied are On or Off.
    Data_Integrity_Check_00_Summary = Data_Integrity_Check_00_Summary_Entity_ID.select(col('Data_Integrity_Check_Name'), col('Description'),col('Error_Warning'),col('Capability_Impacted'),col('Related_Fields'),col('Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries'),col('Number_of_Affected_Lines'),col('Credit_Amount_EC'),col('Credit_Amount_GC'),col('Credit_Amount_OC'),col('Debit_Amount_EC'),col('Debit_Amount_GC'),col('Debit_Amount_OC'), col('Data_Integrity_Check_Toggled_Off'))

    Data_Integrity_Check_00_Summary = Data_Integrity_Check_00_Summary.unionByName(Data_Integrity_Check_13b_Warning_JE_Group_Multiple_Currency_Summary)

    ## The below step is used to check whether the Data_Integrity_Check's for which toggled parameter is applicable is present in the Data_Integrity_Check_Summary or not                                                            
    Data_Integrity_Check_00_Summary = Data_Integrity_Check_00_Summary.groupby('Data_Integrity_Check_Name', 'Data_Integrity_Check_Toggled_Off', 'Description', 'Error_Warning','Capability_Impacted','Related_Fields').agg(sum(col('Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries')).alias('Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries'),sum(col('Number_of_Affected_Lines')).alias('Number_of_Affected_Lines'),sum(col('Credit_Amount_EC')).alias('Credit_Amount_EC'), sum(col('Credit_Amount_OC')).alias('Credit_Amount_OC') , sum(col('Credit_Amount_GC')).alias('Credit_Amount_GC') , sum(col('Debit_Amount_GC')).alias('Debit_Amount_GC') , sum(col('Debit_Amount_OC')).alias('Debit_Amount_OC'), sum(col('Debit_Amount_EC')).alias('Debit_Amount_EC'))
except Exception as e:
    logger.exception(e)
    # re-raise the exception
    raise Exception from e

import openpyxl

from copy import copy

import requests
import pandas as pd

# COMMAND ----------

import json
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass
class JeReconDicSummary(object):
    unqiue_id:str

# COMMAND ----------

def get_input_details() -> JeReconDicSummary:
        
  return JeReconDicSummary(
    unqiue_id                   = str(uuid.uuid4())
  )

# COMMAND ----------

def create_GlobalTempViews(df_dict, unqiue_id):
    tables = []
    for name,df in df_dict.items():
        tempview_name = f'`{unqiue_id}_tempView_{name}`'
        df.createOrReplaceTempView(tempview_name)
        tables.append(tempview_name) 
    return tables

def populate_dww_template(je_recon_dic_summary_details: JeReconDicSummary, mapping_info: List[dict], df_reconciliation: DataFrame, df_reconciliation_currency: DataFrame, df_dic_summary: DataFrame ,output_path: str,input_template_path: str) -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
    unqiue_id = je_recon_dic_summary_details.unqiue_id
    output_excel_path = f'{output_path}/JE-Recon-and-DIC-Template.xlsx'
    input_template_path = "CONTENT_LIBRARY/FILES/dww-templates/JE-Recon-and-DIC-Template.xlsx"
    template_data = [{"tables":create_GlobalTempViews({"df_reconciliation":df_reconciliation,"df_reconciliation_currency":df_reconciliation_currency, "df_dic_summary":df_dic_summary},unqiue_id)}]
    template_data = json.dumps(template_data)
    template_id =dww_template(input_template_path,output_excel_path,str(json.dumps(mapping_info)),str(template_data),unqiue_id)
    return template_id

# COMMAND ----------

def create_reconciliation(input_reconciliation) -> Tuple[DataFrame, DataFrame]:
    df_reconciliation_ordered = input_reconciliation.select('entity_id','account_number','account_description','financial_statement_line','financial_statement_category','number_of_entries','number_of_lines','debit_amount','credit_amount','je_activity','beginning_balance','ending_balance','trial_activity','variance','reconciliation')
    df_reconciliation_ordered=df_reconciliation_ordered.withColumn("tickmarks",lit(None))
    df_reconciliation_currency = input_reconciliation.select("reconciliation_currency").distinct()


    return df_reconciliation_ordered, df_reconciliation_currency

# COMMAND ----------

mapping_info = [{"sheetname": "1. Reconciliation","field_type": "table","field":"df_reconciliation_currency","cell": "L3"},
{"sheetname": "1. Reconciliation","field_type": "table","field":"df_reconciliation","cell": "C13"},
{"sheetname": "2. Data Check Summary","field_type": "table","field":"df_dic_summary","insert_rows":"False","cell": "I25"}
]

# COMMAND ----------

def create_dic_summary(input_dic):
    Data_Integrity_Check_names = [["Data_Integrity_Check_01a_Error_COA_Blank_Values"],["Data_Integrity_Check_01b_Error_TB_Blank_Values"],["Data_Integrity_Check_01c_Error_JE_Blank_Values"],["Data_Integrity_Check_01d_Warning_JE_Blank_UserID"],["Data_Integrity_Check_01e_Warning_JE_Blank_Transaction_Type"],["Data_Integrity_Check_02a_Error_TB_Accounts_Not_In_COA"],["Data_Integrity_Check_02b_Error_JE_Accounts_Not_In_COA"],["Data_Integrity_Check_03a_Error_TB_Amount_Digits_TooLong"],["Data_Integrity_Check_03b_Error_JE_Amount_Digits_TooLong"],["Data_Integrity_Check_04a_Error_COA_Duplicate_Account_Values"],["Data_Integrity_Check_04b_Error_TB_Duplicate_Account_Values"],["Data_Integrity_Check_05_Error_JE_Unknown_Classification"],["Data_Integrity_Check_06_Error_JE_Multiple_Classification"],["Data_Integrity_Check_07_Error_COA_Unknown_Financial_Statement_Category"],["Data_Integrity_Check_08_Warning_JE_Sum_of_Amount_by_Entry_Not_Net_Zero"],["Data_Integrity_Check_09_Warning_JE_One_Line_Entries"],["Data_Integrity_Check_10_Warning_JE_Entry_Amount_Consistency"],["Data_Integrity_Check_11_Warning_JE_Debit_Credit_Same_Line"],["Data_Integrity_Check_12_Warning_JE_Amount_Currency_Inconsistency"],["Data_Integrity_Check_13a_Warning_JE_Entity_Multiple_Currency"],["Data_Integrity_Check_13b_Warning_JE_Group_Multiple_Currency"],["Data_Integrity_Check_14_Warning_JE_Multiple_Date_Values"],["Data_Integrity_Check_15_Warning_JE_Multiple_Transaction_Type"],["Data_Integrity_Check_16_Warning_JE_Prior_Post_Effective_Date"],["Data_Integrity_Check_17_Observation_JE_Multiple_User_ID"],["Data_Integrity_Check_18_Observation_JE_Multiple_Entry_Description"],["Data_Integrity_Check_19_Observation_JE_Sum_of_Amount_by_Transaction_Type_Not_Net_Zero"],["Data_Integrity_Check_20_Observation_UserID_Entered_Multiple_User_Name_Entered"]]
    df_data_integrity_check = spark.createDataFrame(Data_Integrity_Check_names, ['Data_Integrity_Check_Name'])
    df_data_integrity_check = df_data_integrity_check.select(df_data_integrity_check["*"],lit(None).alias("Credit_Amount_GC"),
                           lit(None).alias("Debit_Amount_GC"),
                           lit(None).alias("Data_Integrity_Check_Toggled_Off"),
                           lit(None).alias("Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries"),
                           lit(None).alias("Number_of_Affected_Lines")
    )
    df_dic_summary_joined = input_dic.join(df_data_integrity_check, (input_dic.Data_Integrity_Check_Name == df_data_integrity_check.Data_Integrity_Check_Name), how = "outer") \
                        .orderBy(df_data_integrity_check.Data_Integrity_Check_Name) \
                        .select(input_dic.Data_Integrity_Check_Toggled_Off ,input_dic.Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries, \
                            input_dic.Number_of_Affected_Lines, input_dic.Debit_Amount_GC, \
                            input_dic.Credit_Amount_GC,input_dic.Debit_Amount_EC,input_dic.Credit_Amount_EC, \
                            input_dic.Debit_Amount_OC,input_dic.Credit_Amount_OC)
    df_dic_summary_joined = df_dic_summary_joined.withColumn("data_integrity_matters_present",when(col("Number_of_Affected_Lines")!=0, "Yes").otherwise("No"))

    df_dic_ordered = df_dic_summary_joined.select('Data_Integrity_Check_Toggled_Off','data_integrity_matters_present','Number_of_Affected_Journal_Entries','Number_of_Affected_Lines','Debit_Amount_GC','Credit_Amount_GC')

    return df_dic_ordered

# COMMAND ----------

def dww_template(input_template_path,output_template_path,str_mapping_info,str_template_data,id):
    mapping_info = json.loads(str_mapping_info)
    template_data = json.loads(str_template_data)

    def fill_template(template):
        start_index = { sheet : 0 for sheet in template.sheetnames }
        for data in template_data:
            for field in mapping_info:
                if field['field_type']=="table":
                    if 'insert_rows' in field:
                        if field['insert_rows'] == 'False': 
                            template = fill_table(template,field,start_index)
                    else:    
                        template = add_table(template,field,start_index)
            for table in data['tables'] : 
                spark.catalog.dropTempView(table)
        return template 

    def shift_merge_cells(sheet,num_rows_insert,row_index,field):
        for crange in sheet.merged_cells.ranges:
            clo,rlo,chi,rhi = crange.bounds
            if rlo>row_index:
                row_dim_merged_cell = sheet.row_dimensions[rlo]
                crange.shift(0,num_rows_insert)
                sheet.row_dimensions[rlo+num_rows_insert]=row_dim_merged_cell
                sheet.row_dimensions[rlo] = sheet.row_dimensions[rlo-1]            
        return

    def fill_and_copy_cell_style(rows,row_index,col_index,sheet,first_cell):
        for r_idx, row in enumerate(rows, row_index):
            for c_idx, value in enumerate(row, col_index):
                new_cell = sheet.cell(row=r_idx, column=c_idx, value=value)
                if first_cell.has_style:
                    new_cell.font = copy(first_cell.font)
                    new_cell.border = copy(first_cell.border)
                    new_cell.fill = copy(first_cell.fill)
                    new_cell.number_format = copy(first_cell.number_format)
                    new_cell.protection = copy(first_cell.protection)
                    new_cell.alignment = copy(first_cell.alignment)
        return

    def add_table(template,field,start_index):
        table_name = f'`{id}_tempView_{field["field"]}`'
        df = spark.read.table(table_name)
        if df.count()==0 and 'header' not in field:
            return template
        df = df.toPandas()
        sheet = template[field['sheetname']]
        rows = dataframe_to_rows(df,index=False,header= True if 'header' in field else False)
        xy = coordinate_from_string(field['cell'])
        col_index = column_index_from_string(xy[0])
        row_index = xy[1]+start_index[field['sheetname']]
        first_cell = sheet.cell(row= row_index , column = col_index)
        #since one row will always be added for a table in template so no need to insert row if table has only one row.
        num_rows_insert = df.shape[0] if 'header' in field else df.shape[0]-1
        if num_rows_insert != 0:
            sheet.insert_rows(row_index, num_rows_insert)
            shift_merge_cells(sheet,num_rows_insert, row_index,field)        
            start_index[field['sheetname']]+=num_rows_insert
        fill_and_copy_cell_style(rows,row_index,col_index,sheet,first_cell)                 
        return template

    def fill_table(template,field,start_index):
        table_name = f'`{id}_tempView_{field["field"]}`'
        df = spark.read.table(table_name)
        if df.count()==0 and 'header' not in field:
            return template
        df = df.toPandas()
        sheet = template[field['sheetname']]
        rows = dataframe_to_rows(df,index=False,header= True if 'header' in field else False)
        xy = coordinate_from_string(field['cell'])
        col_index = column_index_from_string(xy[0])
        row_index = xy[1]+start_index[field['sheetname']]
        for r_idx, row in enumerate(rows, row_index):
            for c_idx, value in enumerate(row, col_index):
                new_cell = sheet.cell(row=r_idx, column=c_idx, value=value)                
        return template


    def fill_dww_template():
        # Read
        template = cp.openpyxl.read(input_template_path, workspace_file=True)

  
        
        # Populate
        template=fill_template(template)

 
        # Save
        template_id = cp.openpyxl.write(template, output_template_path,register=True)

        return template_id
    return fill_dww_template()


# COMMAND ----------

# DBTITLE 1,Populating JE Reconciliation Excel Template
## Calling the functions to populate the recon template 
cp.logging.set_command('cmd 114: Populating JE Reconciliation Excel Template')
try:
    logger.info('Populating JE Reconciliation Excel Template')
    if(obj_gcw_details.je_recon_dic_template== 'Yes'):
            je_recon_dic_summary_details = get_input_details()
            cp.write(Reconciliation,f'{obj_gcw_details.output_path}/Parquet_Reconciliation.parquet',
            "overwrite",register=True)
            cp.write(Data_Integrity_Check_00_Summary,f'{obj_gcw_details.output_path}/Parquet_Data_Integrity_Check_00_Summary.parquet',
            "overwrite",register=True)

            Data_Integrity_Check_00_Summary=cp.read(f'{obj_gcw_details.output_path}/Parquet_Data_Integrity_Check_00_Summary.parquet')
            Reconciliation=cp.read(f'{obj_gcw_details.output_path}/Parquet_Reconciliation.parquet')

            df_dic_summary = create_dic_summary(Data_Integrity_Check_00_Summary)
            df_reconciliation, df_reconciliation_currency = create_reconciliation(Reconciliation)

            template_id = populate_dww_template(je_recon_dic_summary_details, mapping_info, df_reconciliation, df_reconciliation_currency, df_dic_summary,obj_gcw_details.output_path,obj_gcw_details.input_template_path)
            
            result = {
            "unqiue_id": je_recon_dic_summary_details.unqiue_id,
            "templateId": template_id
            }
except Exception as e:
    logger.exception(e)
    # re-raise the exception
    raise Exception from e
cp.logging.log_cmd()
